FILE: basic.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import sys
import time
import logging
import random
import threading
import cv2
import numpy as np
from functools import wraps
from datetime import datetime

# Airtest 核心类
from airtest.core.api import Template, wait, sleep
from airtest.core.android.android import Android
from airtest.core.error import TargetNotFoundError
from airtest.core.helper import G
# 👇 【新增】引入这个常量，为了拿我们在 KSAS.py 里设置好的路径
from airtest.core.android import constant
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class TaskInterruptedError(Exception):
    """任务被用户强制中断"""
    pass

# ==========================================
# 🛠️ 实例操作器
# ==========================================
class DeviceOperator:
    def __init__(self, serial, instance_id, data_dir_root="data", interrupt_callback=None):
        self.serial = serial
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{instance_id}.Device")
        
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_dir_root, instance_id)
        self.snapshot_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        os.makedirs(self.snapshot_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 🔒 实例级锁
        self.lock = threading.RLock()
        
        # ⏹️ 中断检查回调 (如果返回 True，说明需要立即停止)
        self.interrupt_callback = interrupt_callback

        self.adb = None
        self._is_connected = False

        # 👇 【新增 2】 加载核心安全资源 (替换 "123456" 为你在 KeyAuth 后台的真实 File ID)
        # 这张图就是 home_check.png
        self.IMG_HOME_CHECK = ResourceLoader().get_template("268132", threshold=0.9)

        # 如果下载失败，给个警告（或者直接抛异常）
        if self.IMG_HOME_CHECK is None:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_core_resource_fail"))

    # ==========================================
    # 🛡️ 装饰器
    # ==========================================
    def _safe_wrap(func):
        @wraps(func)
        def wrapper(self, *args, **kwargs):
            action_name = func.__name__
            with self.lock:
                # 1. 动作前检查中断
                self.check_interrupt()

                if not self._is_connected or not self.adb:
                    self.connect()

                try:
                    result = func(self, *args, **kwargs)
                    return True if result is None else result
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    # 中断信号直接往上抛，给 Scheduler 捕获
                    raise
                except TargetNotFoundError:
                    self.logger.warning(get_text("log_action_target_not_found", action_name))
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_404")
                    return False
                except Exception as e:
                    self.logger.error(get_text("log_action_exception", action_name, e))
                    self._save_snapshot(f"{action_name}_error")
                    return False
        return wrapper

    # ==========================================
    # 🔌 系统连接
    # ==========================================
    def check_interrupt(self):
        """检查是否应该立即停止"""
        if self.interrupt_callback and self.interrupt_callback():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_interrupt_signal"))
            raise TaskInterruptedError("Task interrupted by user")

    def connect(self):
        with self.lock:
            try:
                # 👇 【核心修改】这里不再只传 serial，而是把 adb_path 塞进去！
                # 这样 Airtest 就绝对不敢用别人的 ADB 了
                self.adb = Android(self.serial, adb_path=constant.ADB_PATH)
                
                self._is_connected = True
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_device_mounted", self.serial))
                return True
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_device_mount_failed", e))
                self._is_connected = False
                return False

    def _save_snapshot(self, tag):
        try:
            if not self.adb: return None
            timestamp = datetime.now().strftime("%H%M%S")
            filename = f"{timestamp}_{tag}.png"
            filepath = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, filename)
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=filepath)
            return filepath
        except:
            return None

    def snapshot(self):
        """
        兼容性方法：返回截图路径
        """
        return self._save_snapshot("temp")

    # ==========================================
    # 🔍 内部辅助：图片匹配
    # ==========================================
    def _find_template_pos(self, target):
        """
        手动实现图片匹配，兼容多种返回格式
        """
        try:
            # 1. 截取当前屏幕
            temp_path = os.path.join(self.snapshot_dir, "tmp_match.png")
            self.adb.snapshot(filename=temp_path)
            
            # 2. 读取图片
            screen = cv2.imread(temp_path)
            if screen is None: return None

            # 3. 匹配
            res = target.match_in(screen)
            
            # 4. 【核心修复】兼容性处理
            if res:
                # 情况 A: 如果是字典 (Airtest 标准返回)，取 result 字段
                if isinstance(res, dict):
                    return res.get('result')
                # 情况 B: 如果直接是元组/列表 (坐标)，直接返回
                elif isinstance(res, (tuple, list)):
                    return res
                # 情况 C: 其他奇怪的对象，尝试直接用
                else:
                    return res
            
            return None
        except Exception as e:
            # 这里打印详细一点，方便调试
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_image_match_error", e, type(res) if 'res' in locals() else 'Unknown'))
            return None

    # ==========================================
    # 👆 动作执行
    # ==========================================
    @_safe_wrap
    def drag_and_hold(self, start, end, duration=1.8, hold=1.0, steps=20):
        """
        精准拖拽并保持。
        
        策略 A (Minitouch): 按下 -> 插值移动 -> 悬停不动 -> 松开 (完美防抖)
        策略 B (Maxtouch): 合并时间执行极慢速滑动 (依靠低速消除惯性)
        """
        x1, y1 = start
        x2, y2 = end
        
        try:
            # 1. 尝试获取底层代理
            proxy = self.adb.touch_proxy
            
            # [新增] 预先检查是否支持 touch_down，不支持直接抛出异常跳到 except
            if not hasattr(proxy, "touch_down"):
                raise NotImplementedError("touch_down not supported")
            
            # 2. 尝试执行精准操作
            proxy.touch_down((x1, y1))
            
            step_sleep = duration / steps
            for i in range(steps):
                progress = (i + 1) / steps
                mx = x1 + (x2 - x1) * progress
                my = y1 + (y2 - y1) * progress
                proxy.touch_move((mx, my))
                time.sleep(step_sleep)
            
            if hold > 0:
                time.sleep(hold)
            
            proxy.touch_up((x2, y2))
            time.sleep(0.5)
            
        except Exception:
            # 3. [降级方案] 适用于 MAXTOUCH / Android 10+
            # 报错说明设备不支持原子操作。
            # 解决方案：将 "移动时间" 和 "悬停时间" 相加，执行一次【极慢速】滑动。
            # 原理：滑动时间越长，末端速度越慢，惯性回弹越小。
            total_duration = duration + hold
            # 记录一下如果是调试模式
            # self.logger.debug(f"Device fallback: performing slow swipe for {total_duration}s")
            
            self.adb.swipe(start, end, duration=total_duration)
            time.sleep(0.5)


    @_safe_wrap
    def click(self, target, times=1, offset=(0, 0)):
        pos = target
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            found = False
            for _ in range(3):
                self.check_interrupt() # 每次重试前都检查
                pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
                if pos:
                    found = True
                    break
                time.sleep(0.5)
            
            if not found:
                raise TargetNotFoundError("Template not found")
        
        for _ in range(times):
            self.check_interrupt() # 连点之间检查
            if pos:
                self.adb.touch(pos)
            if times > 1:
                time.sleep(random.uniform(0.2, 0.5))

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe(self, start, end, duration=0.5):
        """
        基础滑动方法 (兼容 Airtest swipe)
        :param start: (x, y) 起点
        :param end: (x, y) 终点
        :param duration: 滑动耗时
        """
        self.adb.swipe(start, end, duration=duration)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_screen(self, direction="up", scale=0.6, area=None):
        try:
            disp = self.adb.get_display_info()
            w, h = disp['width'], disp['height']
        except:
            w, h = 720, 1280

        if area:
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = map(int, area)
            bw, bh = x2 - x1, y2 - y1
            base_x, base_y = x1, y1
        else:
            bw, bh = w, h
            base_x, base_y = 0, 0

        cx, cy = base_x + bw * 0.5, base_y + bh * 0.5
        delta_h, delta_w = bh * scale * 0.5, bw * scale * 0.5

        if direction == "up":    start, end = (cx, cy + delta_h), (cx, cy - delta_h)
        elif direction == "down": start, end = (cx, cy - delta_h), (cx, cy + delta_h)
        elif direction == "left": start, end = (cx + delta_w, cy), (cx - delta_w, cy)
        elif direction == "right": start, end = (cx - delta_w, cy), (cx + delta_w, cy)
        else: return False

        self.adb.swipe(start, end) 
        sleep(0.8)

    @_safe_wrap
    def swipe_until_found(self, target, direction="up", scale=0.5, area=None, max_retry=5):
        for i in range(max_retry):
            self.check_interrupt() # 滑动前检查
            if self._exists_internal(target):
                return True
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_swipe_search", i+1))
            self.swipe_screen(direction=direction, scale=scale, area=area)
            time.sleep(0.5)
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def pinch(self, mode="in"):
        self.logger.warning(get_text("log_pinch_not_supported"))
        pass

    @_safe_wrap
    def input_text(self, content, confirm=True):
        self.adb.text(str(content), enter=confirm)
        sleep(1)

    @_safe_wrap
    def keyevent(self, keyname):
        """
        模拟按键事件
        :param keyname: 键名 (e.g. "BACK", "HOME")
        """
        self.adb.keyevent(keyname)
        sleep(0.5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def press_back(self):
        self.keyevent("BACK")

    @_safe_wrap
    def app_restart(self, package_name):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_app_restart", package_name))
        self.adb.stop_app(package_name)
        sleep(1)
        self.adb.start_app(package_name)
        sleep(5)

    @_safe_wrap
    def exists(self, target):
        return self._exists_internal(target)

    def _exists_internal(self, target):
        if isinstance(target, Template):
            pos = self._find_template_pos(target)
            return pos if pos else False
        return False

    @_safe_wrap
    def sleep(self, seconds):
        # 智能休眠：如果时间长，每隔 0.5s 检查一次中断
        end_time = time.time() + seconds
        while time.time() < end_time:
            self.check_interrupt()
            remaining = end_time - time.time()
            time.sleep(min(0.5, remaining))

    # ==========================================
    # 👁️ 数据获取
    # ==========================================
    def get_text(self, area=None):
        return ""

    def parse_number(self, text):
        if not text: return 0
        s = str(text).lower().replace(" ", "").replace(",", "")
        multiplier = 1
        if 'k' in s:
            multiplier = 1000
            s = s.replace('k', '')
        elif 'm' in s:
            multiplier = 1000000
            s = s.replace('m', '')
        try:
            val = float(s)
            return int(val * multiplier)
        except:
            return 0

    # ==========================================
    # 🏠 导航与状态
    # ==========================================
    def check_resolution(self):
        try:
            output = self.adb.shell("wm size")
            if not output: return False
            import re
            match = re.search(r"(\d+)x(\d+)", output)
            if match:
                w, h = int(match.group(1)), int(match.group(2))
                if (w == 720 and h == 1280) or (w == 1280 and h == 720): return True
            return False
        except:
            return False

    def ensure_home(self):

        img_home = self.IMG_HOME_CHECK
        if not img_home:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_home_resource_missing"))
            return False
        # img_home = Template(r"assets/images/home/home_check.png")
        
        # 兼容打包后的路径
        if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
            if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS'):
                base_path = sys._MEIPASS
            else:
                base_path = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0]))
        else:
            base_path = "."
            
        img_island = Template(os.path.join(base_path, r"assets/images/home/island.png"))
        img_town_btn = Template(os.path.join(base_path, r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png"))
        
        if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_island):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        if self.exists(img_town_btn):
            self.click(img_town_btn)
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        
        self.logger.warning(get_text("log_force_reset"))

        for _ in range(10):
            self.press_back()
            if self.exists(img_home): return True
        return False

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import shutil
import sys
import subprocess


def clean_pycache():
    print("Cleaning __pycache__ and .pyc files...")
    root_dir = os.getcwd() # 获取当前目录
    for root, dirs, files in os.walk(root_dir):
        # 1. 删除 __pycache__ 文件夹
        if "__pycache__" in dirs:
            cache_dir = os.path.join(root, "__pycache__")
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(cache_dir)
                print(f"Removed: {cache_dir}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to remove {cache_dir}: {e}")
            dirs.remove("__pycache__") # 防止 os.walk 继续遍历已删除的目录

        # 2. (可选) 删除散落的 .pyc 文件
        for file in files:
            if file.endswith(".pyc"):
                file_path = os.path.join(root, file)
                try:
                    os.remove(file_path)
                except:
                    pass

def install_requirements():
    print("Checking requirements...")
    try:
        import PyInstaller
        print("PyInstaller is already installed.")
    except ImportError:
        print("PyInstaller not found. Installing...")
        subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", "pyinstaller"])

def build():
    clean_pycache()
    import PyInstaller.__main__

    # Define the app name
    APP_NAME = "KSAS"
    MAIN_SCRIPT = "KSAS.py"
    
    # Clean previous builds
    if os.path.exists('build'):
        shutil.rmtree('build')
    if os.path.exists('dist'):
        shutil.rmtree('dist')
    
    # PyInstaller arguments
    args = [
        MAIN_SCRIPT,
        f'--name={APP_NAME}',
        '--onedir',                 # Pack into a directory (faster startup, easier to debug)
        '--windowed',               # No console window
        '--clean',
        '--noconfirm',
        '--icon=assets/logo.ico', 
        
        # Include data folders (source;destination)
        '--add-data=assets;assets',
        '--add-data=i18n;i18n',
        '--add-data=tools;tools',
        '--add-data=data/defaults;data/defaults',
        
        # Hidden imports
        '--hidden-import=PySide6',
        '--hidden-import=yaml',
        '--hidden-import=cv2',
        '--hidden-import=numpy',
        '--hidden-import=airtest',
        '--hidden-import=pocoui',
        '--hidden-import=win32timezone', # Sometimes needed for datetime
        
        # Collect Airtest dependencies
        '--collect-all=airtest',
        '--collect-all=poco',
        '--collect-all=pywinauto',
    ]
    
    print(f"Starting build for {APP_NAME}...")
    PyInstaller.__main__.run(args)
    print("Build finished! Check the 'dist' folder.")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    install_requirements()
    build()

FILE: build_nuitka.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import shutil
import sys
import subprocess
import airtest # <--- 1. 导入 airtest 以便定位它的路径

# 定义输出的基础目录
APP_NAME = "KSAS"
DIST_DIR = os.path.join("nuitka_dist", f"{APP_NAME}.dist")

def clean_build_dirs():
    print("Cleaning previous build directories...")
    # 保留 build 缓存，加快再次打包速度
    # 注意：不要删除 'nuitka_dist' 根目录，否则会把 KSAS.build 缓存也删掉
    dirs_to_clean = [DIST_DIR, 'build', 'dist', f'{APP_NAME}.dist', f'{APP_NAME}.onefile-build']
    for d in dirs_to_clean:
        if os.path.exists(d):
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(d)
                print(f"Removed: {d}")
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"Failed to remove {d}: {e}")

def copy_folder(src, dst_relative):
    """
    手动复制文件夹的辅助函数
    """
    target_dir = os.path.join(DIST_DIR, dst_relative)
    
    if not os.path.exists(src):
        print(f"⚠️ Warning: Source folder '{src}' not found, skipping.")
        return

    # 如果目标目录的上级目录不存在，先创建上级目录
    parent_dir = os.path.dirname(target_dir)
    if not os.path.exists(parent_dir):
        os.makedirs(parent_dir)

    # 如果目标已存在，先删掉
    if os.path.exists(target_dir):
        shutil.rmtree(target_dir)
    
    print(f"📂 Copying '{src}' -> '{target_dir}' ...")
    try:
        shutil.copytree(src, target_dir)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Failed to copy {src}: {e}")

def install_requirements():
    print("Checking build requirements...")
    required = ["nuitka", "zstandard"] 
    for req in required:
        try:
            __import__(req)
        except ImportError:
            print(f"{req} not found. Installing...")
            subprocess.check_call([sys.executable, "-m", "pip", "install", req])

def fix_airtest_apks():
    """
    专门修复 Airtest 缺少 Yosemite.apk 的问题
    """
    print("\n🔧 Fixing Airtest Dependencies (Yosemite.apk)...")
    try:
        # 1. 找到你电脑上 Airtest 的安装位置
        airtest_root = os.path.dirname(airtest.__file__)
        
        # 2. 定位源路径: airtest/core/android/static/apks
        src_apks = os.path.join(airtest_root, "core", "android", "static", "apks")
        
        # 3. 定位目标路径: dist/airtest/core/android/static/apks
        # 注意：这里必须保持目录结构完全一致，因为 Airtest 代码是硬编码查找的
        dst_relative = os.path.join("airtest", "core", "android", "static", "apks")
        
        if os.path.exists(src_apks):
            copy_folder(src_apks, dst_relative)
            print("✅ Airtest APKs copied successfully!")
        else:
            print(f"⚠️ Warning: Could not find Airtest APKs at {src_apks}")
            
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"❌ Failed to fix Airtest APKs: {e}")

def build():
    clean_build_dirs()
    
    MAIN_SCRIPT = "KSAS.py"
    
    nuitka_args = [
        sys.executable, "-m", "nuitka",
        "--standalone",
        "--mingw64",
        "--show-progress",
        "--show-memory",
        "--enable-plugin=pyside6",
        "--disable-console",
        f"--windows-icon-from-ico=assets/logo.ico",
        "--output-dir=nuitka_dist",
        
        # === 依赖包 ===
        "--include-package=airtest",
        "--include-package=pywinauto",
        "--include-package=cv2",
        "--include-package=numpy",
        "--include-package=yaml",     
        "--include-package=requests", 
        "--include-package=PIL",      
        
        "--noinclude-pytest-mode=nofollow",
        "--noinclude-setuptools-mode=nofollow",
        
        MAIN_SCRIPT
    ]
    
    print(f"Starting Nuitka build for {APP_NAME}...")
    
    try:
        # 1. 编译
        subprocess.check_call(nuitka_args)
        
        print("\n" + "="*50)
        print("✅ Compilation finished. Now copying resources manually...")
        
        # 2. === 修复 Airtest 缺失文件 (新增步骤) ===
        fix_airtest_apks()

        # 3. === 复制项目资源 ===
        copy_folder(os.path.join("tools", "adb"), os.path.join("tools", "adb"))
        copy_folder("assets", "assets")
        copy_folder("i18n", "i18n")
        copy_folder(os.path.join("data", "defaults"), os.path.join("data", "defaults"))
        
        print("="*50)
        print("🎉 Build Process Complete!")
        print(f"🚀 Executable:    {os.path.join(DIST_DIR, APP_NAME + '.exe')}")
        print("="*50)
            
    except subprocess.CalledProcessError as e:
        print(f"\n❌ Build failed with error code {e.returncode}")
        sys.exit(1)
    except Exception as e:
        print(f"\n❌ An error occurred: {e}")
        sys.exit(1)

if __name__ == "__main__":
    install_requirements()
    build()

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import logging
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class GlobalConfig:
    _instance = None
    
    DEFAULT_CONFIG = {
        "language": "zh_CN",
        "close_to_tray": False,   # 默认关闭：点击关闭按钮直接退出
        "auto_connect": True,    # 启动时自动连接
        "adb_path": "",          # 自定义 ADB 路径
        "use_internal_adb": True, # 是否使用内置 ADB
        "license_key": ""        # KeyAuth 卡密
    }

    def __new__(cls, *args, **kwargs):
        if not cls._instance:
            cls._instance = super(GlobalConfig, cls).__new__(cls, *args, **kwargs)
        return cls._instance

    def __init__(self, config_path="config.json"):
        if hasattr(self, "_initialized"):
            return
        self.config_path = config_path
        self.config = self.DEFAULT_CONFIG.copy()
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("GlobalConfig")
        self.load()
        self._initialized = True

    def load(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.config_path):
            try:
                with open(self.config_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    data = json.load(f)
                    # 合并配置，确保新字段存在
                    for key, val in data.items():
                        self.config[key] = val
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_load_config_failed", e))

    def save(self):
        try:
            with open(self.config_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self.config, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_save_config_failed", e))

    def get(self, key, default=None):
        return self.config.get(key, default)

    def set(self, key, value):
        self.config[key] = value
        self.save()

# 全局单例
global_config = GlobalConfig()


==================================================
FILE: datastore.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import json
import threading
import logging
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

class InstanceDataStore(QObject):
    """
    单个实例的数据管家 (Per-Instance Data Manager)
    
    职责：
    1. 管理 config.json (用户配置：读多写少)
    2. 管理 statistics.json (游戏数据：写多读少)
    3. 维护 runtime memory (内存状态：不存盘)
    4. 作为 UI 和 后台脚本 的通讯枢纽 (Signals)
    """

    # ==================================================
    # 📡 信号定义 (Signals for UI)
    # ==================================================
    
    # 1. 配置变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：代码里重置了配置 -> UI 复选框自动更新
    sig_config_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 2. 统计/资源数据变更信号: (key_path, new_value)
    # 场景：脚本 OCR 读到了新体力 -> UI 进度条刷新
    sig_stats_updated = Signal(str, object)

    # 3. 运行时状态变更信号: (key, new_value)
    # 场景：ADB 断开连接 -> UI 状态灯变红
    sig_runtime_changed = Signal(str, object)

    # 4. 日志信号: (level, message)
    # 场景：把后台的 print/log 转发到 UI 的文本框里
    sig_log_msg = Signal(str, str)

    def __init__(self, instance_id, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        
        # --- 路径配置 ---
        # 你的结构: data/deep_sl_002/
        self.base_dir = os.path.join(data_root, instance_id)
        self.config_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "config.json")
        self.stats_path = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "statistics.json")

        # --- 线程安全锁 ---
        # RLock (可重入锁) 允许同一个线程多次获得锁，防止死锁
        self._lock = threading.RLock()

        # --- 内存数据容器 ---
        self._config = {}   # 对应 config.json
        self._stats = {}    # 对应 statistics.json
        self._runtime = {   # 纯内存状态 (关机即焚)
            "is_running": False,       # 调度器运行状态
            "connect_status": "OFFLINE", # ADB 连接状态
            "current_task": "Idle"     # 当前正在执行的任务名
        }

        # --- 初始化 ---
        self._ensure_dirs()
        self.load_all()

    def _ensure_dirs(self):
        """确保专属文件夹及子文件夹结构完整"""
        # 1. 创建实例主目录 (data/deep_sl_002)
        if not os.path.exists(self.base_dir):
            os.makedirs(self.base_dir, exist_ok=True)
            
        # 2. 创建日志目录 (data/deep_sl_002/log)
        log_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "log")
        if not os.path.exists(log_dir):
            os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

        # 3. 创建截图目录 (data/deep_sl_002/snapshots)
        snap_dir = os.path.join(self.base_dir, "snapshots")
        if not os.path.exists(snap_dir):
            os.makedirs(snap_dir, exist_ok=True)

    def load_all(self):
        """加载所有持久化数据"""
        # 1. 加载配置 (带默认值)
        default_config = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_config.json")
        if default_config:
            # 动态设置 instance_name 默认值
            default_config["instance_name"] = self.instance_id
        else:
            # Fallback if file missing
            default_config = {"instance_name": self.instance_id}
            
        self._load_json(self.config_path, self._config, default_config)

        # 2. 加载统计 (默认为空)
        default_stats = self._load_default_json("data/defaults/default_statistics.json")
        if not default_stats:
             default_stats = {}
             
        self._load_json(self.stats_path, self._stats, default_stats)

    def _load_default_json(self, path):
        """从模板文件加载默认配置"""
        try:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    return json.load(f)
        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error loading default json {path}: {e}")
        return None

    def _load_json(self, path, container, default):
        """通用的安全读取逻辑 (带简单的二级补全)"""
        with self._lock:
            if os.path.exists(path):
                try:
                    with open(path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                        saved_data = json.load(f)
                        
                        # 1. 先把默认值深拷贝进来
                        # (这样能保证拥有最全的字段结构)
                        import copy
                        merged_data = copy.deepcopy(default)
                        
                        # 2. 递归更新 (这里只做两层简单的合并，够用了)
                        for key, val in saved_data.items():
                            if isinstance(val, dict) and key in merged_data and isinstance(merged_data[key], dict):
                                # 如果是字典（比如 modules），进去更新里面的字段
                                merged_data[key].update(val)
                            else:
                                # 如果是普通值，直接覆盖
                                merged_data[key] = val
                        
                        # 3. 更新到内存容器
                        container.clear()
                        container.update(merged_data)
                        
                except Exception as e:
                    self.push_log(f"文件 {os.path.basename(path)} 损坏/解析失败: {e}", "ERROR")
                    # 损坏了就用默认值
                    container.clear()
                    container.update(default)
                    self._save_json(path, container)
            else:
                # 文件不存在，初始化为默认值
                container.update(default)
                self._save_json(path, container)

    def _save_json(self, path, container):
        """通用的安全保存逻辑"""
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 临时写入再重命名，防止写入一半断电导致文件损坏 (Atomic Write 思想)
                temp_path = path + ".tmp"
                with open(temp_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    json.dump(container, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
                
                if os.path.exists(path):
                    os.remove(path)
                os.rename(temp_path, path)
            except Exception as e:
                self.push_log(f"保存 {os.path.basename(path)} 失败: {e}", "ERROR")

    def save_config(self):
        """手动触发保存配置"""
        self._save_json(self.config_path, self._config)

    def save_stats(self):
        """手动触发保存统计"""
        self._save_json(self.stats_path, self._stats)

    # ==================================================
    # 🔧 数据操作接口 (Getters & Setters)
    # ==================================================

    # --- 1. 用户配置 (Config) ---
    
    def get_cfg(self, key_path, default=None):
        """
        读取配置
        用法: get_cfg("modules.hunting.enable")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, default)

    def set_cfg(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改配置 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_cfg("modules.hunting.enable", True)
        """
        # 如果值确实改变了
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._config, key_path, value):
            self.save_config() # 持久化
            self.sig_config_changed.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 2. 游戏数据 (Statistics) ---
    
    def get_stat(self, key_path, default=0):
        """
        读取游戏统计
        用法: get_stat("resources.wood")
        """
        return self._get_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, default)

    def set_stat(self, key_path, value):
        """
        修改游戏数据 -> 立即保存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_stat("stamina", 80)
        """
        if self._set_value_by_path(self._stats, key_path, value):
            self.save_stats() # 持久化
            self.sig_stats_updated.emit(key_path, value) # 通知 UI

    # --- 3. 运行时状态 (Runtime Memory) ---

    def get_runtime(self, key, default=None):
        """读取内存状态"""
        with self._lock:
            return self._runtime.get(key, default)

    def set_runtime(self, key, value):
        """
        修改运行时状态 -> 仅内存 -> 通知 UI
        用法: set_runtime("connect_status", "ONLINE")
        """
        with self._lock:
            if self._runtime.get(key) != value:
                self._runtime[key] = value
                self.sig_runtime_changed.emit(key, value) # 通知 UI

    # ==================================================
    # 🔨 辅助工具 (Helpers)
    # ==================================================

    def push_log(self, message, level="INFO"):
        """
        发送日志信号 (给 UI 显示用)
        并不负责写 log 文件 (写文件由 logging 模块在别处处理)
        """
        self.sig_log_msg.emit(level, message)

    def _get_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, default):
        """
        [内部工具] 解析点号路径: 'a.b.c' -> dict['a']['b']['c']
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                for k in keys:
                    data = data[k]
                return data
            except (KeyError, TypeError):
                return default

    def _set_value_by_path(self, root_dict, path, value):
        """
        [内部工具] 设置点号路径: 'a.b.c' = value
        返回 True 表示值发生了变化 (需要保存/刷新)
        返回 False 表示值没变 (节省 IO)
        """
        keys = path.split('.')
        data = root_dict
        with self._lock:
            try:
                # 递归找到倒数第二层
                for k in keys[:-1]:
                    # 如果中间层不存在，自动创建空字典
                    data = data.setdefault(k, {})
                
                last_key = keys[-1]
                old_value = data.get(last_key)
                
                if old_value != value:
                    data[last_key] = value
                    return True # 变了
                return False # 没变
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"设置键值失败 {path}: {e}")
                return False

# 文件结束

FILE: instance_manager.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
# instance_manager.py
import os
import json
import logging
import re
import time
import shutil
from datetime import datetime
from PySide6.QtCore import QObject, Signal

from datastore import InstanceDataStore
from scheduler import InstanceScheduler
from task_registry import get_all_tasks
from basic import DeviceOperator
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class InstanceManager(QObject):
    """
    实例管理器
    """
    sig_instance_list_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, data_root="data"):
        super().__init__()
        self.data_root = data_root
        self.registry_path = os.path.join(data_root, "device_registry.json")
        self.logger = logging.getLogger("InstanceManager")

        self.data_stores = {} 
        self.schedulers = {} 
        self._registry = {}

        self._load_registry()
        self._init_all_instances()

    def _load_registry(self):
        if os.path.exists(self.registry_path):
            try:
                with open(self.registry_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                    content = f.read().strip()
                    if content:
                        self._registry = json.loads(content)
                    else:
                        self._registry = {}
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_registry_corrupted", e))
                self._registry = {}
        else:
            self._registry = {}

    def _save_registry(self):
        try:
            with open(self.registry_path, 'w', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                json.dump(self._registry, f, indent=4, ensure_ascii=False)
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_save_registry_failed", e))

    def _init_all_instances(self):
        """启动时加载所有已注册账号"""
        task_classes = get_all_tasks()

        for instance_id, info in self._registry.items():
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_loading_instance", info.get('display_name'), instance_id))
            store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = store
            
            try:
                device = DeviceOperator(info['serial'], instance_id)
                # device.connect() # 启动时不强制连接，为了速度
                sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, store, task_classes)
                
                # 🔄 【绑定】设置 DeviceOperator 的中断回调
                # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理，避免闭包引用问题)
                # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
                
                self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched
                
                # 🧹 启动自动清理 (异步或同步均可，这里用同步简单处理，因为文件IO通常很快)
                self._cleanup_files(instance_id)
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_load_scheduler_failed", instance_id, e))

    def create_instance(self, display_name, serial, diana_level=0, march_slots=5):
        safe_name = re.sub(r'[^a-zA-Z0-9]', '_', display_name).lower()
        instance_id = safe_name
        counter = 1
        while instance_id in self._registry:
            instance_id = f"{safe_name}_{counter}"
            counter += 1

        try:
            self._registry[instance_id] = {
                "display_name": display_name,
                "serial": serial,
                "created_at": datetime.now().strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
            }
            self._save_registry()

            new_store = InstanceDataStore(instance_id, self.data_root)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.serial", serial)
            new_store.set_cfg("meta.alias", display_name)
            # 设置 Diana 等级和行军队列
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(diana_level))
            new_store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(march_slots))
            
            self.data_stores[instance_id] = new_store

            device = DeviceOperator(serial, instance_id)
            device.connect()
            
            task_classes = get_all_tasks()
            sched = InstanceScheduler(instance_id, device, new_store, task_classes)
            
            # 🔄 【绑定】设置中断回调
            # (已移至 Scheduler.__init__ 内部处理)
            # device.interrupt_callback = lambda: not sched.is_running_flag
            
            self.schedulers[instance_id] = sched

            self.logger.info(get_text("log_instance_created", display_name))
            self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
            return True, instance_id

        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_create_instance_failed", e))
            if instance_id in self._registry:
                del self._registry[instance_id]
            return False, str(e)

    def delete_instance(self, instance_id):
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_deleting_instance", instance_id))

        if instance_id in self.schedulers:
            try:
                sched = self.schedulers[instance_id]
                if sched.isRunning():
                    sched.stop_scheduler()
                
                # 确保移除日志 Handler，防止内存泄漏或重复日志
                if hasattr(sched, 'dispose'):
                    sched.dispose()
                    
                time.sleep(0.1)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_stop_scheduler_error", e))
            del self.schedulers[instance_id]

        if instance_id in self.data_stores:
            del self.data_stores[instance_id]

        dir_path = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
        if os.path.exists(dir_path):
            try:
                shutil.rmtree(dir_path)
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_folder_deleted", dir_path))
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_delete_failed", e))

        del self._registry[instance_id]
        self._save_registry()
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()

    def update_instance(self, instance_id, new_serial=None, new_alias=None, new_diana_level=None, new_march_slots=None):
        """
        更新实例信息
        """
        if instance_id not in self._registry:
            return False

        # 1. 更新注册表 (只存核心信息)
        if new_serial:
            self._registry[instance_id]['serial'] = new_serial
        if new_alias:
            self._registry[instance_id]['display_name'] = new_alias
        self._save_registry()

        # 2. 更新 DataStore (存配置)
        store = self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
        if store:
            if new_serial:
                store.set_cfg("meta.serial", new_serial)
                if instance_id in self.schedulers:
                    self.schedulers[instance_id].device.serial = new_serial
            if new_alias:
                store.set_cfg("meta.alias", new_alias)
            
            # 👇 【新增】保存 Diana 等级
            if new_diana_level is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", int(new_diana_level))

            # 👇 【新增】保存行军队列数量
            if new_march_slots is not None:
                store.set_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", int(new_march_slots))
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_instance_conf_updated", instance_id))
        self.sig_instance_list_changed.emit()
        return True

    def get_all_instances(self):
        result = []
        for iid, info in self._registry.items():
            store = self.data_stores.get(iid)
            status = store.get_runtime("connect_status", "OFFLINE") if store else "ERROR"
            result.append({
                "id": iid,
                "name": info["display_name"],
                "serial": info["serial"],
                "status": status
            })
        return result

    def get_store(self, instance_id):
        return self.data_stores.get(instance_id)
    
    def get_scheduler(self, instance_id):
        return self.schedulers.get(instance_id)

    def _cleanup_files(self, instance_id):
        """清理过期日志和截图 (保留7天)"""
        try:
            base_dir = os.path.join(self.data_root, instance_id)
            targets = [
                os.path.join(base_dir, "log"),
                os.path.join(base_dir, "snapshots")
            ]
            
            now = time.time()
            retention_days = 7
            retention_sec = retention_days * 86400
            
            for folder in targets:
                if not os.path.exists(folder):
                    continue
                    
                for filename in os.listdir(folder):
                    file_path = os.path.join(folder, filename)
                    # 仅处理文件
                    if os.path.isfile(file_path):
                        # 获取修改时间
                        mtime = os.path.getmtime(file_path)
                        if now - mtime > retention_sec:
                            try:
                                os.remove(file_path)
                                self.logger.info(get_text("log_cleanup_file", instance_id, filename))
                            except Exception as del_err:
                                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_cleanup_file_failed", filename, del_err))
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_cleanup_error", instance_id, e))

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import sys
import os
import subprocess
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication, QStyleFactory
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QListView
from PySide6.QtGui import QPalette, QColor

# 限制第三方库线程 (优化性能)
os.environ["OMP_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["MKL_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["PADDLE_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"
os.environ["OPENCV_NUM_THREADS"] = "1"

import logging
import airtest.core.api

# ==============================================================================
# 🩹 Monkeypatch: 修复打包后 Airtest 找不到 assets 图片的问题
# ==============================================================================
_original_template_init = airtest.core.api.Template.__init__

def _patched_template_init(self, filename, *args, **kwargs):
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False) and isinstance(filename, str):
        # 如果是相对路径，尝试在临时目录找
        if not os.path.isabs(filename):
            if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS'):
                base_path = sys._MEIPASS
            else:
                base_path = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0]))
            
            possible_path = os.path.join(base_path, filename)
            if os.path.exists(possible_path):
                filename = possible_path
    _original_template_init(self, filename, *args, **kwargs)

airtest.core.api.Template.__init__ = _patched_template_init
# ==============================================================================

from PySide6.QtWidgets import QApplication
from ui.main_window_logic import MainWindowLogic

from config import global_config
# 引入 Airtest 常量
from airtest.core.android import constant

# 引入 KeyAuth
from tools.keyauth.keyauth import api
from ui.login_dialog import LoginDialog
from tools.resource_loader import ResourceLoader
from i18n.i18n import get_text

def getchecksum():
    # 简单的 checksum 占位符，实际应读取文件 MD5
    return "checksum_placeholder"

def init_keyauth():
    return api(
        name = "KSAS",
        ownerid = "NVEZ7gY47U",
        version = "1.0",
        hash_to_check = getchecksum()
    )

def setup_global_adb():
    """
    设置 Airtest 全局 ADB 路径 (绝对路径 + 和平共处版)
    """
    logger.info("-" * 30)
    logger.info(get_text("log_adb_init"))
    
    use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
    custom_path = global_config.get("adb_path", "")
    
    target_adb = None
    
    # 1. 【核心修改】获取 KSAS.py 文件所在的绝对目录
    # 这确保了无论脚本在哪里运行，都能找到旁边的 tools 文件夹
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS'):
            project_root = sys._MEIPASS
        else:
            project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0]))
    else:
        project_root = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
    
    if use_internal:
        # 拼接绝对路径
        internal_path = os.path.join(project_root, "tools", "adb", "adb.exe")
        if os.path.exists(internal_path):
            target_adb = internal_path
        else:
            logger.error(get_text("log_adb_internal_not_found", internal_path))
    elif custom_path and os.path.exists(custom_path):
        target_adb = custom_path
            
    if target_adb:
        adb_dir = os.path.dirname(target_adb)
        logger.info(get_text("log_adb_target_locked", target_adb))
        
        # 2. 【核心】修改 Airtest 内部常量
        constant.ADB_PATH = target_adb
        
        # 3. 【核心】将我们的 ADB 目录插入系统 PATH 的第一位
        # 这能拦截大部分直接调用 "adb" 命令的库
        os.environ["PATH"] = adb_dir + os.pathsep + os.environ["PATH"]

        # 4. 【核心】修正 adbutils 库的指向 (如果有的话)
        # 这就是解决你刚才看到“指向 site-packages”问题的关键
        try:
            import adbutils
            adbutils._adb_path = target_adb
            logger.info("[System] adbutils path corrected")
        except ImportError:
            pass
            
    else:
        logger.warning(get_text("log_adb_fallback"))
    
    logger.info("-" * 30)

# 配置日志
logging.basicConfig(
    level=logging.INFO,
    format='%(asctime)s - [%(name)s] - %(levelname)s - %(message)s',
    datefmt='%H:%M:%S'
)
logger = logging.getLogger("Main")

if __name__ == "__main__":

FILE: logging_setup.py

# logging_setup.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import logging
import os
# 👇 引入轮转文件处理器
from logging.handlers import RotatingFileHandler

class GuiLogHandler(logging.Handler):
    """
    自定义日志处理器：拦截日志并转发给 DataStore -> UI
    """
    def __init__(self, datastore):
        super().__init__()
        self.datastore = datastore
        self.instance_id = datastore.instance_id
        # 设置格式: 只取消息内容
        self.setFormatter(logging.Formatter("%(message)s"))

    def emit(self, record):
        """
        当有日志产生时触发
        """
        try:
            msg = self.format(record)
            level = record.levelname
            # 拼装 ID，防止 UI 串台
            full_msg = f"{self.instance_id}|||{msg}"
            self.datastore.push_log(full_msg, level)
        except Exception:
            self.handleError(record)

def setup_file_logging(instance_id, log_dir):
    """
    给指定实例挂载文件日志功能 (带自动切割功能)
    :return: Handler 对象
    """
    logger = logging.getLogger(instance_id)
    logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
    
    # 1. 检查是否已经有文件处理器了 (防止重复添加)
    for h in logger.handlers:
        if isinstance(h, RotatingFileHandler):
            return h

    # 2. 确保目录存在
    if not os.path.exists(log_dir):
        os.makedirs(log_dir, exist_ok=True)

    # 3. 创建轮转文件处理器 (核心修改)
    log_path = os.path.join(log_dir, "ksas.log")
    
    # maxBytes=2*1024*1024 (2MB)
    # backupCount=3 (最多保留3个旧文件，比如 ksas.log.1, ksas.log.2)
    file_handler = RotatingFileHandler(
        log_path, 
        mode='a', 
        maxBytes=2*1024*1024, 
        backupCount=3, 
        encoding='utf-8'
    )
    
    # 4. 设置格式
    formatter = logging.Formatter('%(asctime)s - [%(name)s] - %(levelname)s - %(message)s')
    file_handler.setFormatter(formatter)
    
    # 5. 挂载
    logger.addHandler(file_handler)
    
    return file_handler

==================================================
FILE: pack_project.py
==================================================
import os

# =================配置区域=================

# 1. 黑名单：完全忽略这些目录，连文件名都不看 (节省大量 Token)
IGNORE_DIRS = {
    "venv", ".venv", "ksas_env", "__pycache__", ".git", ".idea", ".vscode", 
    "build", "dist", "nuitka_dist", "KSAS.build", "KSAS.dist", # 构建目录
    "paddleocr", "snapshots", "log", "backup", # 运行时垃圾
    "assets", # 图片资源通常不需要发给AI看代码
    "ui/generated" # 自动生成的代码通常不需要看
}

# 2. 白名单后缀：只读取这些文件的内容
INCLUDE_EXTS = {".py", ".json", ".yaml", ".ui", ".md"}

# 3. 【核心功能】聚焦列表
# 如果列表为空 []，则打包整个项目（除了黑名单）。
# 如果列表有内容，例如 ["ui", "config.py"]，则只打包这些指定的文件或文件夹。
FOCUS_TARGETS = [
    # "ui", 
    # "tasks",
    # "config.py" 
]

# =========================================

def is_target(path):
    """判断文件或目录是否在聚焦列表中"""
    if not FOCUS_TARGETS:
        return True
    
    # 路径标准化，防止 sep 差异
    norm_path = os.path.normpath(path)
    
    for target in FOCUS_TARGETS:
        norm_target = os.path.normpath(target)
        # 检查是否是目标本身，或者是目标的子文件/子目录
        if norm_path == norm_target or norm_path.startswith(norm_target + os.sep):
            return True
    return False

def pack_project():
    output_file = "project_summary.txt"
    
    with open(output_file, "w", encoding="utf-8") as out:
        # --- 第一部分：目录结构 (Tree) ---
        out.write("=== PROJECT STRUCTURE (Simplified) ===\n")
        out.write("./\n")
        
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
            # 1. 原地修改 dirs 列表，剪除黑名单目录，阻止 os.walk 进入
            # 这一步非常关键，它能阻止扫描 nuitka_dist 下的几万个文件
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            # 如果开启了聚焦模式，且当前目录不在聚焦路径上，只打印目录名但不展开（或者完全跳过）
            # 这里简化处理：结构树还是打印出来，方便AI了解整体架构，但内容只打印聚焦的
            
            level = root.replace(os.getcwd(), '').count(os.sep)
            indent = ' ' * 4 * level
            
            # 只显示非根目录
            if root != ".":
                out.write(f"{indent}{os.path.basename(root)}/\n")
                
            subindent = ' ' * 4 * (level + 1)
            for f in files:
                ext = os.path.splitext(f)[1]
                if ext in INCLUDE_EXTS or f == "requirements.txt":
                     out.write(f"{subindent}{f}\n")

        out.write("\n\n=== FILE CONTENTS ===\n")

        # --- 第二部分：文件内容 ---
        for root, dirs, files in os.walk("."):
            # 同样剪除黑名单
            dirs[:] = [d for d in dirs if d not in IGNORE_DIRS]
            
            for filename in files:
                ext = os.path.splitext(filename)[1]
                filepath = os.path.join(root, filename)
                
                # 1. 检查后缀白名单
                if ext not in INCLUDE_EXTS:
                    continue
                
                # 2. 检查聚焦模式 (这是关键！)
                # 相对路径，例如 "ui\main_window.py"
                rel_path = os.path.relpath(filepath, ".")
                if not is_target(rel_path):
                    continue

                # 写入分隔符和文件名
                out.write(f"\n{'='*50}\n")
                out.write(f"FILE: {rel_path}\n")
                out.write(f"{'='*50}\n")
                
                try:
                    with open(filepath, "r", encoding="utf-8") as f:
                        content = f.read()
                        out.write(content)
                except Exception as e:
                    out.write(f"[Error reading file: {e}]")
                
                out.write("\n")

    print(f"✅ 打包完成！")
    if FOCUS_TARGETS:
        print(f"🎯 聚焦模式已开启，仅包含: {FOCUS_TARGETS}")
    else:
        print(f"📂 全量模式 (已忽略构建目录)")

if __name__ == "__main__":
    pack_project()

==================================================
FILE: scheduler.py
==================================================
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import time
import logging
import threading
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from PySide6.QtCore import QThread, Signal

# 引入日志桥梁
from logging_setup import GuiLogHandler, setup_file_logging
# 引入异常
from basic import TaskInterruptedError
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class InstanceScheduler(QThread):
    """
    ALAS 风格的智能调度器 (优化版)
    
    特性：
    1. 任务调度：原子执行 + 循环扫描。
    2. 体力回复：独立倒计时逻辑 (5分钟回1点)，不受任务打断。
    """
    
    def __init__(self, instance_id, device, data, task_classes):
        super().__init__()
        self.setObjectName(f"Scheduler_{instance_id}")
        self.instance_id = instance_id
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{instance_id}.Scheduler")
        
        # 1. 挂载日志桥梁 (UI显示)
        self.instance_root_logger = logging.getLogger(instance_id)
        self.instance_root_logger.setLevel(logging.INFO)
        self.gui_handler = GuiLogHandler(data)
        self.instance_root_logger.addHandler(self.gui_handler)
        
        # 2. 挂载文件日志 (文件存储)
        import os
        log_dir = os.path.join(data.base_dir, "log")
        self.file_handler = setup_file_logging(instance_id, log_dir)

        self._is_running = False
        
        # 3. 智能闹钟 (Event)
        self._wake_event = threading.Event()

        # 4. ⏳ 独立体力计时器 (Next Stamina Tick)
        # 存储下一次回复体力的绝对时间 (datetime 对象)
        # 初始化为 None，表示闹钟目前是关着的
        self._next_stamina_tick = None
        
        # 5. 信号连接
        self.data.sig_config_changed.connect(self.on_config_changed)
        self.data.sig_stats_updated.connect(self.on_stat_changed)

        # 6. 装载任务实例
        self.tasks = []
        for TaskCls in task_classes:
            try:
                task_obj = TaskCls(device, data)
                self.tasks.append(task_obj)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_task_init_failed", TaskCls.__name__, e))

        # 7. 绑定中断回调 (直接绑定到当前实例的方法)
        self.device.interrupt_callback = self._check_interrupt

    def _check_interrupt(self):
        """
        检查是否应该中断设备操作
        返回 True 表示需要中断
        """
        # 如果调度器不在运行状态，则中断
        return not self._is_running

    # ==========================================
    # 📡 信号处理
    # ==========================================
    
    def on_config_changed(self, key, value):
        """配置变更：立即唤醒"""
        if self._is_running:
            self._wake_event.set()

    def on_stat_changed(self, key, value):
        """任务时间变更：立即唤醒"""
        if self._is_running and "next_run" in key:
            self._wake_event.set()

    # ==========================================
    # 🎮 控制接口
    # ==========================================
    
    @property
    def is_running_flag(self):
        """对外暴露的运行状态标志"""
        # self.logger.debug(f"Checking is_running_flag: {self._is_running}")
        return self._is_running

    def start_scheduler(self):
        if not self.isRunning():
            self._is_running = True
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", True)
            self._wake_event.clear()
            self.start()
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_started"))

    def stop_scheduler(self):
        if self.isRunning():
            self._is_running = False
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", False)
            self._wake_event.set() # 唤醒退出
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_stopping"))
            self.wait()
            
            # self.instance_root_logger.removeHandler(self.gui_handler)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_stopped"))

    def dispose(self):
        """
        销毁调度器时的清理工作
        """
        try:
            self.instance_root_logger.removeHandler(self.gui_handler)
        except Exception:
            pass

    # ==========================================
    # 🔄 核心循环 (大脑)
    # ==========================================

    def run(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_thread_started"))
        
        # 1. 飞行前安检
        if not self.device.connect():
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_device_connect_fail_stop"))
            self.data.set_runtime("is_running", False)
            return

        # 🏠 启动时先尝试回到首页 (初始化状态)
        try:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_init_game_state"))
            self.device.ensure_home()
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_init_home_fail", e))

        while self._is_running:
            self._wake_event.clear()
            now = datetime.now()
            
            # ==========================================
            # 🟢 模块一：体力自动回复逻辑 (独立倒计时)
            # ==========================================
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            
            if current_stamina >= 200:
                # 满体力：关闭闹钟
                self._next_stamina_tick = None
            else:
                # 不满体力：检查闹钟
                if self._next_stamina_tick is None:
                    # 如果闹钟没开，现在立刻开启 (当前时间 + 300秒)
                    self._next_stamina_tick = now + timedelta(seconds=300)
                    # self.logger.debug(f"体力不满，开始回复计时 (下次: {self._next_stamina_tick.strftime('%H:%M:%S')})")
                
                # 检查时间是否到了
                if now >= self._next_stamina_tick:
                    # 时间到！体力+1
                    new_val = current_stamina + 1
                    self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_val)
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_recover", new_val))
                    
                    # 闹钟往后顺延 300秒 (保持节奏)
                    self._next_stamina_tick += timedelta(seconds=300)
                    
                    # 防御性编程：如果休眠太久(比如关机了)，tick 时间远远落后于 now，就重置为 now+300
                    if self._next_stamina_tick < now:
                         self._next_stamina_tick = now + timedelta(seconds=300)

            # ==========================================
            # 🟡 模块二：任务扫描逻辑
            # ==========================================
            ready_tasks = []
            next_task_time = None 

            for task in self.tasks:
                if not task.enabled:
                    continue 
                
                if now >= task.next_run:
                    ready_tasks.append(task)
                else:
                    # 记录最近的一个未来任务时间
                    if next_task_time is None or task.next_run < next_task_time:
                        next_task_time = task.next_run

            # ==========================================
            # 🔵 模块三：决策与执行
            # ==========================================
            if ready_tasks:
                # A. 有任务：按优先级执行
                ready_tasks.sort(key=lambda t: t.priority)
                winner = ready_tasks[0]
                
                self.data.set_runtime("current_task", winner.name)
                
                try:
                    winner.execute()
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    self.logger.warning(get_text("log_task_interrupted", winner.name))
                    self.data.set_runtime("current_task", "Interrupted")
                    
                    # 🏠 中断后尝试回到首页 (善后处理)
                    # 必须先临时禁用中断回调，否则 ensure_home 里的点击操作也会被立刻中断
                    original_callback = self.device.interrupt_callback
                    self.device.interrupt_callback = None
                    
                    try:
                        self.logger.info(get_text("log_trying_home"))
                        self.device.ensure_home()
                    except Exception as e:
                        self.logger.error(get_text("log_home_fail", e))
                    finally:
                        # 恢复回调 (虽然线程马上要退出了，但保持状态一致性是个好习惯)
                        self.device.interrupt_callback = original_callback

                    # ⏰ 重新安排任务 (推迟 60 秒)
                    # 这样任务会回到队列中，而不是停留在 "运行中" 状态
                    winner.set_next_run(seconds=60)
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_rescheduled", winner.name, 60))

                    # 中断后不需要 continue，直接让 while 循环去判断 self._is_running 即可
                
                # 跑完立即进入下一次循环 (不 sleep)，以便快速响应
                continue
            
            else:
                # B. 没任务：计算休眠时间
                # 我们需要同时考虑 "下个任务的时间" 和 "下个体力的时间"
                # 谁先到，就睡到谁那个时候醒
                
                wait_seconds = 3600 # 默认睡很久
                status_msg = get_text("status_idle")

                # 1. 考虑任务
                if next_task_time:
                    delta_task = (next_task_time - now).total_seconds()
                    wait_seconds = min(wait_seconds, delta_task)
                    status_msg = get_text("status_sleep_task", int(delta_task))

                # 2. 考虑体力 (为了准时加体力，不能睡过头)
                if self._next_stamina_tick:
                    delta_stamina = (self._next_stamina_tick - now).total_seconds()
                    # 至少睡 0.1 秒防止 CPU 空转
                    delta_stamina = max(0.1, delta_stamina)
                    wait_seconds = min(wait_seconds, delta_stamina)
                    # 如果体力马上就要回了，状态栏也可以显示一下
                    if delta_stamina < wait_seconds:
                        status_msg = get_text("status_sleep_stamina", int(delta_stamina))

                # 确保至少睡一点点，防止逻辑死循环跑太快
                wait_seconds = max(0.5, wait_seconds)
                
                self.data.set_runtime("current_task", status_msg)
                
                # 智能休眠
                wake_time = now + timedelta(seconds=wait_seconds)
                wake_time_str = wake_time.strftime("%H:%M:%S")
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_sleep_until", wake_time_str, status_msg))
                self._wake_event.wait(wait_seconds)
        
        # 线程结束时的清理工作
        self.data.set_runtime("current_task", "Stopped")
        self.data.set_runtime("is_running", False)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_scheduler_stopped"))

# tasks/task_registry.py

# 1. 引入你所有的任务类
from tasks.example_task import ExampleTask
from tasks.combat.beast import BeastTask
from tasks.combat.wolf import WolfTask
from tasks.combat.terror import RallyTask
from tasks.daily.intel import IntelTask
from tasks.daily.gather import GatherTask
from tasks.shop.general_shop import ShopTask
from tasks.test_sidebar import TestSidebarTask
from tasks.daily.alliance import AllianceTechTask, AllianceChestTask, AllianceHelpTask
from tasks.daily.mail import MailTask
from tasks.daily.stamina_control import StaminaControlTask
from tasks.daily.warehouse import WarehouseTask
from tasks.daily.recruit_adv import RecruitAdvTask
from tasks.daily.recruit_epic import RecruitEpicTask
from tasks.daily.training import TroopTrainingTask

# 2. 把它们放进一个列表
TASK_CLASSES = [
    # TestSidebarTask, # 测试任务放最前面方便找
    # ExampleTask, # 示例任务可以先注释掉
    StaminaControlTask, 
    BeastTask,
    WolfTask,
    RallyTask,
    IntelTask,
    # GatherTask,
    # ShopTask,
    AllianceTechTask,
    AllianceChestTask,
    AllianceHelpTask,
    MailTask,
    WarehouseTask,
    RecruitAdvTask,
    RecruitEpicTask,
    TroopTrainingTask,
]

def get_all_tasks():
    return TASK_CLASSES

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

class HeroManager:
    """
    英雄状态管理器
    负责管理英雄的拥有状态、出击队伍信息，并与 DataStore 进行同步。
    """

    # 英雄白名单
    # 格式: 内部代号 (小写)
    HERO_LIST = [
        # Gen 4
        "rosa", "alcar", "margot",
        # Gen 3
        "jaeger", "eric", "petra",
        # Gen 2
        "hilde", "zoe", "marlin",
        # Gen 1
        "jabel", "amadeus", "helga", "saul",
        # SR (Epic)
        "amane", "yeonwoo", "fahd", "chenko", "gordon", "diana", "howard", "quinn",
        # R (Rare)
        "olive", "edwin", "seth", "forrest",
    ]

    def __init__(self, data):
        """
        :param data: InstanceDataStore 实例
        """
        self.data = data

    def update_hero_status(self, hero_name, owned=None, state=None):
        """
        更新英雄状态
        :param hero_name: 英雄代号 (必须在 HERO_LIST 中)
        :param owned: bool, 是否拥有 (None 表示不更新此项) -> 存入 config
        :param state: str, 状态 "idle" / "busy" (None 表示不更新此项) -> 存入 stats
        """
        if hero_name not in self.HERO_LIST:
            return

        # 1. 更新拥有状态 (Config)
        if owned is not None:
            self.data.set_cfg(f"heroes.{hero_name}", owned)
        
        # 2. 更新状态信息 (Stats)
        if state is not None:
            self.data.set_stat(f"heroes.{hero_name}", state)

    def get_hero_info(self, hero_name):
        """
        获取单个英雄的完整信息
        :return: dict { "owned": bool, "state": str }
        """
        # 拥有状态从 config 读取
        owned = self.data.get_cfg(f"heroes.{hero_name}", False)
        # 状态信息从 stats 读取
        state = self.data.get_stat(f"heroes.{hero_name}", "idle")
        
        return {"owned": owned, "state": state}

    def get_owned_heroes(self):
        """
        获取所有已拥有的英雄列表
        :return: list [hero_name, ...]
        """
        # 从 config 读取所有英雄配置
        all_heroes_cfg = self.data.get_cfg("heroes", {})
        owned_list = []
        for name, is_owned in all_heroes_cfg.items():
            if is_owned:
                owned_list.append(name)
        return owned_list

    def get_heroes_by_state(self, target_state="idle"):
        """
        获取指定状态的英雄列表
        :param target_state: "idle" or "busy"
        :return: list [hero_name, ...]
        """
        # 从 stats 读取所有英雄状态信息
        all_heroes_stats = self.data.get_stat("heroes", {})
        result = []
        for name, state in all_heroes_stats.items():
            if state == target_state:
                result.append(name)
        return result

    def is_hero_available(self, hero_name):
        """
        检查英雄是否可用 (已拥有且空闲)
        """
        info = self.get_hero_info(hero_name)
        # 拥有且状态为空闲
        return info.get("owned", False) and (info.get("state") == "idle")

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import time
import os
import logging
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class MapSearcher:
    """
    通用地图搜索组件
    负责：进出野外 -> 打开搜索菜单 -> 选图标 -> 调等级 -> 搜索 -> 结果校验(RGB) -> 点击定位
    """
    
    # === 坐标常量 ===
    POS_SLIDER_LEFT = (120, 1053) # 滑动条最左侧 (Lv.1 归零用)
    POS_BTN_MINUS   = (69, 1053)  # 减号 (归零用)
    POS_BTN_PLUS    = (487, 1053) # 加号 (调级用)
    
    # 滑动相关 (在图标区域滑动)
    SWIPE_START     = (636, 898)
    SWIPE_END       = (311, 880)

    # === 图标映射 ===
    ICON_MAP = {
        "beast":  r"assets/images/map_level/beast.png",
        "terror": r"assets/images/map_level/terror.png",
        "bread":  r"assets/images/map_level/bread.png",
        "wood":   r"assets/images/map_level/wood.png",
        "stone":  r"assets/images/map_level/stone.png",
        "iron":   r"assets/images/map_level/iron.png"
    }

    # 定义资源类型集合，用于判断按钮逻辑
    RESOURCE_TYPES = {"bread", "wood", "stone", "iron"}

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{data.instance_id}.MapSearcher")

        # === 核心资源加载 ===
        # 基础导航
        self.IMG_FIND = Template(r"assets/images/home/find.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # [修改点 2] 加上 rgb=True 确保准确识别灰色状态
        self.IMG_GREY_MINUS = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/grey_minus.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        
        # 搜索与确认按钮 (Nuitka 兼容路径)
        self.IMG_BTN_SEARCH = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/btn_search.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # 目标确认按钮 (开启 RGB 校验)
        self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/btn_orange.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_GATHER = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/btn_gather.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_RALLY  = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/btn_rally.png",  threshold=0.9, rgb=True)

    def run(self, target_type, target_level):
        """
        执行完整的搜索流程 (带重试机制)
        :return: bool (True=成功找到并点击, False=彻底失败)
        """
        max_retries = 2
        
        for attempt in range(max_retries):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_map_search_start", target_type, target_level) + f" (Attempt {attempt+1}/{max_retries})")
            
            # 1. 环境检查 (Ensure Wild)
            if not self._step_ensure_wild():
                continue 
                
            # 2. 点击放大镜
            if not self._step_click_find():
                continue 
                
            # 3. 寻找并选择图标
            if not self._step_select_icon(target_type):
                continue 
                
            # 4 & 5. 检查与调整等级
            self._step_adjust_level(target_level)
            
            # 6. 点击搜索按钮
            if not self._step_click_search_btn():
                self.logger.warning("搜索按钮未找到")
                continue

            # 7 & 8. 结果校验与定位点击 (Verify & Click)
            if self._step_validate_and_click_target(target_type):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_map_search_finish"))
                return True 
            
            # 如果校验失败
            self.logger.warning(f"搜索后未找到有效目标 [{target_type}]，准备重试...")
            self.device.sleep(2.0)

        # 循环结束仍未成功
        self.logger.error(f"连续 {max_retries} 次未找到目标 [{target_type} Lv.{target_level}]，建议推迟任务。")
        return False

    def _step_ensure_wild(self):
        """步骤 1: 确保在野外"""
        self.device.ensure_home()
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_ensure_wild_success") + " (Switching)")
            self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_ensure_wild_success"))
        return True

    def _step_click_find(self):
        """步骤 2: 点击放大镜"""
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_FIND):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_find"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_FIND)
            self.device.sleep(0.8)
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_find_icon_missing"))
            return False

    def _step_select_icon(self, target_type):
        """步骤 3: 寻找目标图标 (带滑动)"""
        path = self.ICON_MAP.get(target_type)
        if not path:
            self.logger.error(f"Unknown target type: {target_type}")
            return False
            
        tpl = Template(path, threshold=0.9)
        
        for i in range(3):
            if self.device.exists(tpl):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_target_icon_found", target_type))
                self.device.click(tpl)
                self.device.sleep(0.5)
                return True
            
            if i < 2:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_target_icon_not_found", target_type))
                self.device.swipe(self.SWIPE_START, self.SWIPE_END)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        self.logger.error(get_text("err_target_icon_missing", target_type))
        return False

    def _step_adjust_level(self, target_level):
        """步骤 4 & 5: 检查并调整等级"""
        img_level_path = f"assets/images/map_level/{target_level}.png"
        
        # 检查是否已匹配
        if os.path.exists(img_level_path):
            if self.device.exists(Template(img_level_path, threshold=0.9)):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_search_level_match", target_level))
                return

        # 归零
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_search_level_reset"))
        self.device.click(self.POS_SLIDER_LEFT)
        self.device.sleep(0.2)
        
        for _ in range(10):
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_GREY_MINUS):
                break
            self.device.click(self.POS_BTN_MINUS)
            
        # 递增
        clicks_needed = target_level - 1
        if clicks_needed > 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_search_level_adjust", clicks_needed))
            for _ in range(clicks_needed):
                self.device.adb.touch(self.POS_BTN_PLUS)
                time.sleep(0.1) 
            self.device.sleep(0.5)

    def _step_click_search_btn(self):
        """步骤 6: 点击搜索按钮"""
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_search_btn"))
        if self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_SEARCH):
            self.device.sleep(1.5) # 等待镜头移动
            return True
        return False

    def _step_validate_and_click_target(self, target_type):
        """
        步骤 7: 校验搜索结果并点击
        使用 rgb=True 确保精确匹配
        """
        target_btn = None
        
        # [修改点 1] 逻辑简化：资源类用 Gather，其他用 Orange
        if target_type in self.RESOURCE_TYPES:
            target_btn = self.IMG_BTN_GATHER
        else:
            # Beast 或 Terror
            target_btn = self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE

        # 2. 在屏幕上搜索该按钮
        if not self.device.exists(target_btn):
            self.logger.warning(f"未在屏幕上找到目标操作按钮 ({target_type})")
            return False
            
        # 3. 点击按钮
        self.logger.info(f"定位到目标 [{target_type}]，执行点击...")
        self.device.click(target_btn)
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 4. Terror 的特殊第2步 (点击集结)
        if target_type == "terror":
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_RALLY):
                self.logger.info("点击集结按钮...")
                self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_RALLY)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
                return True
            else:
                self.logger.warning("点击第1步后未发现集结按钮 (btn_rally.png)")
                return False
        
        return True

FILE: game\sidebar.py
