# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import logging
import cv2
import os
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class Sidebar:
    """
    游戏侧边栏交互类
    负责处理侧边栏的展开/收起，以及读取侧边栏上的信息（如行军队列状态）
    """
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{data.instance_id}.Game.Sidebar")
        
        # 初始化空闲队列数量 (从统计数据中读取，默认为 0)
        self.idle_march_count = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0)

        # --- 资源定义 ---
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_SIDEBAR_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/sidebar_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_SIDEBAR_QUEUE_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/sidebar_queue_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # --- 坐标与区域定义 ---
        self.POS_EXPAND_BTN = (4, 550)
        self.AREA_CHECK = (6, 242, 444, 297)
        
        # 侧边栏页面切换按钮
        self.POS_AFFAIRS_TAB = (149, 263)
        self.POS_MARCH_PAGE_BTN = (290, 270)

        # 行军队列检测区域 (x1, y1, x2, y2)
        self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS = [
            (370, 340, 430, 400), # Queue 1
            (370, 415, 430, 475), # Queue 2
            (370, 490, 430, 550), # Queue 3
            (370, 560, 430, 625), # Queue 4
            (370, 637, 430, 696), # Queue 5
            (370, 710, 430, 770)  # Queue 6
        ]

    def expand(self):
        """
        展开侧边栏
        逻辑:
        1. 检查是否有 btn_town.png (判断是否在野外)
           - 是: 点击 (4, 550)
           - 否: 点击 btn_go_map.png -> 等待 1.5s -> 点击 (4, 550)
        2. 检查 sidebar_check.png 是否出现
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_expanding"))
        
        

        for i in range(3):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_attempt", i+1))
            # 0. 确保在主界面
            self.device.ensure_home()

            
            # 1. 场景判断与点击
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_wilderness"))
                self.device.click(self.POS_EXPAND_BTN)
            else:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_switch_map"))
                if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                    self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                    self.device.sleep(1.5)
                    self.device.click(self.POS_EXPAND_BTN)
                else:
                    self.logger.warning(get_text("log_sidebar_no_buttons"))

            # 2. 结果验证
            self.device.sleep(1.0) # 等待动画
            
            # 使用区域截图进行验证
            # AREA_CHECK = (6, 242, 444, 297) -> (x1, y1, x2, y2)
            screen_path = self.device._save_snapshot("sidebar_check")
            if screen_path:
                screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
                if screen is not None:
                    # Crop: y1:y2, x1:x2
                    x1, y1, x2, y2 = self.AREA_CHECK
                    cropped = screen[y1:y2, x1:x2]
                    
                    # 匹配
                    res = self.IMG_SIDEBAR_CHECK.match_in(cropped)
                    if res:
                        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_success"))
                        return True
        
        self.logger.error(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_failed"))
        return False

    def collapse(self):
        """
        收起侧边栏
        逻辑: 点击 btn_town.png -> 等待 1.5s -> 点击 btn_go_map.png
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_collapsing"))
        
        # 1. 点击回城
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN)
        
        # 2. 等待
        self.device.sleep(1.5)
        
        # 3. 点击出城
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
            self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)

    def find_in_anchor_row(self, anchor_tpl, target_tpl, x_range=(373, 434), y_padding=15):
        """
        [修复版] 在锚点同一行寻找目标
        兼容 Airtest 返回 dict 或 tuple 的情况
        """
        # 1. 截取全屏
        screen_path = self.device._save_snapshot("row_search")
        if not screen_path:
            return None
        
        screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
        if screen is None:
            return None

        # 2. 寻找锚点
        anchor_res = anchor_tpl.match_in(screen)
        
        if not anchor_res:
            return None

        try:
            # === [修复核心] 类型检查与边界计算 ===
            y_top = 0
            y_bottom = 0
            
            # 情况 A: 返回字典 (包含 rectangle)
            if isinstance(anchor_res, dict):
                rect = anchor_res.get('rectangle')
                if rect:
                    y_top = int(rect[0][1])     # 左上角 Y
                    y_bottom = int(rect[2][1])  # 右下角 Y
                else:
                    # 字典里没有矩形信息，回退到使用中心点
                    cx, cy = anchor_res['result']
                    h, w = anchor_tpl._imread().shape[:2]
                    y_top = int(cy - h/2)
                    y_bottom = int(cy + h/2)
            
            # 情况 B: 返回元组/列表 (只包含坐标) -> 报错就是因为进了这里
            elif isinstance(anchor_res, (tuple, list)):
                cx, cy = anchor_res
                # 获取模板图片的高度
                h, w = anchor_tpl._imread().shape[:2]
                y_top = int(cy - h/2)
                y_bottom = int(cy + h/2)
            
            else:
                self.logger.error(f"Unknown anchor_res type: {type(anchor_res)}")
                return None

            # 3. 应用外扩 (Padding)
            crop_y_min = max(0, y_top - y_padding)
            crop_y_max = min(screen.shape[0], y_bottom + y_padding)
            
            crop_x_min = max(0, x_range[0])
            crop_x_max = min(screen.shape[1], x_range[1])

            # 4. 裁剪图片
            cropped_img = screen[crop_y_min:crop_y_max, crop_x_min:crop_x_max]
            
            # 5. 在裁剪区寻找目标
            target_res = target_tpl.match_in(cropped_img)
            
            if target_res:
                # 6. 坐标转换
                # 同样要处理 target_res 可能是字典或元组的情况
                if isinstance(target_res, dict):
                    rel_x, rel_y = target_res['result']
                else:
                    rel_x, rel_y = target_res
                
                abs_x = int(crop_x_min + rel_x)
                abs_y = int(crop_y_min + rel_y)
                
                self.logger.info(f"Found target in row. Coords: ({abs_x}, {abs_y})")
                return (abs_x, abs_y)
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(f"Row search error: {e}")
            import traceback
            traceback.print_exc() # 打印堆栈以便调试
            return None

        return None

    def switch_to_affairs_page(self):
        """
        切换到侧边栏的'事务'页面 (左侧标签)
        """
        # 1. 确保侧边栏已展开
        if not self.expand():
            return False
            
        self.logger.info("Switching to Affairs page...")
        
        # 2. 点击事务页签 (149, 263)
        self.device.click(self.POS_AFFAIRS_TAB)
        self.device.sleep(0.5) # 等待切换动画
        return True

    def scroll_up(self):
        """
        向上滑动 (查看下方内容)
        (227, 830) -> (224, 169)
        移动 1秒，悬停 1.5秒
        """
        self.logger.info("Sidebar: Scrolling UP...")
        self.device.drag_and_hold((227, 830), (224, 169), duration=1.8, hold=1.0)
        self.device.sleep(0.8)

    def reset_to_top(self):
        """
        回到顶部 (列表归位)
        (222, 317) -> (201, 1243)
        移动 1秒，悬停 1秒
        """
        self.logger.info("Sidebar: Resetting to TOP...")
        self.device.drag_and_hold((222, 317), (201, 1243), duration=1.0, hold=1.0)
        self.device.sleep(0.8)

    def get_idle_march_queues(self):
        """
        获取空闲行军队列数量
        逻辑:
        1. 展开侧边栏
        2. 点击行军页面按钮
        3. 循环检测 sidebar_queue_check.png
        4. 计算空闲数量并保存
        :return: int 空闲队列数量
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_checking_queues"))

        # 1. 展开侧边栏
        if not self.expand():
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_sidebar_expand_failed"))
            return 0

        # 2. 点击行军页面按钮
        self.device.click(self.POS_MARCH_PAGE_BTN)
        self.device.sleep(0.4) # 等待切换

        # 3. 获取用户设置的总队列数
        total_slots = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.total_march_slots", 5)
        
        # 4. 循环检测
        # 假设: 队列从上到下排列，如果上面的忙碌，下面的才可能是空闲？
        # 或者: 只要有一个空闲，它下面的所有队列也都是空闲的？(通常游戏逻辑如此)
        # 逻辑: 找到第一个"没有 sidebar_queue_check.png" (即空闲) 的位置
        
        first_idle_index = -1
        
        # 截取屏幕一次，提高效率 (假设所有队列都在一屏内)
        screen_path = self.device._save_snapshot("march_check")
        if not screen_path:
            return 0
        screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
        if screen is None:
            return 0

        for i in range(total_slots):
            if i >= len(self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS):
                break
                
            area = self.MARCH_QUEUE_AREAS[i]
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = area
            
            # 裁剪区域
            cropped = screen[y1:y2, x1:x2]
            
            # 检测忙碌图标 (sidebar_queue_check.png)
            # 如果存在 -> 忙碌
            # 如果不存在 -> 空闲
            if not self.IMG_SIDEBAR_QUEUE_CHECK.match_in(cropped):
                first_idle_index = i
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_queue_idle_found", i + 1))
                break
        
        # 5. 计算结果
        if first_idle_index != -1:
            # 如果在索引 i 处发现空闲，说明从 i 开始到底部都是空闲的
            # 例如: 总数 5，索引 2 (第3个) 空闲 -> 空闲数为 5 - 2 = 3 (即 3, 4, 5)
            idle_count = total_slots - first_idle_index
        else:
            # 循环结束都没 break，说明全部忙碌
            idle_count = 0
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_idle_count", idle_count))
        
        # 6. 保存并更新
        self.idle_march_count = idle_count
        self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", idle_count)
        
        return idle_count

==================================================
FILE: game\troop_manager.py
==================================================
import time
import cv2
from airtest.core.api import Template
from game.sidebar import Sidebar
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class TroopType:
    INFANTRY = "infantry" # 对应 barracks
    CAVALRY = "cavalry"   # 对应 stable (骑兵)
    ARCHER = "archer"     # 对应 range (弓兵)

class TrainMode:
    TRAIN = "train"      # 新兵
    PROMOTE = "promote"  # 晋升

class TroopManager:
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = self.device.logger
        self.sidebar = Sidebar(device, data)

        # ==========================================
        # 1. 侧边栏锚点与状态资源 (Sidebar Assets)
        # ==========================================
        self.anchors = {
            TroopType.INFANTRY: Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/inf.png", threshold=0.9),
            TroopType.CAVALRY:  Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/cav.png", threshold=0.9),
            TroopType.ARCHER:   Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/arc.png", threshold=0.9)
        }
        
        # 状态信号
        # go.png: 空闲 (需开启 RGB 区分灰色状态)
        self.IMG_STATUS_GO = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/go.png", threshold=0.94, rgb=True)
        # troop_done.png: 训练完成 (待领取)
        self.IMG_STATUS_DONE = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/troop_done.png", threshold=0.9)

        # ==========================================
        # 2. 入口与导航资源 (Entry)
        # ==========================================
        self.IMG_BTN_TRAIN_ENTRY = Template(r"assets/images/troop/train.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # ==========================================
        # 3. 内部状态验证资源 (Inside State)
        # ==========================================
        self.IMG_STATE_PLUS = Template(r"assets/images/troop/plus.png", threshold=0.9) 
        self.IMG_STATE_STOP = Template(r"assets/images/troop/stop.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # ==========================================
        # 4. 功能按钮 (Buttons)
        # ==========================================
        # 蓝色训练按钮 (通常用于训练新兵)
        self.IMG_BTN_BLUE_TRAIN = Template(r"assets/images/troop/btn_blue_train.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        # 蓝色晋升按钮 (通常用于晋升确认)
        self.IMG_BTN_BLUE_PROMO = Template(r"assets/images/troop/btn_blue_promo.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        # 黄色按钮 (可能用于加速或金币训练?)
        self.IMG_BTN_YELLOW = Template(r"assets/images/troop/btn_yellow.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        # 晋升切换图标 (向上箭头)
        self.IMG_ICON_PROMOTE = Template(r"assets/images/troop/promote.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)

        # ==========================================
        # 5. 页签状态图标 (Tabs)
        # ==========================================
        # 激活状态 (用来判断当前在哪)
        self.tabs_on = {
            TroopType.INFANTRY: Template(r"assets/images/troop/barracks_on.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True),
            TroopType.CAVALRY:  Template(r"assets/images/troop/stable_on.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True),
            TroopType.ARCHER:   Template(r"assets/images/troop/range_on.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        }
        
        # 未激活状态 (用来点击切换)
        self.tabs_off = {
            TroopType.INFANTRY: Template(r"assets/images/troop/barracks_off.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True),
            TroopType.CAVALRY:  Template(r"assets/images/troop/stable_off.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True),
            TroopType.ARCHER:   Template(r"assets/images/troop/range_off.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        }

        # ==========================================
        # 6. 等级图标 (1-10) & 常量
        # ==========================================
        self.tier_icons = {}
        for i in range(1, 11): 
            self.tier_icons[i] = Template(f"assets/images/troop/{i}.png", threshold=0.885)

        # 滑动坐标 (根据需求定点滑动)
        self.POS_SCROLL_LEFT = (10, 700)
        self.POS_SCROLL_RIGHT = (706, 710)

    # ==========================================
    # [功能] 兵营状态扫描 (高效)
    # ==========================================
    def scan_building_states(self):
        """
        一次截图，批量判断三个兵营的状态。
        返回: dict { 'infantry': True/False/None, ... }
        - True: Idle (Go or Done) -> 可操作
        - False: Busy (Nothing found) -> 忙碌中
        - None: Unknown (Anchor not found) -> 未解锁或需滑动
        """
        self.logger.info("Scanning troop building states...")
        results = {}
        
        # 1. 确保在事务页
        if not self.sidebar.switch_to_affairs_page():
            self.logger.error("Failed to open sidebar for scanning.")
            return {}

        # 2. 截取一次全屏快照
        screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
        if not screen_path:
            return {}
        
        screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
        if screen is None:
            return {}

        # 3. 遍历三个兵种
        # 搜索区域 X 范围 (参考 sidebar.py 的 find_in_anchor_row)
        CROP_X_MIN, CROP_X_MAX = 373, 434
        Y_PADDING = 15

        for t_type, anchor_tpl in self.anchors.items():
            # A. 在快照中寻找锚点
            anchor_res = anchor_tpl.match_in(screen)
            
            if not anchor_res:
                results[t_type] = None # Unknown
                continue

            try:
                # B. 计算裁剪区域 (兼容 dict 或 tuple 返回值)
                if isinstance(anchor_res, dict):
                    rect = anchor_res.get('rectangle')
                    if rect:
                        y_top = int(rect[0][1])
                        y_bottom = int(rect[2][1])
                    else:
                        cx, cy = anchor_res['result']
                        h, w = anchor_tpl._imread().shape[:2]
                        y_top = int(cy - h/2)
                        y_bottom = int(cy + h/2)
                else:
                    # tuple
                    cx, cy = anchor_res
                    h, w = anchor_tpl._imread().shape[:2]
                    y_top = int(cy - h/2)
                    y_bottom = int(cy + h/2)

                # 应用边界检查
                y_min = max(0, y_top - Y_PADDING)
                y_max = min(screen.shape[0], y_bottom + Y_PADDING)
                
                # C. 裁剪出该行右侧区域
                cropped_img = screen[y_min:y_max, CROP_X_MIN:CROP_X_MAX]
                
                # D. 在小图里搜索状态信号
                is_idle = False
                
                # 检查1: 是否有 Done (训练完成)
                if self.IMG_STATUS_DONE.match_in(cropped_img):
                    is_idle = True
                
                # 检查2: 是否有 Go (空闲)
                elif self.IMG_STATUS_GO.match_in(cropped_img):
                    is_idle = True
                
                # E. 记录结果
                # True = Idle (可练), False = Busy (倒计时)
                results[t_type] = is_idle
                
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.error(f"Error scanning {t_type}: {e}")
                results[t_type] = None

        self.logger.info(f"Scan results: {results}")
        return results

    # ==========================================
    # 核心动作 (Atomic Actions)
    # ==========================================

    def enter_barracks(self):
        """[动作] 进入兵营"""
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_enter_start"))

        if not self.sidebar.switch_to_affairs_page():
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_sidebar_fail"))
            return False

        # 使用 self.anchors 中的步兵图标
        pos_inf = self.device.exists(self.anchors[TroopType.INFANTRY])
        if not pos_inf:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_icon_missing"))
            return False
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_click_inf"))
        self.device.click(pos_inf)
        self.device.sleep(1.5)

        target_pos = (352, 580)
        for _ in range(3):
            self.device.click(target_pos)
            time.sleep(0.4)
            
        pos_train = self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TRAIN_ENTRY)
        if not pos_train:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_bubble_missing"))
            return False
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_click_train"))
        self.device.click(pos_train)
        self.device.sleep(1.0) 

        # 验证进入
        if self.check_state() != "unknown":
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_enter_success"))
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_enter_valid_fail"))
            return False

    def check_state(self):
        """[状态检测]"""
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_STATE_PLUS):
            return "idle"
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_STATE_STOP):
            return "busy"
        return "unknown"

    def get_current_tab(self):
        """获取当前所在的兵营页面类型"""
        for t_type, tpl in self.tabs_on.items():
            if self.device.exists(tpl):
                return t_type
        return None

    def switch_to_tab(self, target_type):
        """[动作] 切换到指定兵营页签"""
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_switch_tab", target_type))
        current = self.get_current_tab()
        
        # 1. 已经在目标页签
        if current == target_type:
            return True
            
        # 2. 无页签显示 (小号模式)
        if current is None:
            if target_type == TroopType.INFANTRY:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_no_tab_default"))
                return True
            else:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("warn_troop_tab_locked", target_type))
                return False

        # 3. 有页签，执行切换
        target_off_tpl = self.tabs_off.get(target_type)
        if not target_off_tpl:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_asset_missing", target_type))
            return False
            
        if self.device.click(target_off_tpl):
            self.device.sleep(1.0) # 等待切换
            
            # 验证
            if self.device.exists(self.tabs_on[target_type]):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_switch_success", target_type))
                return True
            else:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("warn_troop_switch_fail", target_type))
                return False
        
        self.logger.warning(get_text("warn_troop_tab_not_found", target_type))
        return False

    def select_tier(self, level):
        """
        [动作] 选择兵种等级
        逻辑: 尝试直接找 -> 根据范围滑动 -> 再次找
        """
        target_tpl = self.tier_icons.get(level)
        if not target_tpl:
            self.logger.error(f"Asset missing for Tier {level}")
            return False

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_select_tier", level))

        # 1. 第一次寻找
        if self.device.exists(target_tpl):
            self.logger.info(f"Tier {level} found directly.")
            self.device.click(target_tpl)
            self.device.sleep(0.5)
            return True
        
        # 2. 没找到，执行定点滑动
        if 1 <= level <= 5:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_scroll_low")) 
            self.device.drag_and_hold(self.POS_SCROLL_LEFT, self.POS_SCROLL_RIGHT, duration=1.0, hold=1.0)
        
        elif 6 <= level <= 10:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_scroll_high")) 
            self.device.drag_and_hold(self.POS_SCROLL_RIGHT, self.POS_SCROLL_LEFT, duration=1.0, hold=1.0)
        
        else:
            self.logger.warning(f"Level {level} out of range (1-10).")
            return False

        # 3. 滑动后再次寻找
        self.device.sleep(0.8)
        if self.device.exists(target_tpl):
            self.logger.info(f"Tier {level} found after scrolling.")
            self.device.click(target_tpl)
            self.device.sleep(0.8)
            return True
        
        self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_tier_not_found", level))
        return False

    def execute_train_action(self):
        """[动作] 执行训练新兵"""
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_action_train"))
        
        train_btn = self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE_TRAIN)
        if train_btn:
            self.device.click(train_btn)
            self.device.sleep(1.0) # 改为1s
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_train_btn_missing"))
            return False

        # 验证结果 (检查 Stop 图标)
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_STATE_STOP):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_train_success"))
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("err_troop_train_verify_fail"))
            return False

    def execute_promote_action(self):
        """
        [动作] 执行晋升流程
        返回: True (成功) / False (失败) / "NO_PROMOTABLE" (无兵可升)
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_action_promote"))
        
        # 1. 检查箭头
        arrow_pos = self.device.exists(self.IMG_ICON_PROMOTE)
        if not arrow_pos:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_no_promotable"))
            return "NO_PROMOTABLE"
            
        # 2. 点击箭头
        self.device.click(arrow_pos)
        self.device.sleep(0.8) 
            
        # 3. 点击晋升确认
        promo_btn = self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE_PROMO)
        if promo_btn:
            self.device.click(promo_btn)
            self.device.sleep(1.0) # 改为1s
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_troop_promote_btn_missing"))
            return False

        # 4. 验证 (检查 Stop)
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_STATE_STOP):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_troop_promote_success"))
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("err_troop_promote_verify_fail"))
            return False

    def exit_barracks(self):
        """[动作] 退出界面"""
        self.device.keyevent("BACK")
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

FILE: i18n\i18n.py

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import os
import sys
import yaml
import logging
# from config import global_config  <-- 移除顶层导入

logger = logging.getLogger("i18n")

# ==========================================
# 📖 翻译缓存 (Translation Cache)
# ==========================================
# 结构: { "zh_CN": {key: value}, "en_US": {key: value} }
_TRANSLATION_CACHE = {}

def load_language(lang_code):
    """
    加载指定语言的 YAML 文件
    :param lang_code: e.g. "zh", "en"
    """
    # 映射: zh_CN -> zh, en_US -> en
    short_lang = "zh" if "zh" in lang_code else "en"
    
    if short_lang in _TRANSLATION_CACHE:
        return _TRANSLATION_CACHE[short_lang]

    # 获取当前文件所在目录
    if getattr(sys, 'frozen', False):
        # 打包后，资源文件位于 sys._MEIPASS/i18n 或 exe 同级目录
        if hasattr(sys, '_MEIPASS'):
            base_dir = os.path.join(sys._MEIPASS, 'i18n')
        else:
            base_dir = os.path.join(os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(sys.argv[0])), 'i18n')
    else:
        base_dir = os.path.dirname(os.path.abspath(__file__))
        
    file_path = os.path.join(base_dir, f"{short_lang}.yaml")
    
    data = {}
    if os.path.exists(file_path):
        try:
            with open(file_path, 'r', encoding='utf-8') as f:
                data = yaml.safe_load(f) or {}
            logger.info(f"Loaded language pack: {short_lang}.yaml")
        except Exception as e:
            logger.error(f"Failed to load language pack {file_path}: {e}")
    else:
        logger.warning(f"Language pack not found: {file_path}")
        
    _TRANSLATION_CACHE[short_lang] = data
    return data

# 预加载默认语言
load_language("zh")
load_language("en")

# ==========================================
# 🔧 工具函数 (Helper Functions)
# ==========================================

def get_text(key, *args):
    """
    获取翻译文本
    :param key: 字典中的键
    :param args: 如果文本中有 {} 占位符，用这些参数填充
    Usage: get_text("msg_del_warn", "InstanceA")
    """
    # 1. 从全局配置读取语言 (延迟导入以避免循环引用)
    try:
        from config import global_config
        current_lang_code = global_config.get("language", "zh_CN")
    except ImportError:
        current_lang_code = "zh_CN"

    short_lang = "zh" if "zh" in current_lang_code else "en"
    
    # 2. 获取对应语言的字典
    lang_data = _TRANSLATION_CACHE.get(short_lang)
    if not lang_data:
        # 尝试重新加载
        lang_data = load_language(short_lang)
    
    # 3. 查找 Key
    text = lang_data.get(key)
    
    # 4. 如果没找到，尝试回退到中文 (Fallback)
    if text is None and short_lang != "zh":
        fallback_data = _TRANSLATION_CACHE.get("zh")
        if fallback_data:
            text = fallback_data.get(key)
            
    # 5. 还是没找到，直接返回 Key
    if text is None:
        return key
    
    # 6. 格式化参数
    if args:
        try:
            # 确保 text 是字符串
            return str(text).format(*args)
        except Exception as e:
            logger.warning(f"Failed to format translation text [{key}]: {e}")
            return str(text)
            
    return str(text)

# tasks/base_task.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

import os
import logging
import time
from airtest.core.api import Template
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from game.sidebar import Sidebar

class BaseTask:
    """
    所有任务的基类 (ALAS 风格)
    """
    
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        self.device = device
        self.data = data
        self.logger = logging.getLogger(f"{self.data.instance_id}.Task.{self.name}")
        self.sidebar = Sidebar(device, data)
        self._manually_scheduled = False

    # ==========================================
    # ⚙️ 属性配置
    # ==========================================
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "BaseTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 50

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 3600

    # ==========================================
    # ⏱️ 调度逻辑
    # ==========================================
    @property
    def enabled(self):
        return self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.{self.name.lower()}.enable", False)

    @property
    def next_run(self):
        time_str = self.data.get_stat(f"tasks.{self.name}.next_run", "1970-01-01 00:00:00")
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(time_str, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        except ValueError:
            return datetime.min 

    def set_next_run(self, seconds=None, timestamp=None):
        if timestamp:
            future_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(timestamp)
        else:
            if seconds is None:
                seconds = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.{self.name.lower()}.interval", self.default_interval)
            future_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(seconds=int(seconds))
            
        time_str = future_time.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        
        self.data.set_stat(f"tasks.{self.name}.next_run", time_str)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_next_run_scheduled", time_str))
        self._manually_scheduled = True

    # ==========================================
    # 🏃 运行入口
    # ==========================================
    def execute(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_started", self.name))
        self.data.set_runtime("current_task", self.name)
        self._manually_scheduled = False
        
        try:
            success = self.run_impl()
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_task_crashed", e))
            success = False
        
        if success:
            # 任务成功：
            # 如果子任务没有自己安排时间，且任务仍处于启用状态，则使用默认间隔安排下次运行
            if self.enabled and not self._manually_scheduled:
                self.set_next_run()
            elif not self.enabled:
                self.logger.info(f"任务 {self.name} 已禁用，停止调度")
        else:
            # 任务失败：
            # 如果子任务已经安排了时间（比如体力不足推迟20分钟），则尊重子任务的安排
            # 只有当子任务啥也没做直接 return False 时，才执行默认的 5分钟 重试
            if not self._manually_scheduled:
                self.logger.warning(f"任务 {self.name} 失败且未指定推迟时间，默认 300秒 后重试")
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_finished", self.name))

    def run_impl(self):
        raise NotImplementedError

    # ==========================================
    # 🎮 通用游戏操作 (核心逻辑)
    # ==========================================
    """
    def select_search_level(self, target_level):
        '''
        通用的等级调整逻辑 (侦察 -> 归零 -> 调整 -> 核实)
        :param target_level: int, 目标等级 (1-29)
        '''
        # ==========================================
        # 🛑 请务必修改这些坐标！(720x1280分辨率下)
        # ==========================================
        SLIDER_LEFT = (120, 1053) # 滑动条最左端 (能点到1级的位置)
        BTN_MINUS = (69, 1053)    # 减号按钮中心
        BTN_PLUS = (487, 1053)    # 加号按钮中心
        BTN_SEARCH = (360, 1214) # 底部巨大的搜索按钮
        
        # 图片路径模板
        IMG_LEVEL_TPL = r"assets/images/map_level/{}.png"
        IMG_MINUS_GRAY = Template(r"assets/images/map_level/grey_minus.png", threshold=0.95)

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_adjust_level_start", target_level))

        # --- 1. 侦察阶段 (Pre-Check) ---
        # 如果当前屏幕上已经显示了目标等级，直接去搜索，省时省力
        target_img_path = IMG_LEVEL_TPL.format(target_level)
        
        # 检查图片是否存在 (利用 basic.py 的 _exists_internal)
        if os.path.exists(target_img_path):
            target_tpl = Template(target_img_path, threshold=0.9)
            if self.device.exists(target_tpl):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_already_match", target_level))
                self.device.click(BTN_SEARCH)
                return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_level_img_missing", target_img_path))

        # --- 2. 归零阶段 (Reset to 1) ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_resetting"))
        
        # A. 猛击滑动条左侧 (快速降级)
        self.device.click(SLIDER_LEFT)
        self.device.sleep(0.5) # 等动画

        # B. 灰色检测与补刀 (确保降到 1)
        is_reset_success = False
        for _ in range(15): # 最多补点15次，防死循环
            if self.device.exists(IMG_MINUS_GRAY):
                # self.logger.debug("减号已变灰，归零成功")
                is_reset_success = True
                break
            else:
                # 还没灰，说明还没到1级，点减号补刀
                self.device.click(BTN_MINUS, times=1)
                # time.sleep(0.1) # click 内部已有 sleep，这里不用加太久

        if not is_reset_success:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_reset_warning"))

        # --- 3. 调整阶段 (Adjust) ---
        # 现在默认是 Lv.1
        clicks_needed = target_level - 1
        
        if clicks_needed > 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_upgrading", clicks_needed))
            # 为了速度，我们可以直接调用 adb 连点，绕过 device.click 的 sleep
            for _ in range(clicks_needed):
                self.device.adb.touch(BTN_PLUS)
                time.sleep(0.1) # 80ms 间隔，极速连点
            self.device.sleep(0.8) # 等数字跳完

        # --- 4. 核实阶段 (Verify) ---
        # 再看一眼是不是我们要的等级
        if os.path.exists(target_img_path):
            if not self.device.exists(target_tpl):
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_level_adjust_failed", target_level))
                # 失败策略：是重试还是硬着头皮搜？
                # 这里选择硬着头皮搜，万一只是图片识别不到呢
            else:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_level_verify_success", target_level))

        # --- 5. 搜索 ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_search"))
        self.device.click(BTN_SEARCH)
        self.device.sleep(1.5) # 等待界面切换
        return True
    """

    # ==========================================
    # 📊 辅助功能
    # ==========================================

class TestSidebarTask(BaseTask):
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "TestSidebarTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 100  # 高优先级，方便测试

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_test_sidebar_start"))
        
        # 1. 测试展开/收起 (get_idle_march_queues 内部会调用 expand)
        # self.sidebar.collapse() # 先收起一次，确保状态
        # time.sleep(1)

        # 2. 测试获取空闲队列
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_checking_idle_queues"))
        idle_count = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_idle_queues_result", idle_count))
        
        # 3. 验证数据持久化
        saved_count = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", -1)
        if saved_count == idle_count:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_persistence_success", saved_count))
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_persistence_failed", idle_count, saved_count))

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_test_sidebar_end"))
        return True

# tasks/combat/beast.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from game.map_searcher import MapSearcher
from i18n.i18n import get_text
import math
from airtest.core.api import Template

class BeastTask(CombatTask):
    # =========================================================================
    # 1. UI 配置定义
    # =========================================================================
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable", 
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Combat"
        },
        {
            "key": "target_level",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_target_level", # 目标等级
            "min": 1,
            "max": 30,
            "default": 1
        },
        {
            "key": "remaining_attacks",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_remaining_attacks", # 剩余次数
            "min": 0,
            "max": 100,
            "default": 10
        },
        {
            "key": "concurrent_queues",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_concurrent_queues", # 并发队列设置
            "min": 1,
            "max": 6,
            "default": 1
        },
        {
            "key": "hero_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_hero_mode", # 英雄模式
            "options": [
                {"value": "any", "label": "opt_hero_any"}, # 任意
                {"value": "diana", "label": "opt_hero_diana"}, # 仅戴安娜
                {"value": "diana_fahd", "label": "opt_hero_fahd_diana"} # 戴安娜+法赫德
            ],
            "default": "any"
        },
        {
            "key": "soldier_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_soldier_mode", # 兵力设置
            "options": [
                {"value": "full", "label": "opt_soldier_full"}, # 满兵
                {"value": "equal", "label": "opt_soldier_equal"} # 均兵
            ],
            "default": "full"
        }
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "BeastTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 40

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 180 # 默认回城等待时间

    def run_impl(self):
        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 1: 读取配置 (Load Config)
        # =====================================================================
        target_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.beasttask.target_level", 1)
        remaining = self.data.get_cfg("modules.beasttask.remaining_attacks", 0)
        user_queue_limit = self.data.get_cfg("modules.beasttask.concurrent_queues", 1)
        hero_mode = self.data.get_cfg("modules.beasttask.hero_mode", "any")
        soldier_mode = self.data.get_cfg("modules.beasttask.soldier_mode", "full")

        # 检查剩余次数
        if remaining <= 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_finished_zero_rem", self.name))
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.beasttask.enable", False)
            return False

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_beast_start_round", remaining, target_level))

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 2: 前置环境与资源检查 (Pre-Check Logic)
        # =====================================================================
        
        # 2.1 确保在主页
        self.device.ensure_home()

        # 2.2 获取游戏内实际空闲队列
        game_idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        
        # 2.3 OCR 更新体力
        if not self.update_stamina():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_read_fail_skip"))
            return False
        
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 3: 计算本轮执行批次 (Calc Batch Size)
        # =====================================================================
        
        # A. 队列限制
        adjusted_queue_limit = min(user_queue_limit, game_idle_queues)
        
        # B. 英雄限制 (强制单队逻辑)
        if hero_mode in ["diana", "diana_fahd"]:
            adjusted_queue_limit = 1
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_mode_limit_queue"))

        # C. 体力限制
        diana_skill_lv = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        
        # 获取单次打野消耗
        unit_cost = 10
        if hero_mode in ["diana", "diana_fahd"] and diana_skill_lv > 0:
            unit_cost = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_skill_lv, (25, 10))[1] # [1]是野怪体力
        
        available_stamina = self.get_disposable_stamina(current_stamina)
        possible_by_stamina = available_stamina // unit_cost

        # --- 最终计算 Batch Size ---
        batch_size = min(adjusted_queue_limit, possible_by_stamina, remaining)

        # 详细日志 (Batch Calc)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_calc_detail", 
                                  user_queue_limit, game_idle_queues, current_stamina, remaining, batch_size))

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 4: 批次有效性检查与等待逻辑 (Wait Logic)
        # =====================================================================
        if batch_size <= 0:
            # 分析原因
            if adjusted_queue_limit == 0:
                # 原因：没队列
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_no_idle_queues_wait"))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=180) # 等3分钟
                return False
            
            if possible_by_stamina == 0:
                # 原因：没体力
                missing_stamina = unit_cost - current_stamina
                wait_seconds = missing_stamina * 300 # 5分钟回复1点
                wait_minutes = math.ceil(wait_seconds / 60)
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_wait", current_stamina, unit_cost, wait_minutes))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
                return False
                
            return False # 其他未知原因

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 5: 内部循环执行 (Execution Loop)
        # =====================================================================
        searcher = MapSearcher(self.device, self.data)
        success_count = 0
        
        # 出击按钮 (蓝色)
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_start", batch_size))

        for i in range(batch_size):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_processing_batch", i+1, batch_size))
            
            # 5.1 地图搜索
            if not searcher.run("beast", target_level):
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_map_search_failed"))
                break
            
            # 5.2 确保在战斗界面
            try:
                self.verify_combat_page()
            except Exception:
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_combat_page_failed"))
                break

            # 5.3 选将逻辑
            target_heroes = []
            if hero_mode == "diana":
                target_heroes = ["diana"]
            elif hero_mode == "diana_fahd":
                target_heroes = ["diana", "fahd"]
            
            try:
                self.select_heroes(target_heroes)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("err_hero_busy_skip", e))
                break

            # 5.4 兵力设置 (Soldier Check)
            if soldier_mode == "equal":
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_clicking_equal"))
                self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL)
                self.device.sleep(0.5)
            else:
                # 满兵模式，通常无需操作
                pass

            real_time_cost = self.get_realtime_cost(mode="wild")
            
            # 双重检查：如果虽然批次计算时以为够，但实际不够（极低概率），防止卡死
            if current_stamina < real_time_cost:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_insufficient_final", current_stamina, real_time_cost, 0))
                break

            # 5.5 出击 (Dispatch)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_dispatching"))
            self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            if self.verify_attack_success():
                success_count += 1
                
                # 5.6 内存数据更新 (Update Data)
                remaining -= 1
                self.data.set_cfg("modules.beasttask.remaining_attacks", remaining)
                
                # 更新本地体力记录
                cost = unit_cost 
                current_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - real_time_cost)
                self.data.set_stat("stamina", current_stamina)
                
                # 更新空闲队列
                game_idle_queues -= 1
                
            else:
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_attack_verify_fail"))
                break
                
            # 循环继续...

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 6: 善后处理 (Post Loop)
        # =====================================================================
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_finished", success_count))
        
        # 6.1 保存数据
        self.data.save_config() # 保存剩余次数
        self.data.save_stats()  # 保存体力

        if remaining <= 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_done_disable", self.name))
            # 禁用任务
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.beasttask.enable", False)
            # 再次保存配置以确保持久化 "enable: false"
            self.data.save_config()
            # 直接返回，不再安排下次运行
            return True
        
        # 6.2 设置下次运行时间 (回城等待)
        # 默认 3分钟 (180s)
        wait_time = 210 + (success_count * 2) 
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_waiting_return", wait_time))
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_time)
        
        # 6.3 确保回到主页
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        return True

==================================================
FILE: tasks\combat\combat_task.py

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from basic import TaskInterruptedError
import cv2
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
import math

class CombatTask(BaseTask):
    """
    战斗任务基类
    处理通用的编队、选将、调兵逻辑
    """

    # 戴安娜减耗表 (技能等级: (集结体力, 打野体力))
    DIANA_COST_TABLE = {
        1: (23, 9),
        2: (22, 9),
        3: (22, 9),
        4: (21, 9),
        5: (20, 8)
    }
    DEFAULT_COST = (25, 10)

    # 战斗配置页面按钮
    IMG_CLEAR_FULL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/clear_full.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_EQUAL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/equal.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_ADD = Template(r"assets/images/combat/add.png", threshold=0.9)

    # Stamina Update Templates
    IMG_INTEL = Template(r"assets/images/home/intel.png", threshold=0.9)
    IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        self.target_heroes = []
        self.soldier_count = 0
        
        # Number templates for stamina update
        self.number_templates = {}
        for i in range(10):
            self.number_templates[i] = Template(f"assets/images/number/{i}.png", threshold=0.9)

    def get_disposable_stamina(self, current_stamina):
        """
        计算可用于普通战斗的'闲钱'体力。
        逻辑：在 Intel 任务刷新前 30 分钟内，强制锁住 75 点体力。
        """
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        
        # 1. 定义锁定窗口 (UTC)
        # Intel 刷新时间: 00:00, 08:00, 16:00
        # 锁定时间段: 23:30-00:00, 07:30-08:00, 15:30-16:00
        
        current_hour = now.hour
        current_minute = now.minute
        
        is_locked = False
        
        # 检查是否处于刷新前30分钟
        if (current_hour == 7 and current_minute >= 30) or \
           (current_hour == 15 and current_minute >= 30) or \
           (current_hour == 23 and current_minute >= 30):
            is_locked = True
            
        if is_locked:
            # 处于锁定窗口，必须保留 75 点
            reserved = 75
            disposable = max(0, current_stamina - reserved)
            
            if disposable < current_stamina:
                # 只有当体力确实被扣减了才打印日志，防止刷屏
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_locked_for_intel", reserved, disposable))
            
            return disposable
        
        # 平时：所有体力都可用
        return current_stamina

    

    def verify_combat_page(self):
        """
        确保在战斗配置页面
        """
        img_formation = Template(r"assets/images/combat/formation1.png", threshold=0.9)
        img_lock = Template(r"assets/images/combat/lock.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        if not (self.device.exists(img_formation) or self.device.exists(img_lock)):
            raise Exception(get_text("err_not_in_combat_page"))
        return True

    def calculate_cost(self, mode="rally"):
        skill_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        if skill_level == 0:
            return self.DEFAULT_COST[0] if mode == "rally" else self.DEFAULT_COST[1]

        if self.find_diana():
            costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(skill_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)
            cost = costs[0] if mode == "rally" else costs[1]
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_diana_cost", skill_level, cost))
            return cost
        else:
            cost = self.DEFAULT_COST[0] if mode == "rally" else self.DEFAULT_COST[1]
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_normal_cost", cost))
            return cost

    def find_diana(self):
        # 确保路径正确
        img_diana = Template(r"assets/images/heroes/diana_big.png", threshold=0.9)
        return self.device.exists(img_diana)
    
    def get_realtime_cost(self, mode="wild"):
        """
        [新增] 实时计算当前屏幕上的体力消耗
        :param mode: "wild" (野怪) 或 "rally" (集结)
        :return: int (消耗值)
        """
        diana_skill_lv = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        
        # 默认消耗
        base_cost = 25 if mode == "rally" else 10
        
        # 如果戴安娜技能为0，无论她在不在场，消耗都不变
        if diana_skill_lv == 0:
            return base_cost

        # 检测屏幕上是否有戴安娜
        if self.find_diana():
            # 查表获取减免后的消耗
            costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_skill_lv, (25, 10))
            real_cost = costs[0] if mode == "rally" else costs[1]
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_diana_detected_cost", real_cost))
            return real_cost
        
        return base_cost

    def select_heroes(self, heroes: list):
        """
        [修复版] 英雄选择逻辑
        修复问题：找到头像后未点击确认按钮
        逻辑：
        1. 尝试清空并打开列表。
        2. 遍历英雄列表。
        3. 尝试在当前屏、回顶后、或滑动中寻找。
        4. 【关键】只要找到了，先点头像，立刻点确认按钮。
        5. 没找到则跳过。
        6. 最后智能退出。
        """
        if not heroes:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_no_hero_specified"))
            return

        # ==========================================
        # 0. 初始化：清空并打开列表
        # ==========================================
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_ADD):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_clearing_heroes"))
            self.device.click((237, 260)) # 点击清除
            self.device.sleep(2.0)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_first_hero_slot"))
        self.device.click((174, 374)) # 打开列表
        self.device.sleep(1.5)

        # ==========================================
        # 1. 遍历目标列表
        # ==========================================
        found_count = 0
        
        for i, hero_name in enumerate(heroes):
            self.logger.info(f"Targeting hero: {hero_name} ({i+1}/{len(heroes)})")
            
            img_hero_small = Template(f"assets/images/heroes/{hero_name}_small.png", threshold=0.85)
            is_found_and_clicked = False # 标记本轮是否成功点到了头像
            
            # --- 步骤 A: 当前屏幕直接寻找 ---
            if self.device.exists(img_hero_small):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_found", hero_name))
                self.device.click(img_hero_small)
                self.device.sleep(0.5)
                is_found_and_clicked = True
            
            # --- 步骤 B: 回顶重置 (如果步骤A没找到，且是第2个英雄) ---
            elif i > 0:
                self.logger.info("Reseting list to top...")
                for _ in range(3):
                    self.device.swipe((351, 600), (351, 1213), duration=0.5)
                    self.device.sleep(0.2)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
                
                if self.device.exists(img_hero_small):
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_found", hero_name))
                    self.device.click(img_hero_small)
                    self.device.sleep(0.5)
                    is_found_and_clicked = True

            # --- 步骤 C: 下滑寻找 (如果前两步都没找到) ---
            if not is_found_and_clicked:
                # self.logger.info(f"Scrolling to find {hero_name}...")
                for attempt in range(3):
                    self.device.drag_and_hold((349, 854), (357, 564), duration=1.0, hold=1.0)
                    
                    if self.device.exists(img_hero_small):
                        self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_found", hero_name))
                        self.device.click(img_hero_small)
                        self.device.sleep(0.5)
                        is_found_and_clicked = True
                        break

            # --- [核心修复] 步骤 D: 确认选择 ---
            if is_found_and_clicked:
                self.logger.info("Clicking Confirm/Assign button...")
                self.device.click((440, 1000)) # 点击确认
                self.device.sleep(1.0)         # 等待窗口反应
                found_count += 1
            else:
                self.logger.warning(f"Hero {hero_name} unavailable. Skipping.")

        # ==========================================
        # 2. 结束：智能退出
        # ==========================================
        self.logger.info(f"Hero selection complete. Picked {found_count}/{len(heroes)}.")
        
        # 检查是否还在列表里 (如果没有均兵按钮，说明还在列表，需要补 BACK)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_EQUAL):
            self.logger.info("Still in list view, pressing BACK.")
            self.device.keyevent("BACK")
            self.device.sleep(0.5)

    def set_one_soldier(self, enable=True):
        """
        设置是否以1兵出击
        :param enable: bool, True=1兵, False=平均分配
        """
        # 1兵选项坐标 (这个还是保留坐标，因为可能是弹窗里的选项)
        BTN_ONE_SOLDIER = (658, 690)
        
        if enable:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_set_one_soldier"))
            # 点击清除/满兵按钮 (打开设置菜单)
            self.device.click(self.IMG_CLEAR_FULL)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)
            # 点击1兵选项
            self.device.click(BTN_ONE_SOLDIER)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)
        else:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_set_avg_soldier"))
            # 点击平均分配按钮
            self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL)
            self.device.sleep(0.2)

    def verify_attack_success(self):
        """
        验证出击是否成功
        判断屏幕上是否同时有：home_check.png 和 btn_town.png
        :return: bool
        """
        # img_home_check = Template(r"assets/images/home/home_check.png", threshold=0.9)
        img_home_check = self.device.IMG_HOME_CHECK
        img_btn_town = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # 等待一小段时间让界面切换
        self.device.sleep(1)
        
        if self.device.exists(img_home_check) and self.device.exists(img_btn_town):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_attack_success"))
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_attack_failed"))
            return False

    def check_stamina_sufficient(self, cost):
        """
        检查体力是否足够 (基于缓存 + Double Check)
        
        :param cost: int, 需要消耗的体力值
        :return:
            - True: 体力充足，可以直接继续
            - "RESTART": 体力充足，但因为执行了回城更新，需要任务层重启流程
            - int: 体力不足，返回缺少的体力值 (missing_stamina)
        """
        # 1. Check Cached Stamina
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        
        if current_stamina >= cost:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_sufficient_cached", current_stamina, cost))
            return True
            
        # 2. Double Check (Go Home -> Update -> Check Again)
        self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_low_cached", current_stamina, cost))
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_trigger_stamina_update"))
        
        self.device.ensure_home()
        self.update_stamina()
        
        new_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        
        if new_stamina >= cost:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_sufficient_updated", new_stamina, cost))
            return "RESTART"
        else:
            missing = cost - new_stamina
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_insufficient_final", new_stamina, cost, missing))
            return missing

    def detect_current_heroes(self):
        """
        检测当前页面显示的英雄
        :return: list, 英雄名称列表 (e.g. ['diana', 'jessie'])
        """
        import os
        
        detected_heroes = []
        heroes_dir = "assets/images/heroes"
        
        if not os.path.exists(heroes_dir):
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_hero_dir_missing", heroes_dir))
            return []
            
        # 遍历目录下的所有文件
        for filename in os.listdir(heroes_dir):
            if filename.endswith("_big.png"):
                # 提取英雄名称 (去掉 _big.png)
                hero_name = filename.replace("_big.png", "")
                
                # 构建 Template
                img_path = os.path.join(heroes_dir, filename)
                tpl = Template(img_path, threshold=0.9)
                
                # 检测是否存在
                if self.device.exists(tpl):
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_detected", hero_name))
                    detected_heroes.append(hero_name)
                    
        return detected_heroes

    def update_stamina(self):
        """
        执行体力更新逻辑
        """
        retry_count = 0
        max_retries = 1
        
        while retry_count <= max_retries:
            # Step 0: Ensure Home
            self.device.ensure_home()

            # Step 0.5: Check for btn_go_map
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_found_go_map"))
                self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            # Step 0_Click: Click Intel
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL):
                self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL)
            else:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_intel_icon_missing"))
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Wait 0.8s
            self.device.sleep(0.8)
            
            # Step 1: Snapshot and Crop
            # Area: 600, 25, 660, 53
            screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
            if not screen_path:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_snapshot_failed"))
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            img = cv2.imread(screen_path)
            if img is None:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_read_snapshot_failed"))
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Crop: y1:y2, x1:x2
            # Area from MD: 600, 25, 660, 53 (x1, y1, x2, y2)
            # OpenCV uses img[y:h, x:w]
            x1, y1, x2, y2 = 600, 25, 660, 53
            crop_img = img[y1:y2, x1:x2]
            
            # Step 2: Match Numbers
            results = [] # List of (x_coord, digit)
            
            # Loop 0-9
            for digit in range(10):
                template = self.number_templates[digit]
                # match_all_in returns list of results
                matches = template.match_all_in(crop_img)
                
                if matches:
                    for match in matches:
                        # match is usually a dict with 'result' (center point)
                        if isinstance(match, dict) and 'result' in match:
                            x = match['result'][0]
                            results.append((x, digit))
                        elif isinstance(match, (tuple, list)):
                                x = match[0]
                                results.append((x, digit))
                
                # Check Limit
                if len(results) >= 3:
                    break
            
            # Check Empty
            if not results:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_ocr_failed", retry_count, max_retries))
                retry_count += 1
                continue
            
            # Sort by X coordinate
            results.sort(key=lambda x: x[0])
            
            # Concat
            stamina_str = "".join([str(r[1]) for r in results])
            try:
                stamina_val = int(stamina_str)
            except ValueError:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_stamina_parse_failed", stamina_str))
                retry_count += 1
                continue
                
            # Save
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_updated", stamina_val))
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", stamina_val)
            
            # Return to home to be safe (optional, but good practice if we left it in Intel screen)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            
            # End
            return True
            
        self.logger.error(get_text("log_stamina_update_failed_final"))
        self.device.ensure_home()
        return False
