# tasks/combat/rally.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from game.map_searcher import MapSearcher
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from airtest.core.api import Template
import math

class RallyTask(CombatTask):
    # =========================================================================
    # 1. UI 配置定义
    # =========================================================================
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable", 
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Combat"
        },
        {
            "key": "target_level",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_target_level", 
            "min": 1,
            "max": 7, # 巨兽等级通常较低 (1-7)
            "default": 1
        },
        {
            "key": "remaining_attacks",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_remaining_attacks", 
            "min": 0,
            "max": 100,
            "default": 3 # 巨兽次数通常较少
        },
        {
            "key": "concurrent_queues",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_concurrent_queues",
            "min": 1,
            "max": 6,
            "default": 1
        },
        {
            "key": "hero_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_hero_mode",
            "options": [
                {"value": "any", "label": "opt_hero_any"},
                {"value": "diana", "label": "opt_hero_diana"},
                {"value": "diana_fahd", "label": "opt_hero_fahd_diana"}
            ],
            "default": "any"
        },
        {
            "key": "soldier_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_soldier_mode",
            "options": [
                {"value": "full", "label": "opt_soldier_full"},
                {"value": "equal", "label": "opt_soldier_equal"}
            ],
            "default": "full"
        }
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "RallyTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 45 # 优先级略高于打野怪

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 480 # 默认 10 分钟 (因为集结本身需要5分钟等待)

    def run_impl(self):
        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 1: 读取配置 (Load Config)
        # =====================================================================
        # 注意: key 是 modules.rallytask.*
        target_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.rallytask.target_level", 1)
        remaining = self.data.get_cfg("modules.rallytask.remaining_attacks", 0)
        user_queue_limit = self.data.get_cfg("modules.rallytask.concurrent_queues", 1)
        hero_mode = self.data.get_cfg("modules.rallytask.hero_mode", "any")
        soldier_mode = self.data.get_cfg("modules.rallytask.soldier_mode", "full")

        # 检查剩余次数
        if remaining <= 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_finished_zero_rem", self.name))
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.rallytask.enable", False)
            return False

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_rally_start_round", remaining, target_level))

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 2: 前置环境与资源检查 (Pre-Check Logic)
        # =====================================================================
        
        # 2.1 确保在主页
        self.device.ensure_home()

        # 2.2 获取游戏内实际空闲队列
        game_idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        
        # 2.3 OCR 更新体力
        if not self.update_stamina():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_read_fail_skip"))
            return False
        
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 3: 计算本轮执行批次 (Calc Batch Size)
        # =====================================================================
        
        # A. 队列限制
        adjusted_queue_limit = min(user_queue_limit, game_idle_queues)
        
        # B. 英雄限制
        if hero_mode in ["diana", "diana_fahd"]:
            adjusted_queue_limit = 1
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_hero_mode_limit_queue"))

        # C. 体力限制 (与 Beast 的主要区别)
        diana_skill_lv = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
        
        # 获取单次集结消耗 (默认25)
        unit_cost = 25
        if hero_mode in ["diana", "diana_fahd"] and diana_skill_lv > 0:
            # 查表: DIANA_COST_TABLE[lv][0] 是集结消耗
            unit_cost = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_skill_lv, (25, 10))[0] 
        
        available_stamina = self.get_disposable_stamina(current_stamina)
        possible_by_stamina = available_stamina // unit_cost

        # --- 最终计算 Batch Size ---
        batch_size = min(adjusted_queue_limit, possible_by_stamina, remaining)

        # 详细日志
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_calc_detail", 
                                  user_queue_limit, game_idle_queues, current_stamina, remaining, batch_size))

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 4: 批次有效性检查与等待逻辑 (Wait Logic)
        # =====================================================================
        if batch_size <= 0:
            # 4.1 原因：没队列
            if adjusted_queue_limit == 0:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_no_idle_queues_wait"))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=180) # 3分钟后重试
                return False
            
            # 4.2 原因：没体力
            if possible_by_stamina == 0:
                missing_stamina = unit_cost - current_stamina
                wait_seconds = missing_stamina * 300 # 5分钟/点
                wait_minutes = math.ceil(wait_seconds / 60)
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_wait", current_stamina, unit_cost, wait_minutes))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
                return False
                
            return False

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 5: 内部循环执行 (Execution Loop)
        # =====================================================================
        searcher = MapSearcher(self.device, self.data)
        success_count = 0
        
        # 出击按钮 (蓝色) - 集结也是点这个
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_start", batch_size))

        for i in range(batch_size):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_processing_batch", i+1, batch_size))
            
            # 5.1 地图搜索 (参数 "terror")
            # MapSearcher 内部会自动处理 "搜索 -> 点击橙色 -> 点击集结" 的逻辑
            if not searcher.run("terror", target_level):
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_map_search_failed"))
                break
            
            # 5.2 确保在战斗界面
            try:
                self.verify_combat_page()
            except Exception:
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_combat_page_failed"))
                break

            # 5.3 选将逻辑
            target_heroes = []
            if hero_mode == "diana":
                target_heroes = ["diana"]
            elif hero_mode == "diana_fahd":
                target_heroes = ["diana", "fahd"]
            
            try:
                self.select_heroes(target_heroes)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("err_hero_busy_skip", e))
                break

            # 5.4 兵力设置
            if soldier_mode == "equal":
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_clicking_equal"))
                self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL)
                self.device.sleep(0.5)
            else:
                pass

            real_time_cost = self.get_realtime_cost(mode="rally")
            
            if current_stamina < real_time_cost:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_insufficient_final", current_stamina, real_time_cost, 0))
                break

            # 5.5 出击 (Dispatch)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_dispatching"))
            self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            if self.verify_attack_success():
                success_count += 1
                
                # 5.6 内存数据更新 (Update Data)
                remaining -= 1
                self.data.set_cfg("modules.rallytask.remaining_attacks", remaining)
                
                # 更新本地体力记录 (使用当前任务计算出的消耗)
                current_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - real_time_cost)
                self.data.set_stat("stamina", current_stamina)
                
                # 更新空闲队列
                game_idle_queues -= 1
                
            else:
                self.logger.error(get_text("err_attack_verify_fail"))
                break
                
            # 循环继续...

        # =====================================================================
        # 阶段 6: 善后处理 (Post Loop)
        # =====================================================================
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_batch_finished", success_count))
        
        # 6.1 保存数据
        self.data.save_config()
        self.data.save_stats()

        if remaining <= 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_task_done_disable", self.name))
            # 禁用任务
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.rallytask.enable", False)
            self.data.save_config()
            return True
        
        # 6.2 设置下次运行时间 (回城等待)
        # 集结等待逻辑：固定集结等待(5min=300s) + 行军战斗回城(约3min=180s)
        # 基础等待 480秒，每多一队稍微加一点缓冲
        wait_time = 420 + (success_count * 5)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_waiting_return", wait_time))
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_time)
        
        # 6.3 确保回到主页
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        return True

==================================================
FILE: tasks\combat\wolf.py
==================================================
from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from airtest.core.api import Template
from basic import TaskInterruptedError
import time

class WolfTask(CombatTask):
    # UI 配置定义
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable_wolf",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Combat"
        },
        {
            "key": "interval",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_interval",
            "min": 1,
            "max": 1440,
            "default": 450
        },
        {
            "key": "max_attacks",
            "type": "spinbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_attacks",
            "min": 1,
            "max": 100,
            "default": 10
        },
        {
            "key": "one_soldier",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_one_soldier",
            "default": False
        },
        {
            "key": "heroes",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_hero",
            "options": [
                {"value": "none", "label": "opt_hero_any"},
                {"value": "diana", "label": "opt_hero_diana"},
                {"value": "diana_fahd", "label": "opt_hero_fahd_diana"}
            ],
            "default": "none"
        },
        {
            "key": "fallback_policy",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_wolf_fallback",
            "options": [
                {"value": "use_default", "label": "opt_fallback_default"},
                {"value": "wait_1h", "label": "opt_fallback_wait"} # UI显示叫Wait 1h，实际逻辑现在改为重试机制
            ],
            "default": "use_default"
        }
    ]
    
    @property
    def name(self):
        return "WolfTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 40

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        # 默认 7分30秒
        return 450

    def run_impl(self):
        # 1. 读取配置
        use_one_soldier = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.one_soldier", False)
        hero_config = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.heroes", "none")
        fallback_policy = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.fallback_policy", "use_default")
        max_attacks = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.max_attacks", 10)
        interval = self.data.get_cfg("modules.wolftask.interval", 450)
        
        # 2. 检查剩余次数
        if max_attacks <= 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_finished"))
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.enable", False)
            return False # 停止执行

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_start", use_one_soldier, hero_config))

        # 3. 预检查 (Pre-Check)
        # 如果预检查失败，pre_check 内部会设置好 next_run 并返回 False
        if not self.pre_check(hero_config):
            return False

        # 4. 寻找与战斗配置循环 (处理 "验证页面失败 -> 重试" 的逻辑)
        # 外层循环处理 "RESTART" 信号 (体力更新后重启流程)
        max_restarts = 2
        restart_count = 0
        
        while restart_count < max_restarts:
            combat_ready = False
            
            # 对应流程图中 "CombatRetryCheck < 3" 的循环
            for attempt in range(3):
                # 4.1 寻找狼 (内部包含 3 次寻找重试)
                if not self.find_and_click_wolf():
                    # find_and_click_wolf 内部重试失败后返回 False
                    # 根据流程图，寻找失败直接结束
                    self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_search_fail"))
                    return False

                # 4.2 验证是否在出击界面
                # 这里对应 "VerifyPage" 节点
                is_combat_page = False
                try:
                    self.verify_combat_page()
                    is_combat_page = True
                except TaskInterruptedError:
                    raise
                except:
                    is_combat_page = False
                
                if not is_combat_page:
                    self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_combat_page_fail", attempt+1))
                    continue # 对应 "CombatRetryCheck -- 是 --> NavStart"
                
                # 4.3 战斗准备 (英雄 & 兵力)
                # 对应 "NeedHeroes", "SelectHeroes", "Fallback" 等逻辑
                if not self.prepare_combat_logic(use_one_soldier, hero_config, fallback_policy):
                    # 如果 prepare 返回 False，说明触发了 Wait 策略且次数未满
                    # 此时 next_run 已经被设置为 5 分钟后
                    return False
                
                # 如果成功准备好
                combat_ready = True
                break
            
            if not combat_ready:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_wolf_combat_page_fail_final"))
                return False

            # 5. 发起集结 (Start Rally)
            rally_result = self.start_rally_logic()
            
            if rally_result == "RESTART":
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_restart_for_stamina"))
                restart_count += 1
                continue
            
            if rally_result is True:
                # === 数据更新 (Update Stats) ===
                # 成功发起集结，跳出 while 循环
                break
            else:
                # 返回 False (体力不足或其他错误)，直接结束
                return False
        
        # 如果循环结束是因为成功
        if rally_result is True:
            
            # A. 扣除剩余次数
            new_max = max(0, max_attacks - 1)
            self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.max_attacks", new_max)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_attacks_left", new_max))
            
            # B. 扣除体力 (统计数据)
            cost = self.get_actual_cost()
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            new_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - cost)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_stamina)
            
            # C. 扣除空闲队列 (统计数据)
            current_idle = self.sidebar.idle_march_count
            new_idle = max(0, current_idle - 1)
            self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", new_idle)
            self.sidebar.idle_march_count = new_idle
            
            # D. 判断是否结束
            if new_max == 0:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_disabled_zero_attacks"))
                self.data.set_cfg("modules.wolftask.enable", False)
                # 不设置下次运行，任务自然停止
            else:
                # E. 安排下一次运行
                # 显式调用 set_next_run，确保按照配置的 interval (如 450s) 运行
                self.set_next_run(seconds=interval)
                
            return True
        
        return False

    def pre_check(self, hero_config):
        """
        0. 预检查
        """
        # 0.1 Ensure Home
        self.device.ensure_home()

        # 0.2 Check Queue
        idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        if idle_queues <= 0:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_no_queue"))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            return False

        # 0.2.1 Update Stamina (新增步骤)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_updating_stamina"))
        self.update_stamina()

        # 0.3 Check Stamina
        required_stamina = self.get_required_stamina(hero_config)
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        available_stamina = self.get_disposable_stamina(current_stamina)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_stamina_check", available_stamina, required_stamina))

        # 判定时使用 available_stamina
        if available_stamina < required_stamina:
            # 计算缺口 (注意：如果是因为被锁住了，这里会算出需要等待)
            # 例如: 当前100, 锁了75 -> 可用25. 需求30.
            # 缺口 = 30 - 25 = 5. 程序会进入等待，这正是我们想要的。
            missing = required_stamina - available_stamina
            wait_seconds = missing * 300
            
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_stamina_gap", missing, missing * 5))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
            return False
        
        return True

    def find_and_click_wolf(self):
        """
        寻找狼牙棒，包含内部重试逻辑
        """
        IMG_BACKPACK = Template(r"assets/images/home/backpack.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/wolf/check.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_WOLF = Template(r"assets/images/wolf/wolf.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        IMG_BTN_ORANGE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_orange.png", threshold=0.9)

        # 重试循环 (Retry < 3)
        for i in range(3):
            self.logger.info(f"寻找物品尝试 {i+1}/3")
            
            # 每次重试都先 Ensure Home (回路起点)
            self.device.ensure_home()

            # 1. 点击 backpack
            if not self.device.click(IMG_BACKPACK):
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_backpack_fail"))
                continue
            self.device.sleep(0.5)

            # 2. 点击标签页
            self.device.click((630, 120))
            self.device.sleep(0.8)

            # 3. 检查 check.png
            if not self.device.exists(IMG_CHECK):
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_check_fail"))
                continue
            
            # 4. 点击 wolf.png
            if not self.device.click(IMG_WOLF):
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_icon_fail"))
                continue
            self.device.sleep(0.5)

            # 成功找到并点击，跳出循环进入下一步
            break
        else:
            # 循环正常结束说明3次都失败
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_wolf_item_fail"))
            return False

        # 后续操作 (点击使用 -> 搜索 -> 攻击)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_click_use"))
        self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE)
        self.device.sleep(1)

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_click_pos"))
        self.device.click((360, 570))
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_click_search"))
        self.device.click(IMG_BTN_ORANGE)
        self.device.sleep(1.5) # 等待跳转

        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_click_rally"))
        self.device.sleep(1)
        if not self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_attack_btn_missing"))
            return False
        
        self.device.sleep(1.5)
        return True

    def prepare_combat_logic(self, use_one_soldier, hero_config, fallback_policy):
        """
        战斗配置核心逻辑 (包含英雄选将和失败策略)
        """
        # 解析需要选择的英雄
        target_heroes = []
        if hero_config == "diana":
            target_heroes = ["diana"]
        elif hero_config == "diana_fahd":
            target_heroes = ["diana", "fahd"]
            
        hero_selection_success = True
        
        if target_heroes:
            try:
                self.select_heroes(target_heroes)
                # [成功路径] 顺便重置计数
                self.data.set_runtime("wolf_hero_retry_count", 0)
            except TaskInterruptedError:
                raise
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("log_hero_selection_failed", e))
                hero_selection_success = False
                
                # [失败路径]
                if fallback_policy == "wait_1h": # UI上叫 Wait 1h，实际逻辑是有限重试
                    # 获取当前重试次数
                    retry_count = self.data.get_runtime("wolf_hero_retry_count", 0)
                    
                    if retry_count < 16:
                        # [情况 A] 次数未满: 推迟5分钟
                        new_count = retry_count + 1
                        self.data.set_runtime("wolf_hero_retry_count", new_count)
                        self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_hero_retry", new_count))
                        
                        self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                        return False # 结束本次运行
                    else:
                        # [情况 B] 次数耗尽: 强制执行
                        self.logger.error(get_text("log_wolf_hero_force"))
                        # 按 Back 键退出英雄选择界面 (如果有的话)，或者确保在出击界面
                        self.device.keyevent("BACK")
                        self.device.sleep(0.5)
                        # 继续往下执行 (Fall through)
                else:
                    # Default 策略: 直接按 Back 继续
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_hero_default"))
                    self.device.keyevent("BACK")
                    self.device.sleep(0.5)
        
        # 检查兵力设置
        if use_one_soldier:
            self.set_one_soldier(True)
        else:
            self.set_one_soldier(False) # 显式设置为平均，防止上次残留
            
        return True

    def start_rally_logic(self):
        """
        发起集结逻辑 (包含体力检测)
        :return:
            - True: 成功发起
            - False: 失败 (体力不足或UI错误)
            - "RESTART": 需要重启流程 (体力更新后)
        """
        # 1. 体力检测
        # 获取实际消耗 (通常是 25 或戴安娜优惠值)
        cost = self.get_actual_cost()
        stamina_check = self.check_stamina_sufficient(cost)
        
        if stamina_check == "RESTART":
            return "RESTART"
            
        if isinstance(stamina_check, int): # missing stamina
            missing = stamina_check
            wait_seconds = max(300, missing * 300)
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_stamina_warn", wait_seconds/60))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
            return False

        # stamina_check is True -> Proceed
        
        # 2. 点击出击
        IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_wolf_click_attack"))
        
        if self.device.click(IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.device.sleep(1)
            # 3. 验证是否回到主页
            if self.verify_attack_success():
                return True
            else:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_wolf_attack_home_fail"))
                return False
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_wolf_attack_btn_missing"))
            return False

    # --- 辅助方法 ---

    def get_required_stamina(self, hero_config):
        """计算理论所需体力"""
        required = 25
        if "diana" in hero_config:
            diana_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
            if diana_level > 0:
                costs = self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)
                required = costs[0]
        return required

    def get_actual_cost(self):
        """根据当前是否识别到 Diana 计算实际消耗"""
        # 注意：这里使用父类的方法检查屏幕上是否有 Diana 头像
        if self.find_diana():
            diana_level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.general.diana_level", 0)
            if diana_level > 0:
                return self.DIANA_COST_TABLE.get(diana_level, self.DEFAULT_COST)[0]
        return 25

# tasks/daily/alliance.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class AllianceBaseTask(BaseTask):
    """
    Base class for Alliance tasks to share assets and navigation logic.
    """
    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # --- Assets ---
        self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/home/alliance.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_TECH_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/tech.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_THUMB_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/thumb.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_CHEST_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/chest.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Navigation
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)

    def enter_alliance_view(self):
        """
        Navigate to Alliance View
        """
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_alliance_icon"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_ALLIANCE_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(2.0)
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_alliance_icon_not_found"))
            return False

class AllianceTechTask(AllianceBaseTask):
    # UI Configuration
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Tech Donation",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Alliance"
        }
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "AllianceTechTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 90 # Low priority

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 14400 # 4 hours

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_start_tech_donation"))
        
        if not self.enter_alliance_view():
            return False

        # Click Tech
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_TECH_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_tech_icon"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_TECH_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_tech_icon_not_found"))
            return False

        # Check Thumb/Donation available
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_THUMB_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_found_recommended_tech"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_THUMB_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            # Long Press Donation Button
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_long_press_donation"))
            self.device.swipe((470, 1020), (470, 1020), duration=6.0)
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_no_recommended_tech"))
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return True

class AllianceChestTask(AllianceBaseTask):
    # UI Configuration

            return False

        # Click Chest
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_CHEST_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_chest_icon"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_CHEST_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_chest_icon_not_found"))
            return False

        # Sequence from MD
        # C_Pos1: Click (595, 395)
        self.device.click((595, 395))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos2: Click (572, 1187) x2
        self.device.click((572, 1187))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((572, 1187))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos3: Click (87, 385)
        self.device.click((87, 385))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos4: Click (321, 1193) x2
        self.device.click((321, 1193))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((321, 1193))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # C_Pos5: Click (350, 140) x2 (Close/Back?)
        self.device.click((350, 140))
        self.device.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click((350, 140))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        self.device.ensure_home()
        return True
    

class AllianceHelpTask(AllianceBaseTask):
    # UI 配置定义 (供 UI 自动生成简单的 Enable 选项，复杂配置我们在 task_widgets.py 里写)
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "Enable Alliance Help",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Alliance"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # 定义帮助图标的图片模板
        # 注意：使用文件列表中存在的 alli_help.png
        self.IMG_ALLI_HELP = Template(r"assets/images/alliance/alliance_help.png", threshold=0.9)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "AllianceHelpTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        # 优先级设置得高一点，因为点帮助是举手之劳，应该尽快完成
        return 20 

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        # 默认 10 分钟 (600秒)
        return 600

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_alliance_help_start", "检查联盟帮助"))
        
        # 1. Ensure Home (确保在主页)
        self.device.ensure_home()
        self.device.sleep(1.0) # 稍微等一下主页加载

        # 2. 检测并点击
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_ALLI_HELP):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_alliance_help_found", "发现帮助请求，点击中..."))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_ALLI_HELP)
            self.device.sleep(0.5)
            self.device.ensure_home()
        else:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_alliance_help_not_found", "当前没有帮助请求"))

        # 无论点没点，都算任务成功
        return True

# tasks/daily/intel.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-

import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime, timedelta, timezone
from airtest.core.api import Template
from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from tasks.daily.stamina_control import StaminaControlTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class IntelTask(CombatTask):
    # UI Configuration
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable_intel",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        },
        {
            "key": "desc",
            "type": "label",
            "label": "cfg_intel_desc"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # --- Assets ---
        # Intel Entry
        self.IMG_INTEL_ICON = Template(r"assets/images/home/intel.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Task Icons (Shape Only - Using White/High Contrast version)
        self.ICON_THRESHOLD = 0.85 # Slightly lower for shape matching across colors
        
        self.ICONS = {
            "Tavern": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_tavern_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
            "Tent": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_tent_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
            "Beast": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_beast_white.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
            "Event": Template(r"assets/images/intel/intel_event.png", threshold=self.ICON_THRESHOLD, rgb=False),
        }

        # Task Execution
        self.IMG_TAVERN_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/intel/tavern_check.png", threshold=0.8)
        
        # Buttons
        self.IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_GREEN = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_green.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_orange.png", threshold=0.85, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_EQUAL = Template(r"assets/images/combat/equal.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # Navigation
        self.IMG_BTN_TOWN = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_town.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP = Template(r"assets/images/home/btn_go_map.png", threshold=0.9)

        # Stamina/Red Dot Checks
        # These need to be defined or we use pixel checks. 
        # For now, assuming pixel checks are done via device.get_pixel or similar if coordinates known,
        # or we use small templates if provided. 
        # The MD mentions "Check Red Pixel". We'll implement helper for that.

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "IntelTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 25

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        # 00:02, 08:02, 16:02 UTC
        now = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        targets = [0, 8, 16]
        next_run = None
        
        for h in targets:
            target_time = now.replace(hour=h, minute=2, second=0, microsecond=0)
            if target_time > now:
                next_run = target_time
                break
                
        if not next_run:
            # Tomorrow 00:02
            next_run = now.replace(hour=0, minute=2, second=0, microsecond=0) + timedelta(days=1)
            
        # Return seconds until next run
        return (next_run - now).total_seconds()

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_start"))

        # --- 0. Pre-checks ---
        if not self._check_stamina_and_queue():
            return False

        # --- 2. Batch Scan & Execute Loop ---
        while True:
            # --- 1. Navigate to Intel ---
            if not self._navigate_to_intel():
                return False

            # 2.1 Batch Scan
            task_list = self._scan_all_targets()
            
            if not task_list:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_finished", get_text("log_intel_no_more_targets")))
                break
                
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_found_tasks", len(task_list), [t[0] for t in task_list]))
            
            # 2.2 Loop Execution
            for task_type, pos in task_list:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_executing", task_type, pos))
                
                # Click the task icon
                self.device.click(pos)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
                
                # Dispatch
                result = False
                if task_type == "Tavern":
                    result = self._handle_tavern()
                elif task_type == "Tent":
                    result = self._handle_tent()
                elif task_type == "Beast":
                    result = self._handle_beast()
                elif task_type == "Event":
                    result = self._handle_event()
                
                if result == "RESCAN":
                    self.logger.info("Rescanning targets...")
                    break

                if not result:
                    # If a task failed (e.g. stamina low, error), we might need to stop or return
                    # Based on MD, most failures lead to GoHomeError -> return False
                    # But some might just be skip?
                    # For now, if handle returns False, we assume it handled the exit/logging
                    return False

                if not self._navigate_to_intel():
                    return False
                self.device.sleep(1.0) # Wait for view to settle

        # --- 3. Finish & Return Home ---
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_finished", get_text("log_intel_finished_all")))
        self.device.ensure_home()
        return True

    def _check_stamina_and_queue(self):
        self.update_stamina()
        # 1. Check Stamina
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 120)
        if current_stamina < 10:
            delay_min = (10 - current_stamina) * 5
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_stamina_low", current_stamina, 10))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            return False
            
        # 2. Check Queue
        idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        if idle_queues == 0:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_sidebar_idle_count", 0))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=600)
            return False
            
        return True

    def _navigate_to_intel(self):
        """
        Navigate to Intel page and reset view.
        This is used both for initial entry and for returning after a task.
        It ensures the view state is consistent (reset) for coordinate reuse.
        """
        # 1. Check if we are already in Wild (Intel icon visible)
        # If we just finished a task, we are likely in Wild.
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_already_wild"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
            # Reset View (Critical for coordinate consistency)
            self._click_reset_pixel()
            return True

        # 2. If not, ensure we are at Home (or Wild) to start fresh
        # This handles cases where we might be in a sub-menu or weird state
        self.device.ensure_home()
        self.device.sleep(0.5)

        # 3. Check if in Wild (Intel icon is only visible in Wild/Map view)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP):
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_switch_map"))
                self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_GO_MAP)
                self.device.sleep(1.0)
            else:
                # If neither Town button nor Go Map button is visible, we might be lost
                pass

        # 4. Click Intel Icon
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_click_search"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
            
            # 5. Reset View (Critical for coordinate consistency)
            self._click_reset_pixel()
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_enter_fail"))
            return False

        return True

    def _click_reset_pixel(self):
        # Click specific coordinate twice to reset view
        # (360, 1120) from previous code
        self.device.click((360, 1120))
        self.device.sleep(0.5)
        self.device.click((360, 1120))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

    def _scan_all_targets(self):
        """
        Snapshot once, then match all 3 types.
        Returns list of (Type, Pos)
        """
        try:
            # 1. Snapshot (Using DeviceOperator wrapper)
            # This saves to file and returns path
            screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
            if not screen_path:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_snapshot_fail"))
                return []
                
            # 2. Read Image
            screen = cv2.imread(screen_path)
            if screen is None:
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_read_fail", screen_path))
                return []
                
            targets = []
            
            # 3. Match All In
            for task_type, template in self.ICONS.items():
                # match_all_in returns list of results (dicts or coordinates depending on version, usually dicts with 'result')
                results = template.match_all_in(screen)
                
                if results:
                    for res in results:
                        # Airtest match_all_in returns list of dicts: {'result': (x,y), 'rectangle': ..., 'confidence': ...}
                        if isinstance(res, dict) and 'result' in res:
                            pos = res['result']
                            targets.append((task_type, pos))
                        elif isinstance(res, (tuple, list)):
                            # Just in case it returns raw coordinates
                            targets.append((task_type, res))
            
            return targets
            
        except Exception as e:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_scan_fail", e))
            return []

    def _post_task_sequence(self):
        # Ensure we are back at Intel (should be handled by task return, but double check)
        # Then click reset pixel
        self._click_reset_pixel()

    def _handle_tavern(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_executing", "Tavern", ""))
        # 1. Click Blue (Start)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_tavern_blue_missing"))
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop
            
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. Check Stamina
        req_stamina = 10
        stamina_check = self.check_stamina_sufficient(req_stamina)
        
        if stamina_check == "RESTART":
            return "RESCAN"
            
        if isinstance(stamina_check, int) and not isinstance(stamina_check, bool): 
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_tavern_stamina_low"))
            
            # [新增] 尝试自救
            if self._try_rescue_stamina():
                self.logger.info("Stamina rescued. Restarting scan.")
                return "RESCAN" # 返回 RESCAN，让主循环重新进入地图
            
            # [旧逻辑] 自救失败，计算等待时间
            delay_min = max(1, stamina_check * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

        # 3. Click blue (Using Region)
        # Region: (365, 628)
        self.device.click((365, 628))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)

        # 4. click green
        if not self.device.click((470, 1200)): return False
        
        # 5. Scan for Check
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_tavern_scan"))
        start_time = time.time()
        found = False
        while time.time() - start_time < 60:
            if self.device.exists(self.IMG_TAVERN_CHECK):
                found = True
                break
            self.device.sleep(1.3)
            
        if found:
            self.device.press_back()
            self.device.sleep(1.0)
            self._update_stats(cost=10)
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_tavern_timeout"))
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

    def _handle_tent(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_executing", "Tent", ""))
        # 1. Click Blue
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_tent_blue_missing"))
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop

        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. Check Stamina
        req_stamina = 12
        stamina_check = self.check_stamina_sufficient(req_stamina)
        
        if stamina_check == "RESTART":
            return "RESCAN"
            
        if isinstance(stamina_check, int) and not isinstance(stamina_check, bool): 
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_tent_stamina_low"))
            
            # [新增] 尝试自救
            if self._try_rescue_stamina():
                return "RESCAN"
            
            # [旧逻辑]
            delay_min = max(1, stamina_check * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
            
        # 3. Click Green (Using Region)
        # Region: (365, 628)
        self.device.click((365, 628))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        self._update_stats(cost=12)
        return True

    def _handle_beast(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_executing", "Beast", ""))
        
        # 1. Check Queue
        idle_queues = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0)
        if idle_queues == 0:
            # Double check logic
            self.logger.info("Cached idle queues is 0. Double checking...")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
            
            if idle_queues == 0:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_no_queue"))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                # self.device.ensure_home() # Already at home
                return False
            else:
                self.logger.info(f"Found {idle_queues} idle queues. Restarting scan.")
                return "RESCAN"

        # 2. Click Blue
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_blue_missing"))
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop

        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 3. Click Orange
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 4. Check Stamina
        req_stamina = 10 # As per instruction
        stamina_check = self.check_stamina_sufficient(req_stamina)
        
        if stamina_check == "RESTART":
            return "RESCAN"
            
        if isinstance(stamina_check, int) and not isinstance(stamina_check, bool):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_stamina_low"))
            
            # [新增] 尝试自救
            if self._try_rescue_stamina():
                return "RESCAN"
            
            # [旧逻辑]
            delay_min = max(1, stamina_check * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
            
        # 5. Calculate Cost (High Stamina)
        # Determine cost before action
        cost = self.calculate_cost(mode="wild")
        
        # 6. Click Equal
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 7. Click Blue2 (March) (Using Region)
        # Region: (546, 1213)
        self.device.click((546, 1213))
        self.device.sleep(2.0)
        
        # 8. Check Home (Success)
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON) or self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            # Success
            self._update_stats(cost=cost, queue=1)
            
            # Check Queue After
            if self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0) == 0:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_queue_full"))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                self.device.ensure_home()
                return False
                
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error(get_text("log_intel_beast_home_fail"))
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
        

    def _handle_event(self):
        """
        处理 Event 任务 (逻辑与 Beast 一致)
        """
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_executing", "Event", ""))
        
        # 1. Check Queue (检查队列)
        idle_queues = self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0)
        if idle_queues == 0:
            # Double check logic
            self.logger.info("Cached idle queues is 0. Double checking...")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
            
            if idle_queues == 0:
                # 复用 Beast 的日志 key，或者你可以去 zh.yaml 加一个新的
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_no_queue")) 
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                return False
            else:
                self.logger.info(f"Found {idle_queues} idle queues. Restarting scan.")
                return "RESCAN"

        # 2. Click Blue (点击蓝色按钮 - 前往)
        if not self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE):
            self.logger.info("Event blue button missing, skipping.")
            return True # Treat as success to continue loop

        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 3. Click Orange (点击橙色按钮 - 搜索/攻击)
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_ORANGE): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 4. Check Stamina (检查体力, 默认10点)
        req_stamina = 10 
        stamina_check = self.check_stamina_sufficient(req_stamina)
        
        if stamina_check == "RESTART":
            return "RESCAN"
            
        if isinstance(stamina_check, int) and not isinstance(stamina_check, bool):
            # 这里之前的日志 key 可能也是 log_intel_beast_stamina_low，保持一致即可
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_stamina_low")) 
            
            # [新增] 尝试自救
            if self._try_rescue_stamina():
                return "RESCAN"
            
            # [旧逻辑]
            delay_min = max(1, stamina_check * 5)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=delay_min * 60)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False
            
        # 5. Calculate Cost (计算戴安娜减耗)
        cost = self.calculate_cost(mode="wild")
        
        # 6. Click Equal (点击均兵)
        if not self.device.click(self.IMG_EQUAL): return False
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 7. Click Blue2 (March) (点击出击 - 使用固定坐标区域)
        # Region: (546, 1213)
        self.device.click((546, 1213))
        self.device.sleep(2.0)
        
        # 8. Check Home (Success) (验证是否返回地图或主页)
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_INTEL_ICON) or self.device.exists(self.IMG_BTN_TOWN):
            # Success
            self._update_stats(cost=cost, queue=1)
            
            # Check Queue After
            if self.data.get_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", 0) == 0:
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_beast_queue_full"))
                self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
                self.device.ensure_home()
                return False
                
            return True
        else:
            self.logger.error("Event attack failed to return to map.")
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

    def _update_stats(self, cost=0, queue=0):
        if cost > 0:
            current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            new_stamina = max(0, current_stamina - cost)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", new_stamina)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_deduct_stamina", cost, new_stamina))

        if queue > 0:
            current_idle = self.sidebar.idle_march_count
            new_idle = max(0, current_idle - queue)
            self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", new_idle)
            self.sidebar.idle_march_count = new_idle
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_intel_deduct_queue", queue, new_idle))

    def _try_rescue_stamina(self):
        """
        借用 StaminaControlTask 的逻辑来尝试领取免费体力。
        """
        self.logger.info("Attempting to rescue stamina using StaminaControlTask logic...")
        
        # 1. 实例化体力任务对象 (借用它的资源和方法)
        # 注意：这里只是创建对象，不会执行它的 run_impl
        helper = StaminaControlTask(self.device, self.data)
        
        # 2. 获取时间窗口信息 (直接复用 helper 的逻辑)
        current_tag, _, is_window_active = helper._get_current_period_info()
        
        if not is_window_active:
            self.logger.info("Not in free stamina claim window.")
            return False

        # 3. 检查本地记录 (双重检查)
        last_claimed = self.data.get_stat("tasks.StaminaControlTask.last_claimed_tag", "")
        if last_claimed == current_tag:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_already_claimed"))
            return False

        # 4. 进入体力界面 (复用 helper 的导航逻辑)
        if not helper._enter_stamina_view():
            self.logger.warning("Failed to enter stamina view.")
            return False

        # 5. 视觉检查 (复用 helper 的 check.png 识别)
        if helper._check_is_claimed():
            self.logger.info("Visual check: Already claimed.")
            helper._mark_as_claimed(current_tag)
            self.device.ensure_home()
            return False

        # 6. 执行领取 (复用 helper 的点击逻辑)
        helper._perform_claim_click()
        helper._mark_as_claimed(current_tag)
        
        # 7. 更新体力并返回主页
        self.update_stamina()
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        self.logger.info("Stamina rescued successfully!")
        return True

FILE: tasks\daily\mail.py

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from airtest.core.api import Template
from i18n.i18n import get_text
import time

class MailTask(BaseTask):
    # 配置 Schema (用于元数据，实际 UI 由 Widget 控制)
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable", 
            "type": "checkbox", 
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"  # <--- 【关键修改】加上这一行，告诉 UI 把它放在“日常”里
        },
        {"key": "collect_system", "default": True},
        {"key": "collect_alliance", "default": True},
        {"key": "collect_report", "default": False},
        {"key": "collect_war", "default": False}
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # 资源: 主页邮件图标
        self.IMG_MAIL = Template(r"assets/images/home/mail.png", threshold=0.9)
        
        # 坐标定义 (x, y)
        self.POS_Categories = {
            "war": (118, 123),      # 战争信息
            "alliance": (194, 111), # 联盟信息
            "system": (391, 114),   # 系统信息
            "report": (466, 121)    # 报告
        }
        self.POS_CLAIM = (600, 1230) # 一键领取

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "MailTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 60

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 3600 # 默认1小时

    def run_impl(self):
        # Log: 开始收取邮件
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_mail_start"))

        # 1. Ensure Home
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        # 2. 搜索并点击 Mail 图标
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_MAIL):
            # Log: 点击邮件图标
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_mail_click_icon"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_MAIL)
            self.device.sleep(0.5) # 等待打开
        else:
            # Log: 未找到邮件图标
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_mail_icon_missing"))
            return False

        # 3. 定义遍历列表 (配置键, 内部键, 翻译键)
        # 翻译键对应 yaml 中的文本，例如 cfg_mail_system="系统信息"
        check_list = [
            ("collect_system", "system", "cfg_mail_system"),
            ("collect_alliance", "alliance", "cfg_mail_alliance"),
            ("collect_report", "report", "cfg_mail_report"),
            ("collect_war", "war", "cfg_mail_war")
        ]

        # 4. 循环处理
        for cfg_key, cat_key, i18n_key in check_list:
            # 获取显示名称 (用于日志)
            display_name = get_text(i18n_key)
            
            # 检查配置是否启用
            if self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.mailtask.{cfg_key}", False):
                # Log: 正在处理: 系统信息
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_mail_process_cat", display_name))
                self.process_category(cat_key)
            else:
                # Log: 跳过: 系统信息 (未启用)
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_mail_skip_cat", display_name))

        # 5. 结束
        self.device.ensure_home()
        # Log: 邮件收取完成
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_mail_finish"))
        return True

    def process_category(self, category_key):
        """处理单个频道的逻辑"""
        cat_pos = self.POS_Categories.get(category_key)
        if not cat_pos: return

        # 动作 1: 点击页面两次，间隔 0.4s
        # 注意：使用 device.click 单次点击 + sleep 确保间隔准确
        self.device.click(cat_pos)
        time.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click(cat_pos)
        
        # 等待界面响应
        time.sleep(0.5)

        # 动作 2: 点击一键领取两次，间隔 0.4s
        self.device.click(self.POS_CLAIM)
        time.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click(self.POS_CLAIM)

        # 等待弹窗或动画
        time.sleep(0.5)

FILE: tasks\daily\recruit_adv.py
