import time
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from airtest.core.api import Template
from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class RecruitAdvTask(BaseTask):
    # UI 配置
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # 资源定义
        self.IMG_BOX = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/silver_box.png", threshold=0.9)
        # 注意：这里开启 rgb=True, threshold=0.94 以区分灰色和亮色的 GO
        self.IMG_GO = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/go.png", threshold=0.94, rgb=True)
        
        # 招募按钮坐标 (根据需求硬编码)
        self.POS_RECRUIT_BTN = (101, 841)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "RecruitAdvTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 11 # 优先级中等

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 310 # 默认间隔并不重要，因为我们会手动 set_next_run

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_start"))
        
        # === 1. 日期检查与重置 (Date Check) ===
        if self._check_new_day():
            self.logger.info("New UTC day detected. Resetting counters.")
            self._reset_counters()

        # === 2. 上限检查 (Limit Check) ===
        daily_count = self.data.get_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.daily_count", 0)
        if daily_count >= 5:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_daily_limit", daily_count))
            self._schedule_tomorrow()
            return True

        # === 3. 导航 (Nav) ===
        if not self.sidebar.switch_to_affairs_page():
            self.logger.warning("Failed to navigate to Affairs page.")
            self._handle_failure()
            return False

        # === 4. 扫描循环 (Scan Loop) ===
        # 策略：先看当前屏，没找到则上滑看下面，共试2次
        found_box_pos = None
        
        for i in range(2):
            self.logger.info(f"Scanning for silver box ({i+1}/2)...")
            
            # 4.1 寻找锚点 (Silver Box)
            # find_in_anchor_row 内部包含了截图，所以我们先用 exists 快速判断有没有箱子
            # 但我们需要箱子的坐标传给 find_in_anchor_row 吗？
            # 不，sidebar.find_in_anchor_row 内部会自己找 anchor_tpl。
            # 为了效率，我们直接调用 find_in_anchor_row
            
            # 在同一行寻找 GO 图标
            # 参数: 锚点, 目标, X范围(默认), Y外扩(默认)
            # 注意：find_in_anchor_row 返回的是 GO 的坐标，不是 Box 的坐标
            # 但我们需要点击 Box。
            # 所以我们需要先手动找 Box。
            
            box_pos = self.device.exists(self.IMG_BOX)
            if box_pos:
                # 找到了盒子，检查同行有没有 GO
                go_pos = self.sidebar.find_in_anchor_row(self.IMG_BOX, self.IMG_GO)
                
                if go_pos:
                    # === 分支 A: 找到了 GO (有免费次数) ===
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_found_free"))
                    self._perform_recruitment(box_pos)
                    return True
                else:
                    self.logger.info("Box found but NO 'GO' button (Cooldown or Used).")
                    # 这里不算失败，可能是CD没好，继续找或者结束
                    # 如果当前屏找到了盒子但没GO，且下面没有盒子了，那就是CD没好
                    # 但为了保险，我们还是继续滑一下看看有没有另一个盒子
            
            # 没找到盒子，或者找到了盒子但没GO -> 滑动
            if i < 1:
                self.sidebar.scroll_up()

        # === 5. 循环结束仍未执行 (分支 B: 无免费次数) ===
        self.logger.warning("No free recruitment available.")
        self._handle_failure()
        return True

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 逻辑辅助函数
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _perform_recruitment(self, box_pos):
        """执行招募动作"""
        # 1. 点击盒子
        self.device.click(box_pos)
        time.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. 点击招募按钮 (固定坐标)
        self.logger.info("Clicking recruit button...")
        self.device.click(self.POS_RECRUIT_BTN)
        time.sleep(1.5)
        
        # 3. 数据更新
        current_daily = self.data.get_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.daily_count", 0) + 1
        self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.daily_count", current_daily)
        self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.retry_count", 0) # 成功则重置重试计数
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_success", current_daily))
        
        # 4. 回主页
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        # 5. 设置下次运行时间
        if current_daily >= 5:
            self._schedule_tomorrow()
        else:
            # 等待CD: 5分10秒 = 310秒
            self.logger.info("Waiting for cooldown (5m 10s)...")
            self.set_next_run(seconds=310)

    def _handle_failure(self):
        """处理失败/未找到的情况 (分支 B)"""
        retry = self.data.get_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.retry_count", 0)
        
        if retry >= 2:
            # B1: 连续失败 2 次 -> 明天再来
            self.logger.warning(f"Failed {retry+1} times. Giving up for today.")
            self._reset_counters(reset_daily=False, reset_retry=True)
            self._schedule_tomorrow()
        else:
            # B2: 还没到限制 -> 稍后重试
            new_retry = retry + 1
            self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.retry_count", new_retry)
            self.logger.warning(f"Retrying later ({new_retry}/2). Wait 5m 30s.")
            self.set_next_run(seconds=330) # 5分30秒
            
        self.sidebar.collapse()

    def _schedule_tomorrow(self):
        """设置下次运行时间为 UTC 明日 00:01"""
        now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        tomorrow = now_utc.replace(hour=0, minute=1, second=0, microsecond=0) + timedelta(days=1)
        
        # 计算秒数差
        wait_seconds = (tomorrow - now_utc).total_seconds()
        
        self.logger.info(f"Task finished for today. Next run: {tomorrow.strftime('%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S UTC')}")
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)

    def _check_new_day(self):
        """检查是否跨天 (UTC)"""
        now_date = datetime.now(timezone.utc).strftime("%Y-%m-%d")
        last_date = self.data.get_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.last_run_date", "")
        
        if now_date != last_date:
            self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.last_run_date", now_date)
            return True
        return False

    def _reset_counters(self, reset_daily=True, reset_retry=True):
        if reset_daily:
            self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.daily_count", 0)
        if reset_retry:
            self.data.set_stat("tasks.RecruitAdvTask.retry_count", 0)

==================================================
FILE: tasks\daily\recruit_epic.py
==================================================
# tasks/daily/recruit_epic.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import time
from airtest.core.api import Template
from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class RecruitEpicTask(BaseTask):
    # UI 配置
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # 资源定义
        # 锚点: 金箱子
        self.IMG_BOX = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/gold_box.png", threshold=0.9)
        # 目标: GO 图标 (开启 RGB 校验以区分灰色状态)
        self.IMG_GO = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/go.png", threshold=0.94, rgb=True)
        
        # 招募按钮坐标 (史诗招募)
        self.POS_RECRUIT_BTN = (139, 1199)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "RecruitEpicTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 66 # 略高于高级招募

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 1200 # 默认 20分钟 (找不到时的CD)

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_epic_start"))
        
        # 1. 导航
        if not self.sidebar.switch_to_affairs_page():
            self.logger.warning("Failed to navigate to Affairs page.")
            self._handle_wait_short()
            return False

        # 2. 扫描循环 (2次)
        for i in range(2):
            self.logger.info(f"Scanning for gold box ({i+1}/2)...")
            
            # 2.1 寻找锚点 (Gold Box)
            box_pos = self.device.exists(self.IMG_BOX)
            
            if box_pos:
                # 2.2 找到了盒子，检查同行 GO
                go_pos = self.sidebar.find_in_anchor_row(self.IMG_BOX, self.IMG_GO)
                
                if go_pos:
                    # === 分支 A: 找到了 GO (有免费次数) ===
                    self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_found_free"))
                    self._perform_recruitment(box_pos)
                    return True
                else:
                    self.logger.info("Gold box found but NO 'GO' button (Cooldown).")
                    # 找到了盒子但没GO，说明CD没好，不用继续找了
                    # 直接跳出循环去设置短CD
                    break 
            
            # 没找到盒子，或者盒子不在当前屏 -> 滑动
            if i < 1:
                self.sidebar.scroll_up()

        # === 3. 没找到或CD中 (分支 B) ===
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_epic_cooldown"))
        self._handle_wait_short()
        return True

    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------
    # 逻辑辅助函数
    # -------------------------------------------------------------------------

    def _perform_recruitment(self, box_pos):
        """执行招募动作"""
        # 1. 点击盒子
        self.device.click(box_pos)
        time.sleep(1.0)
        
        # 2. 点击招募按钮 (固定坐标)
        self.logger.info("Clicking epic recruit button...")
        self.device.click(self.POS_RECRUIT_BTN)
        time.sleep(1.5)
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_recruit_epic_success"))
        
        # 3. 回主页
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        # 4. 设置下次运行: 48小时
        wait_seconds = 48 * 3600
        self.logger.info(f"Epic recruit done. Waiting 48 hours.")
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)

    def _handle_wait_short(self):
        """处理等待逻辑: 20分钟"""
        wait_seconds = 20 * 60
        self.logger.info(f"Not available. Retry in 20 mins.")
        
        self.sidebar.collapse()
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)

==================================================
FILE: tasks\daily\stamina_control.py
==================================================
import time
import cv2
import numpy as np
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from airtest.core.api import Template

from tasks.combat.combat_task import CombatTask
from tasks.combat.wolf import WolfTask
from game.map_searcher import MapSearcher
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class StaminaControlTask(CombatTask):
    # =========================================================================
    # 1. UI 配置定义
    # =========================================================================
    config_schema = [
        {"key": "enable", "type": "checkbox", "default": False, "menu": "Daily"},
        {"key": "dump_method", "default": "beast"},
        {"key": "dump_level", "default": 25},
        {"key": "dump_one_soldier", "default": True},
        {"key": "reserve_stamina", "default": 190},
        {"key": "hero_mode", "default": "diana_fahd"}
    ]

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "StaminaControlTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 30 

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        
        # --- 资源加载 ---
        self.IMG_PLUS = Template(r"assets/images/stamina/plus.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_PAGE_TITLE = Template(r"assets/images/stamina/stamina.png", threshold=0.9)
        self.IMG_CHECK = Template(r"assets/images/stamina/check.png", threshold=0.9, rgb=True)
        self.IMG_BTN_BLUE = Template(r"assets/images/btn/btn_blue.png", threshold=0.9)

        # --- 常量定义 ---
        self.CLAIM_THRESHOLD = 70  
        self.PANIC_MINUTES = 15    
        
        # --- 时间参数 (严格 Mermaid B) ---
        self.WAIT_BEAST = 180    # 打野回城快: 3分钟
        self.WAIT_RALLY = 480    # 集结5分钟+回城: 8分钟
        self.WAIT_NO_QUEUE = 300 # 等待队列回城: 5分钟

    # =========================================================================
    # 2. 任务主入口 (严格遵循 Mermaid B 流程)
    # =========================================================================
    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(f"=== {get_text('task_staminacontroltask')} Start (Step-by-Step Mode) ===")

        # [节点: Update_Start] 动作: OCR 更新当前体力
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_action_update"))
        if not self.update_stamina():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_ocr_fail"))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            return False
        
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)

        # [节点: Init] 获取当前时间窗口信息
        current_tag, seconds_left, is_window_active = self._get_current_period_info()
        minutes_left = seconds_left / 60.0
        
        # [节点: CheckJSON] 本地记录是否已领?
        last_claimed = self.data.get_stat("tasks.StaminaControlTask.last_claimed_tag", "")
        is_claimed_local = (last_claimed == current_tag)

        self.logger.info(f"[Stamina] Tag: {current_tag} | Mins: {int(minutes_left)} | Active: {is_window_active} | Local Claimed: {is_claimed_local}")

        # === 流程分支判定 ===
        
        # 分支 1: 进入 [领奖突击模式] (Mode_Claim)
        # 条件: 窗口期内 AND 本地记录没领
        if is_window_active and not is_claimed_local:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_mode_claim"))
            
            # [节点: NavUI] 进入体力页面
            if not self._enter_stamina_view():
                self.logger.error(get_text("log_stamina_enter_fail"))
                return False # 下次重试
                
            # [节点: CheckImg] 视觉检查 check.png
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_check_visual"))
            if self._check_is_claimed():
                # [节点: WriteJSON] 修正记录
                self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_already_claimed"))
                self._mark_as_claimed(current_tag)
                # 视觉已领 -> 转入 [日常维护模式]
                self.device.ensure_home()
                # 这里不 return，直接往下走去 CheckFull
            else:
                # [节点: CheckImg = 否] 真没领 -> 执行领奖逻辑
                return self._handle_claim_logic(current_stamina, current_tag, minutes_left)

        # 分支 2: 进入 [日常维护模式] (Mode_Maint)
        # 条件: 不在窗口期 OR 本地已领 (代码流转到这里)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_mode_maint"))
        
        # 刷新一下 current_stamina (因为上面可能刚刚发生了领奖或 OCR)
        current_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        
        # [节点: CheckFull] 体力 > 190? (或者用户设置的阈值)
        target = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.reserve_stamina", 190)
        
        if current_stamina > target:
            # [节点: A2] 体力高 -> 单步泄洪
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_high_dump", current_stamina, target))
            return self._execute_single_dump_step()
        else:
            # [节点: A1] 体力不高 -> 计算休息时间
            return self._calc_smart_wait(current_stamina, target, seconds_left)

    # =========================================================================
    # 3. 领奖逻辑分支 (Mode_Claim)
    # =========================================================================
    def _handle_claim_logic(self, current_stamina, current_tag, minutes_left):
        """
        对应 Mermaid B 的 [模式 B: 领奖突击]
        """
        # [节点: CheckPanic] 熔断期 < 15分钟?
        is_panic = minutes_left < self.PANIC_MINUTES
        
        if is_panic:
            # [节点: B1] 熔断 -> 直接领
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_panic", int(minutes_left)))
            return self._perform_claim_sequence(current_tag)
        
        # [节点: CheckStamina] 体力 <= 70?
        if current_stamina <= self.CLAIM_THRESHOLD:
            # [节点: B3] 达标 -> 领奖
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_safe_claim", current_stamina))
            return self._perform_claim_sequence(current_tag)
        else:
            # [节点: B4] 未达标 -> 单步泄洪 (目标是降到70)
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_need_dump", current_stamina, self.CLAIM_THRESHOLD))
            self.device.ensure_home() 
            # 注意：这里调用的是单步泄洪，也就是说，打完一次怪它会退出去休息几分钟再回来
            # 直到体力降到 70 以下，这符合 Mermaid B "打一下动一下" 的逻辑
            return self._execute_single_dump_step()

    def _perform_claim_sequence(self, current_tag):
        """
        对应 Mermaid B 的 [4. 领奖后处理]
        Claim -> Mark -> OCR Update -> CheckFull -> Smart Wait / Dump
        """
        # [节点: Claim_Action]
        self._perform_claim_click()
        
        # [节点: MarkTrue]
        self._mark_as_claimed(current_tag)
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        # [节点: Update_Post] 动作: OCR 再次更新体力
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_action_update"))
        
        if not self.update_stamina():
            # OCR 失败兜底：手动 +120，然后短时间重试
            current = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", current + 120)
            self.set_next_run(seconds=60) 
            return True

        # 读取最新的体力
        new_stamina = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
        target = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.reserve_stamina", 190)
        
        # [节点: CheckFull] 检查是否溢出
        if new_stamina > target:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_high_dump", new_stamina, target))
            # [节点: P2] 立即泄洪
            return self._execute_single_dump_step()
        
        # [核心修复] 如果不需要泄洪，计算智能休眠时间，而不是默认1小时
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_target_hit")) # "体力达标"
        
        # 获取 seconds_left 传给智能等待函数
        _, seconds_left, _ = self._get_current_period_info()
        
        # 转入智能休眠计算 (目标值-当前值)*5
        return self._calc_smart_wait(new_stamina, target, seconds_left)

    # =========================================================================
    # 4. 单步泄洪逻辑 (Dump - Step by Step)
    # =========================================================================
    def _execute_single_dump_step(self):
        """
        对应 Mermaid B 的 [P2 -> CheckQ -> Dump_Method -> Next_Wait]
        执行 **一次** 攻击，然后根据攻击类型设置推迟时间。
        """
        method = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.dump_method", "beast")
        
        # [节点: CheckQ] 检查空闲队列
        self.device.ensure_home()
        idle_queues = self.sidebar.get_idle_march_queues()
        
        if idle_queues <= 0:
            # [节点: Next_Wait_Q] 无队列 -> 设置下次: 5分钟
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_stamina_no_queues"))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=self.WAIT_NO_QUEUE)
            return True # 任务算执行成功（逻辑上的成功），只是在等待

        # 使用标准日志格式
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_dump_start", method, "1 Step")) 
        
        success, actual_cost = False, 0
        
        # 执行对应动作
        if method == "wolf":
            success, actual_cost = self._dump_with_wolf()
        elif method == "beast":
            success, actual_cost = self._dump_with_map_search("beast")
        elif method == "rally":
            success, actual_cost = self._dump_with_map_search("rally") 
        
        # 处理结果与时间设置
        if success:
            # 扣除数据 (更新内存)
            current = self.data.get_stat("stamina", 0)
            self.data.set_stat("stamina", max(0, current - actual_cost))
            self.data.set_stat("sidebar.idle_queues", max(0, idle_queues - 1))
            
            # [节点: Next_Rally / Next_Beast] 根据类型设置等待时间
            if method == "beast":
                wait_time = self.WAIT_BEAST # 3分钟
            else:
                # Wolf 和 Rally 都是集结类，等待时间长
                wait_time = self.WAIT_RALLY # 8分钟
                
            # 这里调用了新的 Key: log_stamina_step_wait
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_step_wait", method, int(wait_time/60)))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_time)
            return True
        else:
            # 失败处理 (保持 60s 重试)
            self.logger.error(get_text("err_dump_failed"))
            self.set_next_run(seconds=60)
            return False

    # =========================================================================
    # 5. 智能休眠计算 (Calc_Next_Wait)
    # =========================================================================
    def _calc_smart_wait(self, current_stamina, target, seconds_to_refresh):
        """
        [节点: Calc_Next_Wait]
        计算逻辑: Min(体力回满时间, 距离下次刷新90分钟前的时间)
        """
        # A. 计算体力回满时间
        # 1点体力 = 5分钟 (300秒)
        needed = target - current_stamina
        if needed <= 0:
            wait_stamina = 300 # 已满，默认检查间隔
        else:
            wait_stamina = needed * 300
            
        # B. 计算距离下次领奖窗口开启的时间
        # 窗口是刷新前90分钟。所以我们要在 (刷新时间 - 90分钟) 的时候醒来
        wait_refresh = seconds_to_refresh - (90 * 60)
        
        if wait_refresh < 0:
            # 如果已经过了窗口期开始点，但我们不需要领奖（因为已经在维护模式了），那这个值就没意义了
            wait_refresh = 999999 

        # 取最小值，但至少睡 5 分钟
        wait_seconds = max(300, min(wait_stamina, wait_refresh))
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_sleep_calc", int(wait_seconds/60)))
        self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
        return True

    # =========================================================================
    # 6. 具体执行动作 (保持不变)
    # =========================================================================

    def _enter_stamina_view(self):
        self.device.ensure_home()
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_action_click_avatar"))
        self.device.click((61, 38))
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        
        if self.device.exists(self.IMG_PLUS):
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_action_click_plus"))
            self.device.click(self.IMG_PLUS)
            self.device.sleep(1.5)
        else:
            self.logger.warning(get_text("err_plus_not_found"))
            return False
            
        return self._is_in_stamina_view()

    def _is_in_stamina_view(self):
        area = (36, 340, 161, 456)
        screen_path = self.device.snapshot()
        if not screen_path: return False
        
        img = cv2.imread(screen_path)
        if img is None: return False
        
        crop = img[area[1]:area[3], area[0]:area[2]]
        res = self.IMG_PAGE_TITLE.match_in(crop)
        return (res is not None)

    def _check_is_claimed(self):
        return self.device.exists(self.IMG_CHECK)

    def _perform_claim_click(self):
        # 纯点击动作，不含逻辑
        if not self._is_in_stamina_view():
            if not self._enter_stamina_view():
                return False
        
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_action_claiming"))
        target_pos = (602, 407)
        
        self.device.click(target_pos)
        time.sleep(0.4)
        self.device.click(target_pos)
        self.device.sleep(2.0)
        return True

    def _mark_as_claimed(self, tag):
        self.data.set_stat("tasks.StaminaControlTask.last_claimed_tag", tag)
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_stamina_mark_saved", tag))

    def _get_current_period_info(self):
        now_utc = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
        refresh_00 = now_utc.replace(hour=0, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
        refresh_12 = now_utc.replace(hour=12, minute=0, second=0, microsecond=0)
        refresh_24 = refresh_00 + timedelta(days=1)
        
        if now_utc < refresh_12:
            current_tag = now_utc.strftime("%Y%m%d_00")
            target_refresh = refresh_12
        else:
            current_tag = now_utc.strftime("%Y%m%d_12")
            target_refresh = refresh_24
            
        seconds_left = (target_refresh - now_utc).total_seconds()
        # minutes_left = seconds_left / 60
        is_window_active = (seconds_left <= 90 * 60) # 90分钟
        
        return current_tag, seconds_left, is_window_active

    def _perform_hero_selection(self):
        """通用选将逻辑"""
        hero_mode = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.hero_mode", "diana_fahd")
        targets = []
        if hero_mode == "diana": targets = ["diana"]
        elif hero_mode == "diana_fahd": targets = ["diana", "fahd"]
        
        if targets:
            try:
                self.select_heroes(targets)
            except Exception as e:
                self.logger.warning(get_text("err_hero_select", e))
                return False
        return True

    def _perform_attack(self):
        """点击蓝色按钮出击并验证"""
        self.device.click(self.IMG_BTN_BLUE)
        self.device.sleep(1.0)
        return self.verify_attack_success()

    def _dump_with_wolf(self):
        """Wolf 流程: 找狼 -> 选将 -> 设兵 -> 计算 -> 出击"""
        wolf_task = WolfTask(self.device, self.data)
        if not wolf_task.find_and_click_wolf():
            return False, 0
            
        try:
            self.verify_combat_page()
        except:
            return False, 0
            
        if not self._perform_hero_selection():
            return False, 0
            
        use_one_soldier = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.dump_one_soldier", True)
        if use_one_soldier:
            self.set_one_soldier(True)
        else:
            self.set_one_soldier(False)
            
        real_cost = self.get_realtime_cost(mode="rally")
            
        if self._perform_attack():
            return True, real_cost
        else:
            return False, 0

    def _dump_with_map_search(self, mode):
        """Beast/Rally 流程: 搜怪 -> 选将 -> [新增:强制均兵] -> 计算 -> 出击"""
        level = self.data.get_cfg("modules.staminacontroltask.dump_level", 25)
        searcher = MapSearcher(self.device, self.data)
        search_key = "terror" if mode == "rally" else "beast"
        
        # 1. 搜索
        if not searcher.run(search_key, level):
            return False, 0
            
        # 2. 验证页面
        try:
            self.verify_combat_page()
        except:
            return False, 0
            
        # 3. 选将
        self._perform_hero_selection()
        
        # === [新增] 4. 强制均兵 (Equal) ===
        # 无论之前配置如何，打野和集结必须带兵，不能是1兵
        # set_one_soldier(False) 的内部实现就是点击 "Equal" 按钮
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_set_avg_soldier"))
        self.set_one_soldier(False) 
        # ================================

        # 5. 计算实时消耗
        cost_mode = "rally" if mode == "rally" else "wild"
        real_cost = self.get_realtime_cost(mode=cost_mode)
            
        # 6. 出击
        if self._perform_attack():
            return True, real_cost
        else:
            return False, 0

==================================================

from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from game.troop_manager import TroopManager, TroopType, TrainMode
from i18n.i18n import get_text
from datetime import datetime, timedelta
import time

class TroopTrainingTask(BaseTask):
    
    # =========================================================================
    # UI 配置定义
    # =========================================================================
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable",
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        },
        # --- 1. 步兵 (Infantry) ---
        {"key": "sep_infantry", "type": "separator", "label": "lbl_infantry_settings"},
        {"key": "infantry_enable", "type": "checkbox", "label": "lbl_enable_troop", "default": False},
        {"key": "infantry_max_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_max_unlock_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 10},
        {
            "key": "infantry_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_train_mode",
            "options": [
                {"value": "train", "label": "opt_mode_train_new"},
                {"value": "promote", "label": "opt_mode_promote"}
            ],
            "default": "train"
        },
        {"key": "infantry_target_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_operate_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 9},

        # --- 2. 骑兵 (Lancer) ---
        {"key": "sep_lancer", "type": "separator", "label": "lbl_lancer_settings"},
        {"key": "lancer_enable", "type": "checkbox", "label": "lbl_enable_troop", "default": False},
        {"key": "lancer_max_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_max_unlock_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 10},
        {
            "key": "lancer_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_train_mode",
            "options": [
                {"value": "train", "label": "opt_mode_train_new"},
                {"value": "promote", "label": "opt_mode_promote"}
            ],
            "default": "train"
        },
        {"key": "lancer_target_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_operate_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 9},

        # --- 3. 弓兵 (Marksman) ---
        {"key": "sep_marksman", "type": "separator", "label": "lbl_marksman_settings"},
        {"key": "marksman_enable", "type": "checkbox", "label": "lbl_enable_troop", "default": False},
        {"key": "marksman_max_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_max_unlock_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 10},
        {
            "key": "marksman_mode",
            "type": "combobox",
            "label": "cfg_train_mode",
            "options": [
                {"value": "train", "label": "opt_mode_train_new"},
                {"value": "promote", "label": "opt_mode_promote"}
            ],
            "default": "train"
        },
        {"key": "marksman_target_level", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_operate_level", "min": 1, "max": 10, "default": 9},

        # --- 4. 时间设置 ---
        {"key": "sep_time", "type": "separator", "label": "lbl_time_settings"},
        {"key": "train_time_h", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_train_time_h", "min": 0, "max": 24, "default": 3},
        {"key": "train_time_m", "type": "spinbox", "label": "cfg_train_time_m", "min": 0, "max": 59, "default": 0}
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        self.manager = TroopManager(device, data)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "TroopTrainingTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 55

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 3600

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("task_trooptrainingtask") + " Started")
        
        # 1. 尝试进入兵营
        self.device.ensure_home()
        
        if not self.manager.enter_barracks():
            self.logger.warning("Entry failed. Postponing task.")
            self.sidebar.collapse()
            self.device.ensure_home()
            self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
            return False

        # 2. 任务定义
        tasks = [
            (TroopType.INFANTRY, "infantry"),
            (TroopType.CAVALRY,  "lancer"),
            (TroopType.ARCHER,   "marksman")
        ]
        
        # 获取用户设置的训练时长
        user_h = self.data.get_cfg("modules.trooptrainingtask.train_time_h", 3)
        user_m = self.data.get_cfg("modules.trooptrainingtask.train_time_m", 0)
        user_duration_sec = user_h * 3600 + user_m * 60
        if user_duration_sec < 300: user_duration_sec = 300 

        # 3. 循环处理
        for t_type, prefix in tasks:
            # A. 检查启用
            is_enabled = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.trooptrainingtask.{prefix}_enable", False)
            if not is_enabled:
                continue

            # B. 切换页签
            if not self.manager.switch_to_tab(t_type):
                self.logger.warning(f"Failed to switch to {t_type}")
                continue

            # C. 检查状态
            is_busy_initially = (self.manager.check_state() == "busy")
            training_just_started = False
            
            if is_busy_initially:
                self.logger.info(f"{t_type} is busy (Training in progress).")
                # 忙碌状态下，不更新时间戳，沿用上次记录
            else:
                # D. 空闲 -> 执行流程
                mode = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.trooptrainingtask.{prefix}_mode", "train")
                
                # 注意：target_level 是多义词。Train模式是目标等级，Promote模式是来源等级。
                level = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.trooptrainingtask.{prefix}_target_level", 9)
                
                # E. 选兵
                if not self.manager.select_tier(level):
                    continue
                    
                # F. 执行
                result = False
                if mode == "train":
                    result = self.manager.execute_train_action()
                else:
                    # === 晋升模式 ===
                    code = self.manager.execute_promote_action()
                    
                    if code == "NO_PROMOTABLE":
                        # === [核心功能] 自动降级逻辑 ===
                        self.logger.info(f"No promotable troops. Fallback to Train New.")
                        
                        # 1. 获取 Fallback 等级 (Max - 1)
                        max_level = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.trooptrainingtask.{prefix}_max_level", 10)
                        fallback_level = max(1, max_level - 1)
                        
                        self.logger.info(f"Fallback: Selecting T{fallback_level} to train.")
                        
                        # 2. 重新选兵
                        if self.manager.select_tier(fallback_level):
                            # 3. 执行普通训练
                            result = self.manager.execute_train_action()
                            if result:
                                self.logger.info(f"Fallback training success.")
                        else:
                            self.logger.warning("Fallback selection failed.")
                            
                    else:
                        result = code

                if result:
                    self.logger.info(f"{t_type} action started.")
                    training_just_started = True

            # === 更新时间戳 ===
            # 无论是正常的训练/晋升，还是降级后的训练，只要成功了，都更新时间
            if training_just_started:
                finish_time = datetime.now() + timedelta(seconds=user_duration_sec)
                self._save_finish_time(prefix, finish_time)

        # 4. 退出
        self.manager.exit_barracks()

        # 5. 调度计算
        self._schedule_next_run(tasks)
        
        return True

    def _save_finish_time(self, prefix, dt_obj):
        """保存预计结束时间到 statistics"""
        ts_str = dt_obj.strftime("%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        self.data.set_stat(f"tasks.TroopTrainingTask.{prefix}_finish_time", ts_str)

    def _get_finish_time(self, prefix):
        """读取预计结束时间"""
        ts_str = self.data.get_stat(f"tasks.TroopTrainingTask.{prefix}_finish_time", "")
        if not ts_str:
            return None
        try:
            return datetime.strptime(ts_str, "%Y-%m-%d %H:%M:%S")
        except:
            return None

    def _schedule_next_run(self, tasks):
        """
        同步调度策略：
        1. 盲区检测：如果有兵种在忙但没记录时间，只睡 10 分钟。
        2. 节奏对齐：找出【最晚结束】的那个时间点，大家一起等它。
        """
        now = datetime.now()
        future_finish_times = []
        
        enabled_count = 0
        valid_future_record_count = 0

        for _, prefix in tasks:
            is_enabled = self.data.get_cfg(f"modules.trooptrainingtask.{prefix}_enable", False)
            if not is_enabled:
                continue
            
            enabled_count += 1
            f_time = self._get_finish_time(prefix)
            
            # 只有当记录的时间确实在未来（说明还在练），才纳入计算
            if f_time and f_time > now:
                future_finish_times.append(f_time)
                valid_future_record_count += 1
            
        # === 核心调度逻辑 ===
        
        if enabled_count == 0:
            # 没启用任何兵种，睡默认时间
            self.set_next_run(seconds=3600)
            return

        # 1. 盲区检测 (Blind Spot)
        # 如果 有效记录数 < 启用数
        # 意味着：有兵种是 Busy 状态，但我们不知道它具体啥时候结束
        # 必须短睡重试，试图捕获它的空闲瞬间
        if valid_future_record_count < enabled_count:
            self.logger.info("Sync pending: Waiting for unknown status troops. Checking in 10 mins.")
            self.set_next_run(seconds=600)
            return

        # 2. 同步对齐计算 (Sync Alignment - Max Strategy)
        if future_finish_times:
            # 取最大值：等最慢的那个练完
            target_time = max(future_finish_times)
            
            wait_seconds = (target_time - now).total_seconds()
            
            # 加上 10 分钟缓冲，确保那个最慢的也肯定好了
            wait_seconds += 600 
            
            # 最小安全间隔 (5分钟)
            wait_seconds = max(300, wait_seconds)
            
            wait_h = int(wait_seconds // 3600)
            wait_m = int((wait_seconds % 3600) // 60)
            self.logger.info(f"Sync Strategy: Aligning to latest troop. Sleep {wait_h}h {wait_m}m.")
            self.set_next_run(seconds=wait_seconds)
        else:
            # 所有兵营都闲着，或者记录的时间都在过去
            # 正常情况下 run_impl 会处理并更新，这里兜底
            self.set_next_run(seconds=600)

import time
from airtest.core.api import Template
from tasks.base_task import BaseTask
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class WarehouseTask(BaseTask):
    # UI 配置
    config_schema = [
        {
            "key": "enable",
            "type": "checkbox",
            "label": "lbl_enable", 
            "default": False,
            "menu": "Daily"
        }
    ]

    def __init__(self, device, data):
        super().__init__(device, data)
        # 资源定义
        self.IMG_GIFT = Template(r"assets/images/sidebar/gift.png", threshold=0.9)

    @property
    def name(self):
        return "WarehouseTask"

    @property
    def priority(self):
        return 10

    @property
    def default_interval(self):
        return 300 

    def run_impl(self):
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_warehouse_start"))
        
        # 1. 导航
        if not self.sidebar.switch_to_affairs_page():
            self.logger.warning(get_text("log_warehouse_nav_fail"))
            self._finish_and_wait(collapse=True) # 失败了还是要收起的
            return False

        # 2. 初始扫描
        self.logger.info("Scanning initial view...")
        if self._check_and_claim():
            return True

        # 3. 循环滑动查找
        for i in range(2):
            self.logger.info(f"Scrolling down ({i+1}/2)...")
            self.sidebar.scroll_up()
            if self._check_and_claim():
                return True

        # 4. 彻底没找到
        self.logger.info(get_text("log_warehouse_gift_not_found"))
        self._finish_and_wait(collapse=True) # 没找到也收起，保持整洁
        return True

    def _check_and_claim(self):
        """
        辅助方法：检查屏幕上是否有礼物并领取
        """
        pos = self.device.exists(self.IMG_GIFT)
        if pos:
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_warehouse_gift_found"))
            
            # 双击领取
            self.device.click(pos)
            time.sleep(0.4) 
            self.device.click(pos)
            
            self.logger.info(get_text("log_warehouse_claim_success"))
            
            # [修改] 成功领取后，传入 collapse=False，不收起侧边栏
            self._finish_and_wait(collapse=False)
            return True
        return False

    def _finish_and_wait(self, collapse=True):
        """
        统一的结束逻辑
        :param collapse: 是否收起侧边栏
        """
        self.set_next_run(seconds=300)
        
        if collapse:
            self.sidebar.collapse()
        else:
            # 如果不收起，也建议 Ensure Home 吗？
            # 不，根据你的要求 "成功就不需要 collapse"，隐含的意思是保留当前状态即可
            # 或者为了保险起见，可以只点一下主页键确保不在弹窗里，但不强制收侧边栏
            # 这里我们什么都不做，保留当前状态
            pass

# tools/resource_loader.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
import cv2
import numpy as np
from airtest.core.api import Template as AirtestTemplate
from cryptography.fernet import Fernet

# ==============================================================================
# 🔑 解密密钥配置 (必须与加密时的密钥完全一致！)
# ==============================================================================
# 请把 encrypt_tool.py 生成的 b'xxx...' 完整填入下面
# 示例: DECRYPTION_KEY = b'A1b2C3d4E5f6G7h8I9j0K1l2M3n4O5p6Q7r8S9t0U1v='
DECRYPTION_KEY = b'gMN3RKaTDbu38pLlA3yah7dZYD8XhxBOWeAtY5eNo64=' 

# 初始化解密器
try:
    cipher = Fernet(DECRYPTION_KEY)
except Exception as e:
    print(f"[ResourceLoader] ⚠️ 密钥格式错误或未配置: {e}")
    cipher = None

class MemoryTemplate(AirtestTemplate):
    """
    安全模版类：
    1. 接收加密的二进制流
    2. 内存解密 (AES)
    3. 内存解码 (OpenCV)
    4. 不落地硬盘
    """
    def __init__(self, encrypted_bytes, threshold=None, target_pos=5, record_pos=None, resolution=(), **kwargs):
        self._img_content = None
        
        if encrypted_bytes and cipher:
            try:
                # --- 第1步：解密 ---
                # 如果这里报错，说明密钥不对，或者文件本身没有加密
                decrypted_bytes = cipher.decrypt(encrypted_bytes)
                
                # --- 第2步：解码图片 ---
                # 将二进制流解码为 OpenCV 图像格式
                nparr = np.frombuffer(decrypted_bytes, np.uint8)
                self._img_content = cv2.imdecode(nparr, cv2.IMREAD_UNCHANGED)
                
                if self._img_content is not None:
                    # [可选] 调试时打印，发布时可注释掉
                    # print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ✅ 图片解密加载成功! 尺寸: {self._img_content.shape}")
                    pass
                else:
                    print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 解密成功但图片解码失败 (数据损坏?)")

            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 加载失败: {e}")
                # 常见原因：密钥不匹配，或者KeyAuth填的是未加密的直链
        
        elif not cipher:
            print("[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 致命错误：解密密钥未配置！")
        else:
            print("[MemoryTemplate] ❌ 错误：接收到的数据为空！")

        # 欺骗父类构造函数，传入虚拟路径防止报错
        super().__init__(filename="MEMORY_ASSET", threshold=threshold, target_pos=target_pos, record_pos=record_pos, resolution=resolution, **kwargs)

    def _imread(self):
        # 核心：重写读取方法，直接返回内存中的 OpenCV 图片对象
        return self._img_content

class ResourceLoader:
    _instance = None
    _keyauth_app = None
    _cache = {}  # 缓存下载好的加密数据，避免重复下载

    def __new__(cls):
        if cls._instance is None:
            cls._instance = super(ResourceLoader, cls).__new__(cls)
        return cls._instance

    def initialize(self, keyauth_app):
        """注入登录成功的 keyauth 实例"""
        self._keyauth_app = keyauth_app

    def get_template(self, file_id, threshold=0.9, record_pos=None, resolution=()):
        """
        获取安全模版：先查缓存，没有则下载
        """
        if not self._keyauth_app:
            print("[ResourceLoader] KeyAuth not initialized!")
            return None

        # 1. 检查缓存 (缓存里存的是加密的 bytes)
        if file_id in self._cache:
            # print(f"[ResourceLoader] 使用缓存 ID: {file_id}")
            encrypted_bytes = self._cache[file_id]
        else:
            # 2. 下载
            print(f"[ResourceLoader] 正在从云端下载资源 ID: {file_id} ...")
            try:
                # KeyAuth 的 file 方法会自动处理 Direct Link 下载
                encrypted_bytes = self._keyauth_app.file(file_id)
                
                # 简单的有效性检查
                if not encrypted_bytes or len(encrypted_bytes) < 100:
                    print(f"[ResourceLoader] ⚠️ 下载数据异常，长度仅 {len(encrypted_bytes) if encrypted_bytes else 0} bytes")
                    # 可能是 404 或 401 错误文本
                    return None
                    
                self._cache[file_id] = encrypted_bytes
            except Exception as e:
                print(f"[ResourceLoader] Download Exception: {e}")
                return None

        # 3. 创建并返回内存模版 (解密过程在 MemoryTemplate 内部发生)
        return MemoryTemplate(encrypted_bytes, threshold=threshold, record_pos=record_pos, resolution=resolution)

FILE: tools\keyauth\keyauth.py

import os
import json as jsond  # json
import time  # sleep before exit
import binascii  # hex encoding
import platform  # check platform
import subprocess  # needed for mac device
import qrcode
from datetime import datetime, timezone, timedelta
from discord_interactions import verify_key # used for signature verification
from PIL import Image


try:
    if os.name == 'nt':
        import win32security  # get sid (WIN only)
    import requests  # https requests
except ModuleNotFoundError:
    print("Exception when importing modules")
    print("Installing necessary modules....")
    if os.path.isfile("requirements.txt"):
        os.system("pip install -r requirements.txt")
    else:
        if os.name == 'nt':
            os.system("pip install pywin32")
        os.system("pip install requests")
    print("Modules installed!")
    time.sleep(1.5)
    os._exit(1)


class api:

    name = ownerid = version = hash_to_check = ""

    def __init__(self, name, ownerid, version, hash_to_check):
        if len(ownerid) != 10:
            print("Visit https://keyauth.cc/app/, copy Pthon code, and replace code in main.py with that")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
    
        self.name = name

        self.ownerid = ownerid

        self.version = version
        self.hash_to_check = hash_to_check
        self.init()

    sessionid = enckey = ""
    initialized = False

    def init(self):
        if self.sessionid != "":
            print("You've already initialized!")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "init",
            "ver": self.version,
            "hash": self.hash_to_check,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid
        }

        response = self.__do_request(post_data)

        if response == "KeyAuth_Invalid":
            print("The application doesn't exist")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

        json = jsond.loads(response)

        if json["message"] == "invalidver":
            if json["download"] != "":
                print("New Version Available")
                download_link = json["download"]
                os.system(f"start {download_link}")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)
            else:
                print("Invalid Version, Contact owner to add download link to latest app version")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

        if not json["success"]:
            print(json["message"])
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

        self.sessionid = json["sessionid"]
        self.initialized = True

    def register(self, user, password, license, hwid=None):
        self.checkinit()
        if hwid is None:
            hwid = others.get_hwid()

        post_data = {
            "type": "register",
            "username": user,
            "pass": password,
            "key": license,
            "hwid": hwid,
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid
        }

        response = self.__do_request(post_data)

        json = jsond.loads(response)

        if json["success"]:
            print(json["message"])
            self.__load_user_data(json["info"])
        else:
            print(json["message"])
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)

    def upgrade(self, user, license):

    def enable2fa(self, code=None):
        self.checkinit()
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "2faenable",
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid,
            "code": code
        }       
        
        response = self.__do_request(post_data)
        
        json = jsond.loads(response)
        
        if json["success"]:
            if code is None:
                # First request: Display the 2FA secret code
                print(f"Your 2FA secret code is: {json['2fa']['secret_code']}")
                qr_code = json['2fa']['QRCode']
                self.display_qr_code(qr_code)
                code_input = input("Enter the 6 digit 2fa code to enable 2fa: ")
                self.enable2fa(code_input);
            else:
                # Second request: Confirm successful 2FA activation
                print("2FA has been successfully enabled!")
                time.sleep(3)
        else:
            print(f"Error: {json['message']}")
            time.sleep(3)
            os._exit(1)
            
    def disable2fa(self, code=None):
        self.checkinit()
        
        code = input("Enter the 6 digit 2fa code to disable 2fa: ")
        
        post_data = {
            "type": "2fadisable",
            "sessionid": self.sessionid,
            "name": self.name,
            "ownerid": self.ownerid,
            "code": code
        }
        
        response = self.__do_request(post_data)
        
        json = jsond.loads(response)
        
        print(json['message'])
        time.sleep(3)
        
            
    def display_qr_code(self, qr_code_url):
            # Generate QR code image
            qr = qrcode.QRCode(
                version=1,
                error_correction=qrcode.constants.ERROR_CORRECT_L,
                box_size=10,
                border=4,
            )

            # Add the QR code URL data
            qr.add_data(qr_code_url)
            qr.make(fit=True)

            # Create an image from the QR code
            img = qr.make_image(fill='black', back_color='white')

            # Display the QR code image
            img.show()            
            
    def __do_request(self, post_data):
        try:
            response = requests.post(
                "https://keyauth.win/api/1.3/", data=post_data, timeout=10
            )

            if post_data["type"] == "log" or post_data["type"] == "file" or post_data["type"] == "2faenable" or post_data["type"] == "2fadisable":
                return response.text

            # Get the signature and timestamp from the headers
            signature = response.headers.get("x-signature-ed25519")
            timestamp = response.headers.get("x-signature-timestamp")

            if not signature or not timestamp:
                print("Missing headers for signature verification.")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            server_time = datetime.fromtimestamp(int(timestamp), timezone.utc)
            current_time = datetime.now(timezone.utc)
            
            #print(f"Server Timestamp (UTC seconds): {timestamp}")
            #print(f"Server Time (UTC seconds): {server_time.timestamp()}")
            #print(f"Current Time (UTC seconds): {current_time.timestamp()}")

            buffer_seconds = 5
            time_difference = current_time - server_time

            if time_difference > timedelta(seconds=20 + buffer_seconds):
                print("Timestamp is too old (exceeded 20 seconds + buffer).")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            if not verify_key(response.text.encode('utf-8'), signature, timestamp, '5586b4bc69c7a4b487e4563a4cd96afd39140f919bd31cea7d1c6a1e8439422b'):
                print("Signature checksum failed. Request was tampered with or session ended most likely.")
                time.sleep(3)
                os._exit(1)

            return response.text

        except requests.exceptions.Timeout: 
            print("Request timed out. Server is probably down/slow at the moment")
                
            
    class application_data_class:
        numUsers = numKeys = app_ver = customer_panel = onlineUsers = ""

    class user_data_class:
        username = ip = hwid = expires = createdate = lastlogin = subscription = subscriptions = ""

    user_data = user_data_class()
    app_data = application_data_class()

    def __load_app_data(self, data):
        self.app_data.numUsers = data["numUsers"]
        self.app_data.numKeys = data["numKeys"]
        self.app_data.app_ver = data["version"]
        self.app_data.customer_panel = data["customerPanelLink"]
        self.app_data.onlineUsers = data["numOnlineUsers"]

    def __load_user_data(self, data):
        self.user_data.username = data["username"]
        self.user_data.ip = data["ip"]
        self.user_data.hwid = data["hwid"] or "N/A"
        self.user_data.expires = data["subscriptions"][0]["expiry"]
        self.user_data.createdate = data["createdate"]
        self.user_data.lastlogin = data["lastlogin"]
        self.user_data.subscription = data["subscriptions"][0]["subscription"]
        self.user_data.subscriptions = data["subscriptions"]


class others:
    @staticmethod
    def get_hwid():
        if platform.system() == "Linux":
            with open("/etc/machine-id") as f:
                hwid = f.read()
                return hwid
        elif platform.system() == 'Windows':
            winuser = os.getlogin()
            sid = win32security.LookupAccountName(None, winuser)[0]  # You can also use WMIC (better than SID, some users had problems with WMIC)
            hwid = win32security.ConvertSidToStringSid(sid)
            return hwid
            '''
            cmd = subprocess.Popen(
                "wmic useraccount where name='%username%' get sid",
                stdout=subprocess.PIPE,
                shell=True,
            )

            (suppost_sid, error) = cmd.communicate()

            suppost_sid = suppost_sid.split(b"\n")[1].strip()

            return suppost_sid.decode()

            ^^ HOW TO DO IT USING WMIC
            '''
        elif platform.system() == 'Darwin':
            output = subprocess.Popen("ioreg -l | grep IOPlatformSerialNumber", stdout=subprocess.PIPE, shell=True).communicate()[0]
            serial = output.decode().split('=', 1)[1].replace(' ', '')
            hwid = serial[1:-2]
            return hwid

from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QLabel,
    QLineEdit, QComboBox, QPushButton, QMessageBox, QFormLayout, QSpinBox, QAbstractSpinBox
)
from airtest.core.android.adb import ADB
import os
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class AddInstanceDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(get_text("title_add_instance"))
        self.resize(400, 200)
        self.setup_ui()
        self.refresh_devices()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        form = QFormLayout()
        
        # 实例名称
        self.line_name = QLineEdit()
        self.line_name.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_instance_name"))
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_name"), self.line_name)

        # 设备序列号
        hbox_serial = QHBoxLayout()
        self.combo_serial = QComboBox()
        self.combo_serial.setEditable(True)
        self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_serial"))
        
        btn_refresh = QPushButton("🔄")
        btn_refresh.setToolTip(get_text("btn_refresh_adb"))
        btn_refresh.setFixedWidth(40)
        btn_refresh.clicked.connect(self.refresh_devices)
        
        hbox_serial.addWidget(self.combo_serial)
        hbox_serial.addWidget(btn_refresh)
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_address"), hbox_serial)

        # Diana 等级
        self.spin_diana = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_diana.setRange(0, 5)
        self.spin_diana.setValue(0)
        self.spin_diana.setSuffix(" Lv")
        self.spin_diana.setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox.NoButtons)
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_diana"), self.spin_diana)

        # 行军队列
        self.spin_march = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_march.setRange(1, 6)
        self.spin_march.setValue(5)
        self.spin_march.setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox.NoButtons)
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_march_slots"), self.spin_march)
        
        layout.addLayout(form)

        # 按钮
        btn_layout = QHBoxLayout()
        btn_ok = QPushButton(get_text("btn_confirm"))
        btn_ok.clicked.connect(self.accept)
        btn_cancel = QPushButton(get_text("btn_cancel_simple"))
        btn_cancel.clicked.connect(self.reject)
        
        btn_layout.addStretch()
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_ok)
        btn_layout.addWidget(btn_cancel)
        layout.addLayout(btn_layout)

    def refresh_devices(self):
        self.combo_serial.clear()
        try:
            # 1. 获取配置的 ADB 路径
            from config import global_config
            adb_path = global_config.get("adb_path", "")
            use_internal = global_config.get("use_internal_adb", True)
            
            # 如果使用内置，尝试构建路径 (这里假设在当前目录下有 adb/adb.exe)
            if use_internal:
                # 简单的内置路径推断，实际可能需要更严谨的判断
                if os.path.exists("adb/adb.exe"):
                    adb_path = os.path.abspath("adb/adb.exe")
                else:
                    # 如果没找到内置的，就让 Airtest 自己去找
                    adb_path = None
            
            # 2. 初始化 ADB
            if adb_path and os.path.exists(adb_path):
                adb = ADB(adb_path=adb_path)
            else:
                adb = ADB() # 尝试自动查找

            # 3. 获取设备列表
            devices = adb.devices() # returns list of (serial, status)
            
            count = 0
            for serial, status in devices:
                if status == 'device':
                    self.combo_serial.addItem(serial)
                    count += 1
            
            if count == 0:
                self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_no_device"))
            else:
                self.combo_serial.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_select_device"))
                self.combo_serial.showPopup() # 自动弹出列表方便选择

        except Exception as e:
            print(f"Error refreshing devices: {e}")
            QMessageBox.warning(self, get_text("msg_adb_error"), get_text("msg_get_device_fail", e))

    def get_data(self):
        return (
            self.line_name.text().strip(),
            self.combo_serial.currentText().strip(),
            self.spin_diana.value(),
            self.spin_march.value()
        )

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtCore import Slot, Signal, Qt, QPropertyAnimation, QEasingCurve, QParallelAnimationGroup, QAbstractAnimation, QSize, QEvent
from PySide6.QtWidgets import QWidget, QToolButton, QVBoxLayout, QHBoxLayout, QFrame, QScrollArea, QSizePolicy, QCheckBox, QLabel

class AutoHeightScrollArea(QScrollArea):
    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setSizePolicy(QSizePolicy.Expanding, QSizePolicy.Fixed)
        self.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        self.setVerticalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setHorizontalScrollBarPolicy(Qt.ScrollBarAlwaysOff)
        self.setWidgetResizable(True)

    def eventFilter(self, o, e):
        if o == self.widget() and e.type() == QEvent.Resize:
            self.updateGeometry()
        return super().eventFilter(o, e)

    def setWidget(self, widget):
        super().setWidget(widget)
        if widget:
            widget.installEventFilter(self)

    def sizeHint(self):
        if self.widget():
            # Add a small buffer to prevent cutting off content
            h = self.widget().sizeHint().height() + 5
            if self.maximumHeight() < 16777215:
                h = min(h, self.maximumHeight())
            return QSize(super().sizeHint().width(), h)
        return QSize(0, 0)

class CollapsibleBox(QWidget):
    # Signal when the content visibility toggles (expanded/collapsed)
    toggled_state = Signal(bool)
    # Signal when the checkbox is toggled (enabled/disabled)
    toggled_enable = Signal(bool)
    # Signal when the size of the box changes (e.g. after animation)
    size_changed = Signal()

    def __init__(self, title="", parent=None, checkable=False):
        super().__init__(parent)

        self.is_checkable = checkable
        
        # --- Header ---
        self.header_widget = QWidget()
        self.header_layout = QHBoxLayout(self.header_widget)
        self.header_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.header_layout.setSpacing(5)

        # 1. Arrow Button
        self.toggle_button = QToolButton()
        self.toggle_button.setStyleSheet("QToolButton { border: none; background: transparent; }")
        self.toggle_button.setToolButtonStyle(Qt.ToolButtonIconOnly)
        self.toggle_button.setArrowType(Qt.RightArrow)
        self.toggle_button.setCheckable(True)
        self.toggle_button.setChecked(False)
        self.toggle_button.released.connect(self.on_toggle)
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.toggle_button)

        # 2. Checkbox (Optional)
        self.checkbox = None
        if checkable:
            self.checkbox = QCheckBox()
            self.checkbox.toggled.connect(self.on_checkbox_toggled)
            self.header_layout.addWidget(self.checkbox)

        # 3. Title Label
        self.title_label = QLabel(title)
        self.title_label.mouseReleaseEvent = self.on_title_clicked
        self.header_layout.addWidget(self.title_label)
        
        self.header_layout.addStretch()

        # --- Content ---
        self.toggle_animation = QParallelAnimationGroup(self)
        self.toggle_animation.finished.connect(self.on_animation_finished)

        self.content_area = AutoHeightScrollArea()
        self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(0)
        self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(0)
        self.content_area.setVisible(False)

        # --- Main Layout ---
        lay = QVBoxLayout(self)
        lay.setSpacing(0)
        lay.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        lay.addWidget(self.header_widget)
        lay.addWidget(self.content_area)

        self.anim_max = QPropertyAnimation(self.content_area, b"maximumHeight")
        self.anim_min = QPropertyAnimation(self.content_area, b"minimumHeight")
        
        self.anim_max.valueChanged.connect(self.on_animation_value_changed)
        self.anim_min.valueChanged.connect(self.on_animation_value_changed)
        
        self.toggle_animation.addAnimation(self.anim_max)
        self.toggle_animation.addAnimation(self.anim_min)

    def on_animation_value_changed(self, value):
        self.content_area.updateGeometry()
        self.updateGeometry()
        self.size_changed.emit()

    def on_title_clicked(self, event):
        if event.button() == Qt.LeftButton:
            # Directly call on_toggle instead of animateClick to ensure consistent behavior
            self.toggle_button.animateClick()

    @Slot()
    def on_toggle(self):
        if self.toggle_animation.state() == QAbstractAnimation.Running:
            # If animating, ignore or handle gracefully.
            self.toggle_button.setChecked(not self.toggle_button.isChecked())
            return

        checked = self.toggle_button.isChecked()
        self.toggle_button.setArrowType(Qt.DownArrow if checked else Qt.RightArrow)
        
        # Always use Forward because we explicitly set Start/End values for both directions
        self.toggle_animation.setDirection(QPropertyAnimation.Forward)

        # Calculate content height
        content_height = 0
        if self.content_area.widget():
            self.content_area.widget().adjustSize()
            content_height = self.content_area.widget().sizeHint().height() + 5

        if checked:
            # Expanding: 0 -> content_height
            self.content_area.setVisible(True)
            self.anim_max.setStartValue(0)
            self.anim_max.setEndValue(content_height)
            self.anim_min.setStartValue(0)
            self.anim_min.setEndValue(content_height)
        else:
            # Collapsing: current -> 0
            # Snap to current height first (in case it was unlocked/MAX)
            current_h = self.content_area.height()
            self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(current_h)
            self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(current_h)
            
            self.anim_max.setStartValue(current_h)
            self.anim_max.setEndValue(0)
            self.anim_min.setStartValue(current_h)
            self.anim_min.setEndValue(0)

        self.toggle_animation.start()
        self.toggled_state.emit(checked)

    @Slot()
    def on_animation_finished(self):
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            # Expanded: Unlock height to allow dynamic resizing of content
            self.content_area.setMaximumHeight(16777215) # QWIDGETSIZE_MAX
            self.content_area.setMinimumHeight(0)
        else:
            self.content_area.setVisible(False)
        
        # Ensure the content area (scroll area) updates its geometry/size hint
        self.content_area.updateGeometry()
        # Trigger layout update
        self.updateGeometry()
        self.size_changed.emit()

    @Slot(bool)
    def on_checkbox_toggled(self, checked):
        self.toggled_enable.emit(checked)

    def setContentLayout(self, layout):
        widget = QWidget()
        widget.setLayout(layout)
        self.content_area.setWidget(widget)
        
        # Connect signals from child CollapsibleBoxes
        self._connect_child_signals(layout)

        # Initial setup
        widget.adjustSize()
        
        self.anim_max.setDuration(300)
        self.anim_max.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.InOutQuad)
        self.anim_min.setDuration(300)
        self.anim_min.setEasingCurve(QEasingCurve.InOutQuad)

    def _connect_child_signals(self, layout):
        for i in range(layout.count()):
            item = layout.itemAt(i)
            if item.widget():
                widget = item.widget()
                if isinstance(widget, CollapsibleBox):
                    widget.size_changed.connect(self.refresh_height)
                elif widget.layout():
                    self._connect_child_signals(widget.layout())
            elif item.layout():
                self._connect_child_signals(item.layout())

    @Slot()
    def refresh_height(self):
        # 如果当前是展开状态，我们需要根据子内容的变化重新调整高度
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            # 1. 获取内部容器
            inner_widget = self.content_area.widget()
            
            if inner_widget:
                # 2. 核心修复：强制激活布局重新计算
                if inner_widget.layout():
                    inner_widget.layout().activate()
                
                # 3. 强制调整大小以适应最新内容 (Shrink to fit)
                inner_widget.adjustSize()
            
            # 4. 更新 ScrollArea 的几何信息
            self.content_area.updateGeometry()
            
            # 5. 更新自身的几何信息
            self.updateGeometry()
            
            # 6. 继续向上传递信号 (如果还有父级折叠框)
            self.size_changed.emit()

    # --- Compatibility Methods ---
    
    def setTitle(self, title):
        self.title_label.setText(title)

    def setCheckable(self, checkable):
        pass 

    def setChecked(self, checked):
        if self.checkbox:
            self.checkbox.setChecked(checked)

    def isChecked(self):
        if self.checkbox:
            return self.checkbox.isChecked()
        return False

    def collapse(self):
        if self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            self.toggle_button.click()

    def expand(self):
        if not self.toggle_button.isChecked():
            self.toggle_button.click()

# ui/edit_instance_dialog.py
# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QDialog, QVBoxLayout, QFormLayout, QLineEdit,
    QDialogButtonBox, QSpinBox, QLabel, QAbstractSpinBox
)
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class EditInstanceDialog(QDialog):
    def __init__(self, parent=None, current_name="", current_serial="", current_diana=0, current_march_slots=5):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.setWindowTitle(get_text("title_edit_instance"))
        self.resize(350, 250)
        
        # 记录原始数据
        self.old_name = current_name
        self.old_serial = current_serial
        self.old_diana = current_diana
        self.old_march_slots = current_march_slots
        
        self.setup_ui()

    def setup_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)

        # 表单区域
        form = QFormLayout()
        
        # 1. 名字
        self.line_name = QLineEdit(self.old_name)
        self.line_name.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_instance_name"))
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_name"), self.line_name)
        
        # 2. 地址
        self.line_serial = QLineEdit(self.old_serial)
        self.line_serial.setPlaceholderText(get_text("ph_serial"))
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_address"), self.line_serial)
        
        # 3. Diana 等级 (新增)
        self.spin_diana = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_diana.setRange(0, 5) # 0表示没有，1-5是等级
        self.spin_diana.setValue(int(self.old_diana))
        self.spin_diana.setSuffix(" Lv")
        self.spin_diana.setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox.NoButtons)
        # 加个小提示
        lbl_tip = QLabel(get_text("tip_diana"))
        lbl_tip.setStyleSheet("color: gray; font-size: 9pt;")
        
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_diana"), self.spin_diana)
        form.addRow("", lbl_tip) # 占位显示提示

        # 4. 行军队列 (新增)
        self.spin_march = QSpinBox()
        self.spin_march.setRange(1, 6)
        self.spin_march.setValue(int(self.old_march_slots))
        self.spin_march.setButtonSymbols(QAbstractSpinBox.NoButtons)
        
        lbl_tip_march = QLabel(get_text("tip_march_slots"))
        lbl_tip_march.setStyleSheet("color: gray; font-size: 9pt;")
        
        form.addRow(get_text("lbl_march_slots"), self.spin_march)
        form.addRow("", lbl_tip_march)
        
        layout.addLayout(form)

        # 按钮区域 (确定/取消)
        buttons = QDialogButtonBox(QDialogButtonBox.Ok | QDialogButtonBox.Cancel)
        buttons.accepted.connect(self.accept)
        buttons.rejected.connect(self.reject)
        layout.addWidget(buttons)

    def get_data(self):
        """返回: (name, serial, diana_level, march_slots)"""
        return (
            self.line_name.text().strip(),
            self.line_serial.text().strip(),
            self.spin_diana.value(),
            self.spin_march.value()
        )

# -*- coding: utf-8 -*-
from PySide6.QtWidgets import (
    QWidget, QVBoxLayout, QCheckBox, 
    QSpinBox, QLabel, QGridLayout, QScrollArea, QFrame, QPushButton
)
from PySide6.QtCore import Qt, QSize
from PySide6.QtGui import QIcon
from game.hero_manager import HeroManager
from i18n.i18n import get_text

class CollapsibleBox(QWidget):
    """自定义折叠控件"""
    def __init__(self, title="", parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.toggle_button = QPushButton(title)
        self.toggle_button.setCheckable(True)
        self.toggle_button.setChecked(False) # 默认收起
        self.toggle_button.setStyleSheet("text-align: left; font-weight: bold; padding: 5px;")
        self.toggle_button.toggled.connect(self.on_toggled)

        self.content_area = QWidget()
        self.content_area.setVisible(False) # 默认隐藏
        
        self.main_layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        self.main_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        self.main_layout.setSpacing(0)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.toggle_button)
        self.main_layout.addWidget(self.content_area)

    def on_toggled(self, checked):
        self.content_area.setVisible(checked)
        arrow = "▼" if checked else "▶"
        self.toggle_button.setText(f"{arrow} {self.toggle_button.text()[2:]}")

    def set_content_layout(self, layout):
        self.content_area.setLayout(layout)
        
    def set_title(self, title):
        arrow = "▼" if self.toggle_button.isChecked() else "▶"
        self.toggle_button.setText(f"{arrow} {title}")

class HeroManagementWidget(QWidget):
    """
    英雄管理组件
    使用折叠菜单展示英雄列表，允许用户配置拥有状态和队列信息。
    """

    # 英雄分组定义 (用于 UI 展示)
    HERO_GROUPS = {
        "Gen 4": ["rosa", "alcar", "margot"],
        "Gen 3": ["jaeger", "eric", "petra"],
        "Gen 2": ["hilde", "zoe", "marlin"],
        "Gen 1": ["jabel", "amadeus", "helga", "saul"],
        "SR (Epic)": ["amane", "yeonwoo", "fahd", "chenko", "gordon", "diana", "howard", "quinn"],
        "R (Rare)": ["olive", "edwin", "seth", "forrest"]
    }

    def __init__(self, parent=None):
        super().__init__(parent)
        self.hero_manager = None
        self.widgets = {} # {hero_name: {"chk": QCheckBox, "spin": QSpinBox}}
        self.init_ui()

    def init_ui(self):
        layout = QVBoxLayout(self)
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)

        # 使用自定义折叠控件包裹整个内容
        self.main_collapsible = CollapsibleBox(f"▶ {get_text('title_hero_management', '英雄状态管理')}")
        layout.addWidget(self.main_collapsible)

        # 内部布局 (放入 ScrollArea)
        scroll_area = QScrollArea()
        scroll_area.setWidgetResizable(True)
        scroll_area.setFrameShape(QFrame.NoFrame)
        
        inner_widget = QWidget()
        inner_layout = QVBoxLayout(inner_widget)
        inner_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        
        # 动态创建分组 (使用 CollapsibleBox 替代 QToolBox)
        for group_name, heroes in self.HERO_GROUPS.items():
            # 创建分组折叠框
            group_box = CollapsibleBox(f"▶ {group_name}")
            
            # 创建分组内容页面
            page_layout = self.create_group_page_layout(heroes)
            group_box.set_content_layout(page_layout)
            
            inner_layout.addWidget(group_box)

        # 添加弹簧，确保内容靠上
        inner_layout.addStretch()
        
        scroll_area.setWidget(inner_widget)
        
        # 创建一个包装布局给 main_collapsible
        wrapper_layout = QVBoxLayout()
        wrapper_layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        # 设置最小高度，避免展开时太小
        scroll_area.setMinimumHeight(200)
        wrapper_layout.addWidget(scroll_area)
        
        self.main_collapsible.set_content_layout(wrapper_layout)

    def create_group_page_layout(self, heroes):
        """创建单个分组页面的布局"""
        # 使用 QWidget 包裹内容，以便放入 ScrollArea (如果需要) 或者直接作为布局
        # 这里直接返回布局，由 CollapsibleBox 的 content_area 使用
        
        # 为了美观，我们在每个分组里也加一个 ScrollArea，或者直接用 Grid
        # 考虑到英雄数量不多，直接用 Grid 即可。如果英雄很多，可以考虑 ScrollArea。
        # 这里为了保持一致性，我们还是用一个 Widget 包裹 Grid
        
        content_widget = QWidget()
        grid = QGridLayout(content_widget)
        grid.setColumnStretch(1, 1) # 让中间空白区域伸缩
        grid.setContentsMargins(10, 5, 10, 5) # 增加一点内边距

        # 表头
        lbl_name = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_name", "英雄"))
        lbl_name.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_name, 0, 0)
        
        lbl_owned = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_owned", "拥有"))
        lbl_owned.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_owned, 0, 2)
        
        lbl_status = QLabel(get_text("lbl_hero_queue", "状态"))
        lbl_status.setStyleSheet("font-weight: bold;")
        grid.addWidget(lbl_status, 0, 3)

        row = 1
        for hero_name in heroes:
            # 1. 英雄名称 (支持中文)
            hero_key = f"hero_{hero_name}"
            display_name = get_text(hero_key, hero_name.capitalize())
            lbl_name = QLabel(display_name)
            grid.addWidget(lbl_name, row, 0)

            # 2. 拥有状态 (CheckBox)
            chk_owned = QCheckBox()
            chk_owned.stateChanged.connect(lambda state, h=hero_name: self.on_owned_changed(h, state))
            grid.addWidget(chk_owned, row, 2, alignment=Qt.AlignCenter)

            # 3. 状态信息 (可点击切换的 Button 或 Label)
            # 这里为了简化操作，使用 QPushButton，点击切换 忙碌/空闲
            btn_status = QPushButton(get_text("val_idle", "空闲"))
            btn_status.setCheckable(True) # 选中表示忙碌
            btn_status.setFixedWidth(60)
            btn_status.clicked.connect(lambda checked, h=hero_name: self.on_status_changed(h, checked))
            grid.addWidget(btn_status, row, 3)

            # 存储控件引用以便后续更新
            self.widgets[hero_name] = {
                "chk": chk_owned,
                "btn_status": btn_status
            }
            row += 1

        # 布局本身
        layout = QVBoxLayout()
        layout.setContentsMargins(0, 0, 0, 0)
        layout.addWidget(content_widget)
        
        return layout

    def set_manager(self, manager: HeroManager):
        """设置 HeroManager 实例并刷新 UI"""
        self.hero_manager = manager
        self.refresh_ui()

    def refresh_ui(self):
        """从 Manager 读取数据更新 UI"""
        if not self.hero_manager:
            return

        # 临时断开信号，防止循环触发
        self.block_signals(True)

        for hero_name, controls in self.widgets.items():
            info = self.hero_manager.get_hero_info(hero_name)
            
            # 更新 CheckBox
            is_owned = info.get("owned", False)
            controls["chk"].setChecked(is_owned)
            
            # 更新状态 Button
            state = info.get("state", "idle")
            is_busy = (state == "busy")
            
            btn = controls["btn_status"]
            btn.setChecked(is_busy)
            self.update_status_btn_style(btn, is_busy)
            
            # 如果未拥有，禁用状态切换
            btn.setEnabled(is_owned)

        self.block_signals(False)

    def update_status_btn_style(self, btn, is_busy):
        if is_busy:
            btn.setText(get_text("val_busy", "忙碌"))
            btn.setStyleSheet("background-color: #ffcccc; color: red; border: 1px solid red;")
        else:
            btn.setText(get_text("val_idle", "空闲"))
            btn.setStyleSheet("color: green;")

    def block_signals(self, block):
        """批量屏蔽/恢复信号"""
        for controls in self.widgets.values():
            controls["chk"].blockSignals(block)
            controls["btn_status"].blockSignals(block)

    def on_owned_changed(self, hero_name, state):
        """处理拥有状态变更"""
        if not self.hero_manager: return
        
        is_owned = (state == Qt.Checked.value)
        self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, owned=is_owned)
        
        # 联动：如果取消拥有，禁用状态按钮并重置为空闲
        controls = self.widgets.get(hero_name)
        if controls:
            btn = controls["btn_status"]
            btn.setEnabled(is_owned)
            if not is_owned:
                self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, state="idle")
                btn.setChecked(False)
                self.update_status_btn_style(btn, False)

    def on_status_changed(self, hero_name, checked):
        """处理状态变更 (点击按钮)"""
        if not self.hero_manager: return
        
        new_state = "busy" if checked else "idle"
        self.hero_manager.update_hero_status(hero_name, state=new_state)
        
        # 更新按钮样式
        controls = self.widgets.get(hero_name)
        if controls:
            self.update_status_btn_style(controls["btn_status"], checked)


==================================================
FILE: ui\login_dialog.py
